  • 已确定 184 例 ESP 病例,代表最大的单中心 队列研究。病变主要是孤立的、小(<5毫米)且位于食管中部。索引内窥镜检查中未发现病变有不典型增生、表皮样化生或浸润性癌。通过 RNA ISH 检测,所有可评估的病变均呈转录活性 HPV 阴性(0/177;95% CI,0-2.1%)。
  • 共纳入24例pSCCis,其中17例(71%)为高危型HPV阳性,5例(21%)为低危型HPV阳性,2例(8%)为HPV阴性。高危型HPV阳性pSCCis相较于低危型HPV阳性pSCCis更可能表现出杯状细胞丢失以及非异型增生与异型增生上皮之间明确的过渡。
  • 在1105名具有可分析样本的参与者中,总体HPV和hrHPV的患病率分别为30.9%和22.1%,且存在地区差异。非HPV 16和18型占hrHPV感染的近三分之二(67.6%)。

  • Overall, HPV prevalence was 33.6%. HPV prevalence was comparable between women aged ≤40 years and those aged ≥41 years (33.6% vs. [10.1371/journal.pgph.0006955].
  • 184 ESP cases were identified, representing the largest single-center cohort. Lesions were predominantly solitary, small (<5 mm), and located in the mid-esophagus.

  • 一项横断面研究显示,不同年龄组的 高危型HPV 感染率相当,但确定了不同人口统计数据的不同危险因素。Bioengineer.org 报道了这一发现。 [news-4963c2e75a6beebf]。

  • Cabo Verde is increasing efforts to eliminate cervical cancer through an initiative led by the Ministry of Health, with WHO technical assistance and Spanish Government financial support.
  • A cross-sectional study revealed comparable rates of high-risk HPV infection across age groups but identified distinct risk factors for different demographics. The findings were reported by Bioengineer.org.

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: PLOS One  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-11-10  |  DOI: 10.1371/journal.pone.0360438  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 21, 11, e0360438

尼日利亚HIV阳性女性中高危人乳头瘤病毒基因型的患病率及分布情况

Prevalence and distribution of high-risk human papillomavirus genotypes among women living with HIV in Nigeria
作者： Ijeoma Maryjoy Ifeorah, Jonadab C. Chinwendu, Stanley Chinedu Eneh, Babayemi O. Olakunde, Uduak Akpan, Nicholas Awodele Awolola, Uwem Etop George, Temitope O. C. Faleye, Popoola O. Bolutife, Chukwuma E. Omoruyi
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
HIV阳性女性(WLHIV)感染持续性高危型人乳头瘤病毒(hrHPV)和由此导致的宫颈癌的风险显著增加。然而,在尼日利亚,关于WLHIV中hrHPV基因型患病率的大型数据仍有限。
设计与对象
我们对来自尼日利亚六个选定的尼日利亚实施科学联盟-模型创新与研究中心(NISA-MIRCs)的1327名接受HIV治疗的25-60岁WLHIV进行了横断面研究。
核心方法
通过使用检测37种HPV基因型(包括14种高危型HPV)的Hybribio Genoarray检测方法获取宫颈拭子样本,并确定HPV DNA基因型。通过访谈式结构化问卷收集了社会人口学和临床数据。采用多变量逻辑回归分析确定与hrHPV感染独立相关的因素。
主要结果
在1105名具有可分析样本的参与者中,总体HPV和hrHPV的患病率分别为30.9%和22.1%,且存在地区差异。非HPV 16和18型占hrHPV感染的近三分之二(67.6%)。
解释与创新
最常检测到的前五种hrHPV分别是HPV-58(14.2%)、HPV-16(12.7%)、HPV-52(12.4%)、HPV-31(10.6%)和HPV-18(10.3%)。
科研与公卫意义
这项多中心研究显示,尼日利亚HIV阳性女性中高危型HPV的负担显著且基因型多样,其中大多数感染由非HPV16/18型引起。
局限与证据强度
In a multivariate analysis, ART usage for one year or less was associated with hrHPV infection (aOR = 8.2, 95% CI 4.3-16.0).

Prevalence and distribution of high-risk human papillomavirus genotypes among women living with HIV in Nigeria

Authors: Ijeoma Maryjoy Ifeorah, Jonadab C. Chinwendu, Stanley Chinedu Eneh, Babayemi O. Olakunde, Uduak Akpan, Nicholas Awodele Awolola, Uwem Etop George, Temitope O. C. Faleye, Popoola O. Bolutife, Chukwuma E. Omoruyi
Original abstract (English)
Women living with HIV (WLHIV) are at significantly higher risk of persistent high-risk human papillomavirus (hrHPV), and consequently, cervical cancer. However, large scale data on the epidemiology of hrHPV genotypes among WLHIV are limited in Nigeria. The study aimed to determine the prevalence and distribution of hrHPV among WLHIV in Nigeria. We conducted a cross-sectional study among 1327 WLHIV aged 25-60 years receiving HIV care from six selected Nigeria Implementation Science Alliance-Model Innovation and Research Centers (NISA-MIRCs) across Nigeria. Cervical swabs were obtained, and HPV DNA genotype was determined using the Hybribio Genoarray assay that detects 37 HPV genotypes including 14 high-risk HPVs. Sociodemographic and clinical data were collected using interviewer-administered structured questionnaire. Factors independently associated with hrHPV infection were identified using multivariable logistic regression. Statistical analysis was performed using R statistical software (version 4.4.1) with significance set at p < 0.05. Among the 1105 participants with analyzable samples, the overall HPV and hrHPV prevalence were 30.9% and 22.1%, respectively, with regional differences. Non-HPV 16 and 18 accounted for nearly two-thirds (67.6%) of hrHPV infections. The top five most often detected hrHPVs were HPV-58 (14.2%), HPV-16 (12.7%), HPV-52 (12.4%), HPV-31 (10.6%), and HPV-18 (10.3%). In a multivariate analysis, ART usage for one year or less was associated with hrHPV infection (aOR = 8.2, 95% CI 4.3-16.0). This multi-site study showed a significant and genotype-diverse burden of hrHPV among HIV-positive women in Nigeria, with most infections caused by non-HPV16/18 variants. These findings emphasize the need for increased integration of cervical cancer screening within HIV care and support the call for HPV vaccines with broader genotype coverage beyond HPV 16 and 18 to address the prevailing genotype distribution in this population.

Question and background
Women living with HIV (WLHIV) are at significantly higher risk of persistent high-risk human papillomavirus (hrHPV), and consequently, cervical cancer. However, large scale data on the epidemiology of hrHPV genotypes among WLHIV are limited in Nigeria.
Design and population
We conducted a cross-sectional study among 1327 WLHIV aged 25-60 years receiving HIV care from six selected Nigeria Implementation Science Alliance-Model Innovation and Research Centers (NISA-MIRCs) across Nigeria.
Core methods

Sociodemographic and clinical data were collected using interviewer-administered structured questionnaire. Factors independently associated with hrHPV infection were identified using multivariable logistic regression.
Main results
Among the 1105 participants with analyzable samples, the overall HPV and hrHPV prevalence were 30.9% and 22.1%, respectively, with regional differences. Non-HPV 16 and 18 accounted for nearly two-thirds (67.6%) of hrHPV infections.
Interpretation and novelty
The top five most often detected hrHPVs were HPV-58 (14.2%), HPV-16 (12.7%), HPV-52 (12.4%), HPV-31 (10.6%), and HPV-18 (10.3%).

In a multivariate analysis, ART usage for one year or less was associated with hrHPV infection (aOR = 8.2, 95% CI 4.3-16.0).

赞比亚西部省女性 人乳头瘤病毒 感染的年龄相关模式差异

Differential age-related patterns of Human Papillomavirus infection among women in Western Province, Zambia
作者： Masiliso A. Liamba, David Chisompola, Charity Mukutuma, Benson M. Hamooya, Grace Cecilia Kahenya, Sepiso Kenias Masenga
摘要中文翻译人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染仍然是全球主要的公共卫生问题,也是 人乳头瘤病毒 的主要原因,特别是在低收入和中等收入国家。赞比亚是全球 宫颈癌 负担最高的国家之一,但有关 HPV 感染的年龄特异性决定因素的证据仍然有限。本研究评估了赞比亚西部省 ≤40 岁和 ≥41 岁女性的 患病率 和 HPV 感染的决定因素。2025 年 10 月至 2026 年 1 月期间,对西部省 宫颈癌 筛查诊所的女性进行了一项横断面研究。通过连续抽样,总共招募了 1,132 名参与者。使用结构化问卷收集社会人口、行为和生殖数据。收集脱落的宫颈样本,并使用 Hologic Panther 平台和转录介导扩增 (TMA) 测试 高危型HPV 基因型。混合效应逻辑回归用于确定与 HPV 感染相关的因素,同时考虑医疗机构层面的聚类情况。总体而言,HPV 患病率 为 33.6%。HPV 患病率 在年龄 ≤ 40 岁的女性和年龄 ≥ 41 岁的女性之间具有可比性(33.6% vs. 在年龄 ≤ 40 岁的女性中,多一生性伴侣、高胎次、缺乏 HPV 疫苗接种、避孕措施的使用、低收入、单一婚姻状况和农村设施地点与 HPV 感染独立相关(P < 0.05)。在 ≥ 41 岁的女性中,高胎次、低胎次HPV 知识、失业、低收入和地理位置与 HPV 感染独立相关(P < 0.05),在年轻和老年女性中,HPV 感染率仍然很高,尽管决定因素因年龄组而异,但大量的 HIV 负担和持续的 HIV 传播 可能导致 HPV 感染的持续存在。疫苗接种、宫颈癌筛查、健康教育和治疗服务减轻HPV感染负担。

问题与背景
人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染仍然是全球主要的公共卫生问题,也是 人乳头瘤病毒 的主要原因,特别是在低收入和中等收入国家。本研究旨在解决有关 HPV 感染的年龄特异性决定因素的有限证据。
设计与对象
2025 年 10 月至 2026 年 1 月期间,对赞比亚西部省 宫颈癌 筛查诊所的女性进行了一项横断面研究。通过连续抽样,总共招募了 1,132 名参与者。
核心方法
使用结构化问卷收集社会人口、行为和生殖数据。收集脱落的宫颈样本,并使用 Hologic Panther 平台和转录介导扩增 (TMA) 测试 高危型HPV 基因型。混合效应逻辑回归用于确定与 HPV 感染相关的因素,同时考虑医疗机构层面的聚类情况。
主要结果
总体而言,HPV 患病率 为 33.6%。HPV 患病率 在年龄≤40 岁的女性和年龄≥41 岁的女性之间具有可比性(33.6% vs.
解释与创新
作者解释说,尽管决定因素因年龄组而异,但大量的 HIV 负担和持续的 HIV 传播 可能导致 HPV 感染的持续存在。该研究强调了 HPV 感染决定因素与年龄相关的差异。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持针对特定年龄的预防策略,将 HPV 疫苗接种、宫颈癌 筛查、健康教育和 治疗 服务结合起来,以减轻 HPV 感染的负担。该研究深入了解了赞比亚 HPV 感染的特定年龄危险因素。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到横截面设计的限制,无法确定因果关系。该证据是温和的,因为它基于观察数据,并且没有考虑所有潜在的混杂因素。

Differential age-related patterns of Human Papillomavirus infection among women in Western Province, Zambia

Authors: Masiliso A. Liamba, David Chisompola, Charity Mukutuma, Benson M. Hamooya, Grace Cecilia Kahenya, Sepiso Kenias Masenga
Original abstract (English)
Human papillomavirus (HPV) infection remains a major global public health concern and is the leading cause of cervical cancer, particularly in low- and middle-income countries. Zambia has one of the highest cervical cancer burdens worldwide, yet evidence on age-specific determinants of HPV infection remains limited. This study assessed the prevalence and determinants of HPV infection among women aged ≤40 years and ≥41 years in Western Province, Zambia. A cross-sectional study was conducted between October 2025 and January 2026 among women attending cervical cancer screening clinics in Western Province. A total of 1,132 participants were recruited using consecutive sampling. Sociodemographic, behavioural, and reproductive data were collected using structured questionnaires. Exfoliated cervical samples were collected and tested for high-risk HPV genotypes using the Hologic Panther platform with transcription-mediated amplification (TMA). Mixed-effects logistic regression was used to identify factors associated with HPV infection while accounting for clustering at the health facility level. Overall, HPV prevalence was 33.6%. HPV prevalence was comparable between women aged ≤40 years and those aged ≥41 years (33.6% vs. Among women aged ≤40 years, multiple lifetime sexual partners, high parity, lack of HPV vaccination, contraceptive use, low income, single marital status, and rural facility location were independently associated with HPV infection (P < 0.05). Among women aged ≥41 years, high parity, low HPV knowledge, unemployment, low income, and geographic location were independently associated with HPV infection (P < 0.05). Spatial variation was also observed, with significantly higher infection rates in Mwandi district. HPV infection remains highly prevalent across, in both younger and older women. Although the determinants differ by age group, the substantial HIV burden and ongoing HIV transmission may contribute to the persistence of HPV infection. These findings support age-specific prevention strategies that integrate HPV vaccination, cervical cancer screening, health education and treatment services to reduce the burden of HPV infection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Human papillomavirus (HPV) infection remains a major global public health concern and is the leading cause of cervical cancer, particularly in low- and middle-income countries. This study aimed to address the limited evidence on age-specific determinants of HPV infection.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted between October 2025 and January 2026 among women attending cervical cancer screening clinics in Western Province, Zambia. A total of 1,132 participants were recruited using consecutive sampling.
Core methods
Sociodemographic, behavioural, and reproductive data were collected using structured questionnaires. Exfoliated cervical samples were collected and tested for high-risk HPV genotypes using the Hologic Panther platform with transcription-mediated amplification (TMA). Mixed-effects logistic regression was used to identify factors associated with HPV infection while accounting for clustering at the health facility level.
Main results
Overall, HPV prevalence was 33.6%. HPV prevalence was comparable between women aged ≤40 years and those aged ≥41 years (33.6% vs.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that although the determinants differ by age group, the substantial HIV burden and ongoing HIV transmission may contribute to the persistence of HPV infection. The study highlights age-related differences in HPV infection determinants.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings support age-specific prevention strategies that integrate HPV vaccination, cervical cancer screening, health education and treatment services to reduce the burden of HPV infection. The study provides insights into the age-specific risk factors for HPV infection in Zambia.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design, which cannot establish causality. The evidence is moderate as it is based on observational data and does not account for all potential confounding factors.

宫颈癌 筛查中流式细胞术 E6/E7 mRNA 检测与 人乳头瘤病毒 DNA 检测和宫颈细胞学的一致性 队列研究

Concordance of flow cytometric E6/E7 mRNA detection with human papillomavirus DNA testing and cervical cytology in a cervical cancer screening cohort
作者： Dat Quoc Ngo, Trang Kieu Ha, Mi Ngoc Thi Tran, Anh Mai Thi Nguyen, Truong Nhat Trinh, Thien Thanh Ly, Linh Ngoc Khanh Nguyen, Thu Dang Anh Phan, Ha Trang Kieu, Mai Ánh Nguyễn
摘要中文翻译背景:高危(HR)–人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)病毒癌基因E6和E7的过度表达在宫颈上皮内瘤变(CIN)和癌症的进展中发挥关键作用。E6/E7 mRNA 的检测可能有助于区分短暂性感染和转化性感染,从而提高 宫颈癌 筛查的准确性。本研究旨在评估 E6/E7 mRNA 表达与 HR-HPV 感染、宫颈细胞学和组织学证实的宫颈病变的关联,并比较其与 HPV DNA 检测和细胞学的一致性。方法:共有 526 名在 2023 年 1 月至 2025 年 5 月期间接受 宫颈癌 筛查的女性入组。使用流式细胞术和 HPV OncoTect 3Dx E6/E7 mRNA 检测评估 E6/E7 mRNA 表达,并与 HR-HPV DNA 检测、宫颈细胞学和活检标本的可用组织病理学结果进行比较。结果:在 526 个样本中,8 个 (1.5%) E6/E7 mRNA 检测呈阳性,而 30 个 (5.7%) HR-HPV DNA 呈阳性,17 个 (3.2%) 细胞学结果异常。HR-HPV 阳性病例和细胞学异常病例中 E6/E7 mRNA 表达中位数较高。E6/E7 mRNA 结果、HR-HPV DNA 检测和细胞学结果之间观察到几乎完美的一致性。对 9 名细胞学异常≥低级别鳞状上皮内病变的女性进行了组织病理学随访,其中包括 4 例 CIN2 病例和 5 例 CIN1 病例。在 1 例与 HPV16 感染相关的 CIN2 病例中,E6/E7 mRNA 表达显着升高(37.51%),而 2 例 CIN1 病例尽管 HPV DNA 结果为阴性,但仍表现出高 mRNA 表达。结论:在本次 队列研究 筛查中,流式细胞术 E6/E7 mRNA 检测与 HPV DNA 检测和细胞学检测显示出高度的总体一致性。需要进行更大规模的前瞻性研究和全面的组织学随访来确定其临床效用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
高危 (HR)–人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 病毒癌基因 E6 和 E7 的过度表达在宫颈上皮内瘤变 (CIN) 和癌症的进展中发挥关键作用。E6/E7 mRNA 的检测可能有助于区分短暂性感染和转化性感染,从而提高 宫颈癌 筛查的准确性。
设计与对象
2023 年 1 月至 2025 年 5 月期间,共有 526 名接受 宫颈癌 筛查的女性入组。该研究旨在评估 E6/E7 mRNA 表达与 HR-HPV 感染、宫颈细胞学和组织学证实的宫颈病变的关系。
核心方法
使用流式细胞术和 HPV OncoTect 3Dx E6/E7 mRNA 检测评估 E6/E7 mRNA 表达,并与 HR-HPV DNA 检测、宫颈细胞学和活检标本的可用组织病理学结果进行比较。
主要结果
在 526 个样本中,8 个 (1.5%) E6/E7 mRNA 检测呈阳性,而 30 个 (5.7%) HR-HPV DNA 呈阳性,17 个 (3.2%) 细胞学结果异常。HR-HPV 阳性病例和细胞学异常病例中 E6/E7 mRNA 表达中位数较高。E6/E7 mRNA 结果、HR-HPV DNA 检测和细胞学结果之间观察到几乎完美的一致性。
解释与创新
在本次筛查 队列研究 中,流式细胞术 E6/E7 mRNA 检测与 HPV DNA 检测和细胞学检测显示出高度的总体一致性。对 9 名细胞学异常≥低级别鳞状上皮内病变的女性进行了组织病理学随访,其中包括 4 例 CIN2 病例和 5 例 CIN1 病例。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,E6/E7 mRNA 检测可能是提高 宫颈癌 筛查准确性的宝贵工具。需要进行更大规模的前瞻性研究和全面的组织学随访来确定其临床效用。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和可能丢失组织病理学数据而受到限制。证据是中等的,因为它基于单个 队列研究 且组织学随访有限。

Concordance of flow cytometric E6/E7 mRNA detection with human papillomavirus DNA testing and cervical cytology in a cervical cancer screening cohort

Authors: Dat Quoc Ngo, Trang Kieu Ha, Mi Ngoc Thi Tran, Anh Mai Thi Nguyen, Truong Nhat Trinh, Thien Thanh Ly, Linh Ngoc Khanh Nguyen, Thu Dang Anh Phan, Ha Trang Kieu, Mai Ánh Nguyễn
Original abstract (English)
Background: Overexpression of viral oncogenes E6 and E7 of high-risk (HR)–human papillomavirus (HPV) plays a key role in the progression to cervical intraepithelial neoplasia (CIN) and cancer. Detection of E6/E7 mRNA may help distinguish between transient and transforming infections, thus improving cervical cancer screening accuracy. This study aimed to evaluate the association of E6/E7 mRNA expression with HR-HPV infection, cervical cytology, and histologically confirmed cervical lesions and to compare its agreement with HPV DNA testing and cytology. Methods: A total of 526 women undergoing cervical cancer screening between January 2023 and May 2025 were enrolled. E6/E7 mRNA expression was assessed using flow cytometry with the HPV OncoTect 3Dx E6/E7 mRNA assay and compared with HR-HPV DNA testing, cervical cytology, and available histopathologic findings from biopsy specimens. Results: Among 526 samples, eight (1.5%) tested positive for E6/E7 mRNA, compared to 30 (5.7%) HR-HPV DNA–positive and 17 (3.2%) abnormal cytology results. Median E6/E7 mRNA expression was higher in HR-HPV– positive cases and in cytologically abnormal cases. Almost perfect agreement was observed between E6/E7 mRNA results, HR-HPV DNA testing, and cytology. Histopathologic follow-up was available for nine women with cytological abnormalities ≥low-grade squamous intraepithelial lesion, including four CIN2 and five CIN1 cases. E6/E7 mRNA expression was markedly elevated in one CIN2 case associated with HPV16 infection (37.51%), while two CIN1 cases demonstrated high mRNA expression despite negative HPV DNA results. Conclusions: Flow-cytometric E6/E7 mRNA detection showed high overall agreement with HPV DNA testing and cytology in this screening cohort. Larger prospective studies with comprehensive histologic follow-up are needed to determine its clinical utility.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Overexpression of viral oncogenes E6 and E7 of high-risk (HR)–human papillomavirus (HPV) plays a key role in the progression to cervical intraepithelial neoplasia (CIN) and cancer. Limitations and evidence strength

食管鳞状乳头状瘤:人乳头瘤病毒 关联和自然史

Esophageal Squamous Papillomas: Human Papillomavirus Association and Natural History
作者： Waqqas Haroon, Ahmed Nadeem, Bassel Zein-Sabatto, Clifton G. Fulmer, Brian Baggot, Ari Garber, Bassel Zein‐Sabatto
摘要中文翻译简介:食管鳞状乳头状瘤 (ESP) 是罕见的上皮病变,其 发病机制 的了解不完全,且恶性潜力不确定。先前评估 ESP 与 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 之间关联的研究显示出相互矛盾的结果,通常受到小型 队列研究 和异质 HPV 检测方法的限制。方法:我们于 2019 年 1 月至 2023 年 7 月在克利夫兰诊所对组织学证实的 ESP 进行了回顾性 队列研究 研究。收集了临床、内镜和组织病理学数据。福尔马林固定、石蜡包埋的组织进行了针对 E6/E7 mRNA 的 RNA 原位杂交 (RNA ISH),以检测转录活性低和 高危型HPV 亚型。结果:发现 184 例 ESP 病例,代表最大的单中心 队列研究。病变主要是孤立的、小(<5毫米)且位于食管中部。胃食管反流病和吞咽困难是内窥镜检查最常见的适应症。索引内窥镜检查中未发现病变有不典型增生、表皮样化生或浸润性癌。通过 RNA ISH 检测,所有可评估的病变均呈转录活性 HPV 阴性(0/177;95% CI,0-2.1%)。70 名患者(38%)接受了内窥镜随访,中位随访时间为 24 个月,监测 随访时间≥4 年。在可用的随访期间没有观察到不典型增生/恶变。在对指标病变进行非切除或仅活检取样后,在 6 名患者中发现了持续/残留的 ESP。结论:转录活性 HPV 的缺乏支持 HPV 在 ESP 发病机制 中的作用有限,并且不支持在典型的非发育不良 ESP 中进行常规 HPV 检测。我们的研究结果支持 ESP 通常是良性的自然史,并强调需要利用标准化长期 监测 进行前瞻性研究,以更好地确定其恶性潜力。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
食管鳞状乳头状瘤 (ESP) 是罕见的上皮病变,其 发病机制 的了解不完全,且恶性潜力不确定。
设计与对象
我们于 2019 年 1 月至 2023 年 7 月期间在克利夫兰诊所对组织学证实的 ESP 进行了回顾性 队列研究 研究。
核心方法
收集临床、内窥镜和组织病理学数据。福尔马林固定、石蜡包埋的组织进行了针对 E6/E7 mRNA 的 RNA 原位杂交 (RNA ISH),以检测转录活性低和 高危型HPV 亚型。
主要结果
已确定 184 例 ESP 病例,代表最大的单中心 队列研究。病变主要是孤立的、小(<5毫米)且位于食管中部。索引内窥镜检查中未发现病变有不典型增生、表皮样化生或浸润性癌。通过 RNA ISH 检测,所有可评估的病变均呈转录活性 HPV 阴性(0/177;95% CI,0-2.1%)。
解释与创新
转录活性 HPV 的缺乏支持 HPV 在 ESP 发病机制 中的有限作用,并且不支持在典型的非发育不良 ESP 中进行常规 HPV 检测。
科研与公卫意义
我们的研究结果支持 ESP 通常是良性的自然史,并强调需要利用标准化长期 监测 进行前瞻性研究,以更好地确定其恶性潜力。
局限与证据强度
70 名患者(38%)接受了内窥镜随访,中位随访时间为 24 个月,监测 随访时间≥4 年。在可用的随访期间没有观察到不典型增生/恶变。

Esophageal Squamous Papillomas: Human Papillomavirus Association and Natural History

Authors: Waqqas Haroon, Ahmed Nadeem, Bassel Zein-Sabatto, Clifton G. Fulmer, Brian Baggot, Ari Garber, Bassel Zein‐Sabatto
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUCTION: Esophageal squamous papillomas (ESPs) are rare epithelial lesions with an incompletely understood pathogenesis and uncertain malignant potential. Prior studies evaluating the association between ESP and human papillomavirus (HPV) have demonstrated conflicting results, often limited by small cohorts and heterogeneous HPV detection methodologies. METHODS: We conducted a retrospective cohort study of histologically confirmed ESPs at Cleveland Clinic between January 2019 and July 2023. Clinical, endoscopic, and histopathologic data were collected. Formalin-fixed, paraffin-embedded tissue underwent RNA in situ hybridization (RNA ISH) targeting E6/E7 mRNA to detect transcriptionally active low- and high-risk HPV subtypes. RESULTS: 184 ESP cases were identified, representing the largest single-center cohort. Lesions were predominantly solitary, small (<5 mm), and located in the mid-esophagus. GERD and dysphagia were the most common indications for endoscopy. No lesion demonstrated dysplasia, epidermoid metaplasia, or invasive carcinoma at index endoscopy. All evaluable lesions were negative for transcriptionally active HPV by RNA ISH (0/177; 95% CI, 0-2.1%). Follow-up endoscopy was available in 70 patients (38%), with a median follow-up of 24 months and surveillance extending to ≥4 years. No dysplasia / malignant transformation was observed during available follow-up. Persistent/residual ESP was identified in 6 patients following non-removal or biopsy-only sampling of the index lesion. CONCLUSIONS: The absence of transcriptionally active HPV supports a limited role for HPV in ESP pathogenesis and does not support routine HPV testing in typical, non-dysplastic ESPs. Our findings support the generally benign natural history of ESPs and highlight the need for prospective studies with standardized long-term surveillance to better define their malignant potential.

与 人乳头瘤病毒 相关和其他 人乳头瘤病毒 独立腺癌相比,胃型宫颈腺癌中脊髓灰质炎病毒受体 ( CD155 ) 的表达

Poliovirus receptor ( CD155 ) expression in gastric‐type adenocarcinoma of the cervix compared with human papillomavirus–associated and other human papillomavirus–independent adenocarcinomas
作者： Kyosuke Kamijo, Hirofumi Ando, Shiori Oshima, Shiho Asaka, Manaka Shinagawa, Yoshinori Sato, Ryoichi Asaka, Marina Fujioka, Natsuki Uchiyama, Yusuke Yokokawa
摘要中文翻译目的:胃型腺癌 (GAS) 是一种 人乳头瘤病毒 独立 (HPVI) 组织学类型的宫颈腺癌,与 HPV 相关 (HPVA) 腺癌相比,预后较差,且具有独特的免疫微环境。脊髓灰质炎病毒受体(PVR、CD155)是一种抑制抗肿瘤免疫的免疫检查点配体,但其在 GAS 中的表达尚不清楚。我们的目的是表征宫颈腺癌组织学组中 PVR 的表达及其临床病理学和免疫学相关性。方法和结果:我们回顾性研究了 142 例宫颈腺癌患者(37 例 GAS、92 例 HPVA 和 13 例 HPVI [非 GAS] 病例)。通过免疫组织化学方法对 PVR、PD-L1(联合阳性评分 [CPS] 和肿瘤区域阳性 [TAP])和 CD8 阳性肿瘤浸润淋巴细胞进行定量。所有 GAS 病例中均存在 PVR 阳性(H 分数 ≥ 50)(100%),而 HPVA 病例为 56.5%,HPVI(非 GAS)病例为 69.2%,且 PVR H 分数在各组之间存在显着差异,其中 GAS 病例中位数最高(HPVA 为 112.8 vs. 70.8,HPVI [非 GAS] 病例为 98.2;P=100)在单变量分析中,与较差的总体生存率和无复发生存率相关(均 P < 0.001),但在调整分期和组织学组后,这种相关性并未得到维持。结论:在组织学组中,GAS 中 PVR 表达最高,并与 PD-L1 评分(CPS 和 TAP)和侵袭性临床病理特征相关,但在调整分期和组织学后并不是独立的预后标志物。这些发现支持进一步研究 PVR 作为 GAS 的治疗靶点。

问题与背景
胃型腺癌 (GAS) 是一种 人乳头瘤病毒 独立 (HPVI) 组织学类型的宫颈腺癌,与 HPV 相关 (HPVA) 腺癌相比,其预后较差,且具有独特的免疫微环境。

核心方法

主要结果
所有 GAS 病例中均存在 PVR 阳性(H 分数 ≥ 50)(100%),而 HPVA 病例为 56.5%,HPVI(非 GAS)病例为 69.2%,且 PVR H 分数在各组之间存在显着差异,其中 GAS 病例中位数最高(HPVA 为 112.8 vs. 70.8,HPVI [非 GAS] 病例为 98.2;P=100)在单变量分析中,与较差的总体生存率和无复发生存率相关(均 P < 0.001),但在调整分期和组织学组后,这种相关性并未得到维持。
解释与创新
PVR 表达在组织学组中的 GAS 中最高,并与 PD-L1 评分(CPS 和 TAP)和侵袭性临床病理特征相关,但在调整分期和组织学后并不是独立的预后标志物。

Poliovirus receptor ( CD155 ) expression in gastric‐type adenocarcinoma of the cervix compared with human papillomavirus–associated and other human papillomavirus–independent adenocarcinomas

Authors: Kyosuke Kamijo, Hirofumi Ando, Shiori Oshima, Shiho Asaka, Manaka Shinagawa, Yoshinori Sato, Ryoichi Asaka, Marina Fujioka, Natsuki Uchiyama, Yusuke Yokokawa
Original abstract (English)
AIMS: Gastric-type adenocarcinoma (GAS), a human papillomavirus-independent (HPVI) histological type of cervical adenocarcinoma, has a poorer prognosis and a distinct immune microenvironment compared with HPV-associated (HPVA) adenocarcinoma. Poliovirus receptor (PVR, CD155) is an immune-checkpoint ligand that suppresses antitumour immunity, but its expression in GAS is unknown. We aimed to characterize PVR expression across histological groups of cervical adenocarcinoma and its clinicopathological and immunological correlates. METHODS AND RESULTS: We retrospectively studied 142 patients with cervical adenocarcinoma (37 GAS, 92 HPVA and 13 HPVI [non-GAS] cases). PVR, PD-L1 (combined positive score [CPS] and tumour area positivity [TAP]) and CD8-positive tumour-infiltrating lymphocytes were quantified immunohistochemically. PVR positivity (H-score ≥ 50) was present in all GAS cases (100%), versus 56.5% of HPVA and 69.2% of HPVI (non-GAS) cases and the PVR H-score differed significantly among groups, with the highest median in GAS (112.8 vs. 70.8 in HPVA and 98.2 in HPVI [non-GAS]; P 100) was associated with poorer overall and recurrence-free survival in univariable analysis (both P < 0.001), but this association was not maintained after adjustment for stage and histological group. CONCLUSIONS: PVR expression was highest in GAS among the histological groups and correlated with PD-L1 scores (CPS and TAP) and aggressive clinicopathological features, but was not an independent prognostic marker after adjustment for stage and histology. These findings support further investigation of PVR as a therapeutic target in GAS.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Gastric-type adenocarcinoma (GAS), a human papillomavirus-independent (HPVI) histological type of cervical adenocarcinoma, has a poorer prognosis and a distinct immune microenvironment compared with HPV-associated (HPVA) adenocarcinoma.
Design and population
We retrospectively studied 142 patients with cervical adenocarcinoma (37 GAS, 92 HPVA and 13 HPVI [non-GAS] cases).
Core methods
PVR, PD-L1 (combined positive score [CPS] and tumour area positivity [TAP]) and CD8-positive tumour-infiltrating lymphocytes were quantified immunohistochemically.
Main results

These findings support further investigation of PVR as a therapeutic target in GAS.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations of the study.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

子宫内膜 LIF 表达的遗传和表观遗传决定因素以及 人乳头瘤病毒 E6 介导的 p53 降解的潜在作用

Genetic and Epigenetic Determinants of Endometrial LIF Expression and the Potential Role of Human Papillomavirus E6-Mediated p53 Degradation
作者： Charalampos Voros, Stylianos Makrydimas, Fotios G. Chatzinikolaou, Diamantis Athanasiou, Aikaterini V. Athanasiou, Nikolaos Thomakos, Panagiotis G. Antsaklis, Georgios Daskalakis, George Makrydimas
摘要中文翻译我们的综述提出了一种机制假设,但没有描述既定的途径。其依据的经验前提是 HPV E6 在子宫内膜组织中表达并在该位点降解 p53,但尚未在人类中得到证实。子宫内膜表达白血病抑制因子 (LIF) 是囊胚植入所必需的,在小鼠中,它是在 p53 的控制下转录的,该 p53 作用于人类基因中保守的内含子 1 中的响应元件。人类对相同关系的支持是遗传的,而不是生化的。TP53 密码子 72 多态性 rs1042522 改变了 LIF 反式激活,并与多个 队列研究 中的植入失败相关,而在最大的 队列研究 中没有显示出影响。LIF 本身 rs929271 以及 MDM2、MDM4 和 USP7 的变异与着床和后期妊娠维持有关。高风险的 人乳头瘤病毒 使第二个 基因组 陷入同样的​​监管问题。E6癌蛋白招募E6相关蛋白泛素连接酶并引导p53进行蛋白酶体降解,并且该反应的效率在高风险基因型之间以及HPV16 E6的自然序列变体之间存在两个以上数量级的差异。p53 的缺失还会升高 DNMT1 并去除 p53 依赖性 microRNA,从而将轴置于循环子宫内膜的表观遗传程序内。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在探讨影响子宫内膜 LIF 表达的遗传和表观遗传因素及其与 HPV E6 介导的 p53 降解的潜在联系,从而影响着床和妊娠结局。
设计与对象
该研究回顾了现有文献,重点关注 HPV E6、p53 降解和子宫内膜 LIF 表达之间的机制关系,重点关注人类数据和遗传关联。
核心方法
该研究依赖于对遗传和表观遗传研究的全面回顾,包括对 TP53 密码子 72 等多态性和 LIF、MDM2、MDM4 和 USP7 基因变异的分析,以及 HPV E6 在 p53 降解中的作用。
主要结果
审查发现,虽然小鼠模型显示 p53 对 LIF 表达的调节,但人类数据仅通过遗传证据而非生化证明来支持这种关系。此外,在一些 队列研究 中,TP53 密码子 72 多态性 rs1042522 与植入失败相关,但在最大的研究中则不然。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为暗示 HPV E6 介导的 p53 降解与子宫内膜 LIF 表达之间存在潜在的遗传联系,尽管他们强调在人类中缺乏直接的生化证据。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能为未来有关 HPV 相关生殖健康问题的遗传和表观遗传机制的研究提供信息,特别是了解病毒癌蛋白如何影响子宫内膜功能和妊娠结局。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于其依赖于遗传关联,而不是人类的直接生化证据,TP53 多态性的混合结果表明需要进一步研究。由于这些限制,证据的强度中等。

Genetic and Epigenetic Determinants of Endometrial LIF Expression and the Potential Role of Human Papillomavirus E6-Mediated p53 Degradation

Authors: Charalampos Voros, Stylianos Makrydimas, Fotios G. Chatzinikolaou, Diamantis Athanasiou, Aikaterini V. Athanasiou, Nikolaos Thomakos, Panagiotis G. Antsaklis, Georgios Daskalakis, George Makrydimas
Original abstract (English)
Our review develops a mechanistic hypothesis and does not describe an established pathway. The empirical premise on which it rests, that HPV E6 is expressed in endometrial tissue and degrades p53 at that site, has not been demonstrated in humans. Endometrial expression of leukaemia inhibitory factor (LIF) is required for blastocyst implantation, and in the mouse it is transcribed under the control of p53 acting at a response element in intron 1 that is conserved in the human gene. Human support for the same relationship is genetic rather than biochemical. The TP53 codon 72 polymorphism rs1042522 alters LIF transactivation and has been associated with implantation failure in several cohorts while showing no effect in the largest. Variation in LIF itself at rs929271, and in MDM2, MDM4 and USP7, has been associated with implantation and with later pregnancy maintenance. High-risk human papillomavirus brings a second genome into the same regulatory problem. The E6 oncoprotein recruits the E6-associated protein ubiquitin ligase and directs p53 to proteasomal degradation, and the efficiency of that reaction varies by more than two orders of magnitude across high-risk genotypes and again between natural sequence variants of HPV16 E6. Loss of p53 also raises DNMT1 and removes p53-dependent microRNAs, which places the axis inside the epigenetic programme of the cycling endometrium.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to explore the genetic and epigenetic factors influencing endometrial LIF expression and their potential connection to HPV E6-mediated p53 degradation, which could impact implantation and pregnancy outcomes.
Design and population
The research was a review of existing literature focusing on the mechanistic relationship between HPV E6, p53 degradation, and endometrial LIF expression, with an emphasis on human data and genetic associations.
Core methods
The study relied on a comprehensive review of genetic and epigenetic studies, including analysis of polymorphisms such as TP53 codon 72 and variations in LIF, MDM2, MDM4, and USP7 genes, as well as the role of HPV E6 in p53 degradation.
Main results
The review found that while the mouse model shows p53 regulation of LIF expression, human data support this relationship only through genetic evidence, not biochemical demonstration. Additionally, TP53 codon 72 polymorphism rs1042522 was linked to implantation failure in some cohorts but not in the largest study.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted these findings as suggesting a potential genetic link between HPV E6-mediated p53 degradation and endometrial LIF expression, though they emphasized the lack of direct biochemical evidence in humans.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform future research on the genetic and epigenetic mechanisms of HPV-related reproductive health issues, particularly in understanding how viral oncoproteins affect endometrial function and pregnancy outcomes.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on genetic associations rather than direct biochemical evidence in humans, and the mixed results of TP53 polymorphism suggest the need for further investigation. The evidence is moderate in strength due to these constraints.

人乳头瘤病毒 RNA原位杂交在结膜乳头状鳞状细胞原位癌中的应用

Human papillomavirus RNA in situ hybridisation in conjunctival papillary squamous cell carcinoma in situ
作者： Lily C. Farrell, Robert J Medina, Justin C. Muste, Sara E. Lally, Carol L. Shields, Ralph C. Eagle, Tatyana Milman, Eagle RC Jr
摘要中文翻译背景/目的 结膜乳头状鳞状细胞原位癌 (pSCCis) 是结膜鳞状细胞癌的一种罕见类型,其形态与鳞状乳头状瘤重叠。本研究的目的是通过 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) RNA 原位杂交 (ISH) 研究来描述 pSCC 的临床、组织病理学特征,并将这些结果与结膜乳头状瘤进行比较。方法 对 2015 年至 2024 年期间发现的所有 pSCC 进行 HPV RNA ISH。收集临床和组织病理学数据,并与结膜乳头状瘤 队列研究(2015-2024 年)进行比较。结果 纳入 24 例 pSCC(24 名患者)。HPV RNA ISH 研究在 17 例 (71%) 中发现 高危型HPV,在 5 例 (21%) 中发现低危 HPV,在 2 例 (8%) 病变中呈阴性。高危型HPV 阳性结膜 pSCC 与低危 HPV 阳性肿瘤的比较分析表明,高危 pSCC 更可能出现杯状细胞丢失(76% vs 0%,p<0.01),且非发育异常和发育异常上皮之间存在明确的过渡(94% vs 0%,p<0.01)。临床上,内在色素在低危 HPV 阳性病变中更为常见(18% vs 100%,p<0.01),两组之间没有其他显着的临床、人口统计学、管理和结果差异。与 高危型HPV 阳性 pSCC 相比,低风险 HPV 阳性病变在人口统计学、临床和组织病理学上与 58 例结膜乳头状瘤更接近(47 例经组织病理学证实,并且在所有 7 个测试病变中均处于低风险 HPV 阳性状态)。结论 结膜 pSCCis 表型富含 HPV(92%),主要 高危型HPV 阳性肿瘤(71%)显示转化表型,低危 HPV 阳性肿瘤显示生产表型,在形态学和临床上与乳头状瘤重叠。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在描述结膜乳头状鳞状细胞原位癌 (pSCCis) 的特征,并将这些发现与结膜乳头状瘤进行比较,以满足了解 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 在这种情况下的作用的需要。
设计与对象
对 2015 年至 2024 年期间发现的 pSCC 进行了一项研究,将其与同期的结膜乳头状瘤 队列研究 进行比较。
核心方法
对所有 pSCC 进行 HPV RNA 原位杂交 (ISH),收集临床和组织病理学数据并与结膜乳头状瘤 队列研究 进行比较。
主要结果
共纳入24例pSCCis,其中17例(71%)为高危型HPV阳性,5例(21%)为低危型HPV阳性,2例(8%)为HPV阴性。高危型HPV阳性pSCCis相较于低危型HPV阳性pSCCis更可能表现出杯状细胞丢失以及非异型增生与异型增生上皮之间明确的过渡。
解释与创新
研究发现,高危型HPV 阳性 pSCCis 显示出转化表型,而低危 HPV 阳性 pSCCis 显示出生产性表型,与结膜乳头状瘤重叠。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,HPV 在 pSCC 中发挥重要作用,高危型HPV 阳性肿瘤显示出独特的表型。这可以为 监测 和 pSCCis 的诊断策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到样本量小和缺乏随机性的限制。由于这些限制,证据强度中等。

Human papillomavirus RNA in situ hybridisation in conjunctival papillary squamous cell carcinoma in situ

Authors: Lily C. Farrell, Robert J Medina, Justin C. Muste, Sara E. Lally, Carol L. Shields, Ralph C. Eagle, Tatyana Milman, Eagle RC Jr
Original abstract (English)
Background/aims Conjunctival papillary squamous cell carcinoma in situ (pSCCis) is an unusual type of conjunctival squamous cell carcinoma, which morphologically overlaps with squamous papilloma. The goal of this study was to characterise pSCCis clinically, histopathologically and with human papillomavirus (HPV) RNA in situ hybridisation (ISH) studies and to compare these findings with conjunctival papilloma. Methods HPV RNA ISH was performed on all pSCCis identified between 2015 and 2024. Clinical and histopathologic data were collected and compared with conjunctival papilloma cohort (2015–2024). Results 24 pSCCis (24 patients) were included. HPV RNA ISH studies identified high-risk HPV in 17 (71%), low-risk HPV in 5 (21%) and were negative in 2 (8%) lesions. Comparative analysis of high-risk HPV-positive conjunctival pSCCis with low-risk HPV-positive tumours showed that high-risk pSCCis were more likely to feature loss of goblet cells (76% vs 0%, p<0.01) with a well-defined transition between non-dysplastic and dysplastic epithelium (94% vs 0%, p<0.01). Clinically, intrinsic pigment was more common in low-risk HPV-positive lesions (18% vs 100%, p<0.01) with no other significant clinical, demographic, management and outcome differences between the two groups. When compared with high-risk HPV-positive pSCCs, low-risk HPV-positive lesions profiled closer demographically, clinically and histopathologically to 58 conjunctival papillomas (47 with histopathologic confirmation and low-risk HPV-positive status in all 7 tested lesions). Conclusions Conjunctival pSCCis phenotype is enriched for HPV (92%), with the predominant high-risk HPV-positive tumours (71%) showing the transformative phenotype and the low-risk HPV-positive tumours showing the productive phenotype morphologically and clinically overlapping with papilloma.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to characterise conjunctival papillary squamous cell carcinoma in situ (pSCCis) and compare these findings with conjunctival papilloma, addressing the need to understand the role of human papillomavirus (HPV) in this condition.
Design and population
A study was performed on pSCCis identified between 2015 and 2024, comparing them with a conjunctival papilloma cohort from the same period.
Core methods
HPV RNA in situ hybridisation (ISH) was performed on all pSCCis, and clinical and histopathologic data were collected and compared with the conjunctival papilloma cohort.
Main results
Twenty-four pSCCis were included, with 17 (71%) showing high-risk HPV, 5 (21%) showing low-risk HPV, and 2 (8%) being HPV-negative. High-risk HPV-positive pSCCis were more likely to feature loss of goblet cells and a well-defined transition between non-dysplastic and dysplastic epithelium compared to low-risk HPV-positive pSCCis.
Interpretation and novelty
The study found that high-risk HPV-positive pSCCis showed a transformative phenotype, while low-risk HPV-positive pSCCis showed a productive phenotype, overlapping with conjunctival papilloma.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that HPV plays a significant role in pSCCis, with high-risk HPV-positive tumours showing a distinct phenotype. This could inform surveillance and diagnostic strategies for pSCCis.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by the small sample size and the lack of randomisation. The evidence strength is moderate due to these limitations.

肛门鳞状细胞癌主要由不同地理区域的 人乳头瘤病毒 感染驱动

Anal squamous cell carcinoma is predominantly driven by human papillomavirus infection across different geographic regions
作者： Iracema Basílio, Jesus López-Díaz, Núria Peñuelas, Natalia Rakislova, Lorena Marimon, Isabel Matas, Lucília Lovane, Katarzyna Darecka, Silvia Valls, Assucena Guisseve
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)被认为是肛门鳞状细胞癌(ASCC)的主要病因。但HPV感染的流行病学特征或患病率是否存在地域差异尚不清楚。
设计与对象
本研究旨在比较莫桑比克和西班牙这两个国家在 HPV 和人类免疫缺陷病毒感染方面的流行病学背景明显不同的 ASCC 的病因和流行病学特征。2019年至2023年间在莫桑比克和西班牙两家三级转诊医院诊断的所有ASCC均被纳入。
核心方法
所有病例均进行了 HPV DNA 检测和基因分型,以及 p16 和 p53 的免疫组织化学染色。
主要结果
No differences were identified in the percentage of HPV-associated tumors: 41/43 (95.3%) in Mozambique and 28/29 (96.6%) in Spain. HPV16 was the most frequently identified type in both countries, but other HPV types were more frequently found in the absence of HPV16 in Mozambique than in Spain (16/36, 44.4% vs. 2/28, 7.1%; p = 0.0016). In Mozambique, ASCC was less frequent in men (7/43; 16.3%, vs. 14/29; 48.3%; p = 0.007) and occurred in younger patients (44.1 ± 9.4 vs. 60.3 ± 12.6; p < 0.001).
解释与创新
作者解释说,两国 HPV 相关 ASCC 的高 患病率 表明 HPV 在该肿瘤的病因学中起主导作用,并且 HPV 患病率 没有显着的地理差异。该研究还确定了与 HPV 无关的 ASCC 发展的两种不同途径,一种与 TP53 突变 相关,另一种与野生型 TP53 相关。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,莫桑比克和西班牙需要针对肛门癌采取基于 HPV 的筛查和预防策略,强调了解 HPV 相关疾病模式的区域差异的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于它只关注两家三级转诊医院,这可能无法代表更广泛的人群。证据有力,两国HPV 患病率和肿瘤特征的比较具有很高的统计学意义。

Anal squamous cell carcinoma is predominantly driven by human papillomavirus infection across different geographic regions

Authors: Iracema Basílio, Jesus López-Díaz, Núria Peñuelas, Natalia Rakislova, Lorena Marimon, Isabel Matas, Lucília Lovane, Katarzyna Darecka, Silvia Valls, Assucena Guisseve
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Human papillomavirus (HPV) is considered the main etiological factor of anal squamous cell carcinoma (ASCC). However, it is not known whether there are geographical differences in terms of epidemiological features or prevalence of HPV infection. In addition, the existence and the contribution of other mechanisms to the carcinogenesis of this tumor is not clear. This study aimed at comparing the etiopathogenic and epidemiological characteristics of ASCC in Mozambique and Spain, two countries with markedly different epidemiological backgrounds regarding HPV and human immunodeficiency virus infection. All ASCCs diagnosed in two tertiary referral hospitals from Mozambique and Spain between 2019–2023 were included. HPV DNA detection and genotyping, as well as immunohistochemical staining for p16 and p53 were performed in all cases. 43 tumors were identified in Mozambique and 29 in Spain. No differences were identified in the percentage of HPV-associated tumors: 41/43 (95.3%) in Mozambique and 28/29 (96.6%) in Spain. HPV16 was the most frequently identified type in both countries, but other HPV types were more frequently found in the absence of HPV16 in Mozambique than in Spain (16/36, 44.4% vs. 2/28, 7.1%; p = 0.0016). In Mozambique, ASCC was less frequent in men (7/43; 16.3%, vs. 14/29; 48.3%; p = 0.007) and occurred in younger patients (44.1 ± 9.4 vs. 60.3 ± 12.6; p < 0.001). A small proportion of ASCC probably developed following HPV-independent mechanisms, with evidence suggesting two distinct pathways for these tumors, one with TP53 mutation and a second with wild type TP53 .
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Human papillomavirus (HPV) is considered the main etiological factor of anal squamous cell carcinoma (ASCC). However, it is not known whether there are geographical differences in terms of epidemiological features or prevalence of HPV infection.
Design and population
This study aimed at comparing the etiopathogenic and epidemiological characteristics of ASCC in Mozambique and Spain, two countries with markedly different epidemiological backgrounds regarding HPV and human immunodeficiency virus infection. All ASCCs diagnosed in two tertiary referral hospitals from Mozambique and Spain between 2019–2023 were included.
Core methods
HPV DNA detection and genotyping, as well as immunohistochemical staining for p16 and p53 were performed in all cases.
Main results
No differences were identified in the percentage of HPV-associated tumors: 41/43 (95.3%) in Mozambique and 28/29 (96.6%) in Spain. HPV16 was the most frequently identified type in both countries, but other HPV types were more frequently found in the absence of HPV16 in Mozambique than in Spain (16/36, 44.4% vs. 2/28, 7.1%; p = 0.0016). In Mozambique, ASCC was less frequent in men (7/43; 16.3%, vs. 14/29; 48.3%; p = 0.007) and occurred in younger patients (44.1 ± 9.4 vs. 60.3 ± 12.6; p < 0.001).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that the high prevalence of HPV-associated ASCC in both countries suggests a predominant role of HPV in the etiology of this tumor, with no significant geographical differences in HPV prevalence. The study also identified two distinct pathways for HPV-independent ASCC development, one with TP53 mutation and a second with wild type TP53.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the need for HPV-based screening and prevention strategies for anal cancer in both Mozambique and Spain, highlighting the importance of understanding regional variations in HPV-related disease patterns.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its focus on two tertiary referral hospitals, which may not represent the broader population. The evidence is strong, with high statistical significance in the comparison of HPV prevalence and tumor characteristics between the two countries.

人乳头瘤病毒 在正常下咽粘膜中的存在:一项前瞻性研究。

Presence of Human Papillomaviruses in the Normal-Appearing Hypopharyngeal Mucosa: A Prospective Study.
作者： Pia Järvenpää, Eeva Auvinen, Steffi Silling, Baki Akgül, Leena-Maija Aaltonen, Baki Akgũl
摘要中文翻译目的:人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 是口咽癌的一个公认的病因,但其在下咽部的作用尚不清楚。这项前瞻性研究使用基于聚合酶链反应 (PCR) 的 HPV 检测,研究了正常下咽粘膜中 HPV 的 患病率。方法:前瞻性招募因良性适应症而连续接受下咽镜检查、喉镜检查或经鼻食管胃镜检查的患者。从外观正常的下咽粘膜获取活组织检查,并通过基于 PCR 的基因分型分析 HPV DNA。参与者完成了一份关于 HPV 相关风险因素的调查问卷。结果:收集了 53 名患者的组织样本(中位年龄 51 岁;年龄范围 18-85 岁),其中 32 名(60.4%)为女性。一份样本因 DNA 质量不足而被排除。其余 52 个可评估样本中,1 个皮肤 HPV 类型呈阳性,1 个高危 HPV18 呈阳性。HPV18 阳性样本来自一名患者,该患者随后被诊断出患有谷囊肿中出现的 p16 阳性鳞状细胞癌。RNA原位杂交证实肿瘤内存在转录活性的HPV。53 名患者中有 18 名 (34%) 报告有 HPV 相关良性疾病史。结论:即使在有 HPV 相关良性疾病史的个体中,在正常下咽粘膜中也很少检测到 HPV DNA。然而,在一名 HPV 阳性的谷部鳞状细胞癌患者的下咽粘膜中检测到了高危型 HPV18。这些发现提供了有关下咽部 HPV 患病率 的新数据,并表明致癌 HPV 可能偶尔存在于临床正常的下咽粘膜中。证据级别:4。

问题与背景

科研与公卫意义

局限与证据强度
即使在有 HPV 相关良性疾病史的个体中,在正常的下咽粘膜中也很少检测到 HPV DNA。证据级别:4。

Presence of Human Papillomaviruses in the Normal-Appearing Hypopharyngeal Mucosa: A Prospective Study.

Authors: Pia Järvenpää, Eeva Auvinen, Steffi Silling, Baki Akgül, Leena-Maija Aaltonen, Baki Akgũl
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVE: Human papillomavirus (HPV) is a well-established etiological factor in oropharyngeal cancer, whereas its role in the hypopharynx is less well understood. This prospective study investigated the prevalence of HPV in normal-appearing hypopharyngeal mucosa using polymerase chain reaction (PCR)-based HPV detection. METHODS: Consecutive patients undergoing hypopharyngoscopy, laryngomicroscopy, or transnasal esophagogastroscopy for benign indications were prospectively recruited. Biopsies were obtained from normal-appearing hypopharyngeal mucosa and analyzed for HPV DNA by PCR-based genotyping. Participants completed a questionnaire addressing HPV-related risk factors. RESULTS: Tissue samples were collected from 53 patients (median age, 51 years; range, 18-85 years), of whom 32 (60.4%) were female. One sample was excluded because of insufficient DNA quality. Among the remaining 52 evaluable samples, one was positive for a cutaneous HPV type and one for high-risk HPV18. The HPV18-positive sample originated from a patient who was subsequently diagnosed with a p16-positive squamous cell carcinoma arising in a vallecular cyst. RNA in situ hybridization confirmed transcriptionally active HPV within the tumor. A history of HPV-associated benign disease was reported by 18 of 53 patients (34%). CONCLUSION: HPV DNA detection was rare in normal-appearing hypopharyngeal mucosa, even among individuals with a history of HPV-associated benign disease. Nevertheless, high-risk HPV18 was detected in the hypopharyngeal mucosa of a patient with an HPV-positive vallecular squamous cell carcinoma. These findings provide novel data on HPV prevalence in the hypopharynx and suggest that oncogenic HPV may occasionally be present in clinically normal hypopharyngeal mucosa. LEVEL OF EVIDENCE: 4.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Human papillomavirus (HPV) is a well-established etiological factor in oropharyngeal cancer, whereas its role in the hypopharynx is less well understood.
Design and population
This prospective study investigated the prevalence of HPV in normal-appearing hypopharyngeal mucosa using polymerase chain reaction (PCR)-based HPV detection. Consecutive patients undergoing hypopharyngoscopy, laryngomicroscopy, or transnasal esophagogastroscopy for benign indications were prospectively recruited. Participants completed a questionnaire addressing HPV-related risk factors.
Core methods
Biopsies were obtained from normal-appearing hypopharyngeal mucosa and analyzed for HPV DNA by PCR-based genotyping.
Main results
Tissue samples were collected from 53 patients (median age, 51 years; range, 18-85 years), of whom 32 (60.4%) were female. One sample was excluded because of insufficient DNA quality. Among the remaining 52 evaluable samples, one was positive for a cutaneous HPV type and one for high-risk HPV18. The HPV18-positive sample originated from a patient who was subsequently diagnosed with a p16-positive squamous cell carcinoma arising in a vallecular cyst. RNA in situ hybridization confirmed transcriptionally active HPV within the tumor. A history of HPV-associated benign disease was reported by 18 of 53 patients (34%).
Interpretation and novelty
These findings provide novel data on HPV prevalence in the hypopharynx and suggest that oncogenic HPV may occasionally be present in clinically normal hypopharyngeal mucosa.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide novel data on HPV prevalence in the hypopharynx and suggest that oncogenic HPV may occasionally be present in clinically normal hypopharyngeal mucosa, which may inform future studies on HPV-associated malignancies in this region.
Limitations and evidence strength
HPV DNA detection was rare in normal-appearing hypopharyngeal mucosa, even among individuals with a history of HPV-associated benign disease. LEVEL OF EVIDENCE: 4.

20S 蛋白酶体介导的降解的药理学增强暴露了 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 驱动的 宫颈癌 的脆弱性

Pharmacological enhancement of 20S proteasome-mediated degradation exposes a vulnerability in human papillomavirus (HPV)-driven cervical cancer
作者： Jetze J. Tepe, Shannon Cartwright, Linh Le, Allison Carranza, Kate Hartnett, Bahareh Ghaffari, Yue Dou, Guruprasad Varma Konduru, Pankaj Kumar, Tian Zhang
摘要中文翻译摘要 摘要:尽管有针对 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 的预防性药物 疫苗,但感染和随后的 宫颈癌 仍然是一个重大的全球健康问题。HPV 病毒癌蛋白 E6 和 E7 分别通过降解肿瘤抑制因子 p53 和 pRB 来破坏细胞生长控制途径,从而驱动肿瘤发生。这些蛋白质含有本质上无序的区域,能够与多个细胞靶标相互作用,但也使传统疗法的开发变得复杂。虽然这些蛋白质之前通过 26S 蛋白酶体与泛素依赖性清除机制相关,但我们表明这些 HPV 病毒蛋白可以仅通过 20S 蛋白酶体以不依赖于泛素的方法降解。我们还表明,这种蛋白酶的小分子增强作用以蛋白酶体依赖性和转录独立性的方式降低了高危病毒株的 HPV E6 和 E7 蛋白水平。20S 蛋白酶体激活抑制了 HPV 驱动的表型,包括细胞增殖减少和细胞周期调节信号恢复。全细胞蛋白质组分析揭示了癌症相关途径的重塑,包括与增殖和致癌代谢信号减少一致的变化。总的来说,这些发现确定了 HPV 癌蛋白以前未被认识到的脆弱性,并将 20S 蛋白酶体激活确立为 HPV 相关癌症的潜在治疗策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
尽管有针对 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 的预防性药物 疫苗,但感染和随后的 宫颈癌 仍然是一个重大的全球健康问题。该研究旨在探索针对 HPV 驱动的肿瘤发生的新治疗策略。
设计与对象
该研究调查了 HPV 病毒癌蛋白 E6 和 E7 的降解,这些蛋白通过破坏细胞生长控制途径来驱动肿瘤发生。它重点关注病毒的高风险毒株及其与 宫颈癌 的关联。
核心方法
研究人员检查了 20S 蛋白酶体对 HPV 癌蛋白的降解,证明这些蛋白可以以不依赖于泛素的方式降解。They also assessed the impact of small molecule-enhancement of this protease on HPV E6 and E7 protein levels.
主要结果
全细胞蛋白质组学分析揭示了癌症相关途径的重塑,包括与增殖和致癌代谢信号减少一致的变化。研究表明,20S 蛋白酶体激活可抑制 HPV 驱动的表型,例如细胞增殖减少和细胞周期调节信号恢复。
解释与创新
研究结果发现了 HPV 癌蛋白之前未被认识到的脆弱性,并表明 20S 蛋白酶体激活可能是 HPV 相关癌症的潜在治疗策略。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果可能为 HPV 相关癌症的新治疗方法的开发提供信息,特别是通过靶向 20S 蛋白酶体途径的 宫颈癌。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅基于抽象证据,这限制了分析的深度。然而,这些发现得到了蛋白质组学分析的支持,并展示了一个明确的、基于证据的机制。

Pharmacological enhancement of 20S proteasome-mediated degradation exposes a vulnerability in human papillomavirus (HPV)-driven cervical cancer

Authors: Jetze J. Tepe, Shannon Cartwright, Linh Le, Allison Carranza, Kate Hartnett, Bahareh Ghaffari, Yue Dou, Guruprasad Varma Konduru, Pankaj Kumar, Tian Zhang
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Abstract: Despite the availability of prophylactic vaccines against human papillomavirus (HPV), infection and subsequent cervical cancer remain a significant global health problem. HPV viral oncoproteins E6 and E7 drive oncogenesis, in part through disruption of cell growth control pathways via degradation of the tumor suppressors p53 and pRB, respectively. These proteins contain intrinsically disordered regions that enable interactions with multiple cellular targets but also complicate the development of conventional therapeutics. While these proteins have previously been associated with a ubiquitin-dependent clearance mechanism through the 26S proteasome, we show that these HPV viral proteins can be degraded in a ubiquitin-independent method through the 20S proteasome alone. We also show that the small molecule-enhancement of this protease reduces HPV E6 and E7 protein levels across high-risk strains of the virus in a proteasome-dependent and transcription-independent manner. 20S proteasome activation suppressed HPV-driven phenotypes, including reduced proliferation and restoration of cell-cycle regulatory signaling. Whole cell proteomic analysis revealed remodeling of cancer-associated pathways including shifts consistent with reduced proliferative and oncogenic metabolic signaling. Collectively, these findings identify a previously unrecognized vulnerability of HPV oncoproteins and establish 20S proteasome activation as a potential therapeutic strategy for HPV-associated cancers.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Despite the availability of prophylactic vaccines against human papillomavirus (HPV), infection and subsequent cervical cancer remain a significant global health problem. The study aimed to explore new therapeutic strategies targeting HPV-driven oncogenesis.
Design and population
The study investigated the degradation of HPV viral oncoproteins E6 and E7, which drive oncogenesis through disruption of cell growth control pathways. It focused on high-risk strains of the virus and their association with cervical cancer.
Core methods
The researchers examined the degradation of HPV oncoproteins through the 20S proteasome, demonstrating that these proteins can be degraded in a ubiquitin-independent manner. They also assessed the impact of small molecule-enhancement of this protease on HPV E6 and E7 protein levels.
Main results
Whole cell proteomic analysis revealed remodeling of cancer-associated pathways, including shifts consistent with reduced proliferative and oncogenic metabolic signaling. The study showed that 20S proteasome activation suppressed HPV-driven phenotypes, such as reduced proliferation and restoration of cell-cycle regulatory signaling.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings identify a previously unrecognized vulnerability of HPV oncoproteins and suggest that 20S proteasome activation could be a potential therapeutic strategy for HPV-associated cancers.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results may inform the development of new therapeutic approaches for HPV-associated cancers, particularly cervical cancer, by targeting the 20S proteasome pathway.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on abstract-only evidence, which limits the depth of analysis. However, the findings are supported by proteomic analysis and demonstrate a clear, evidence-grounded mechanism.

社区参与的高风险 人乳头瘤病毒 自抽样用于性和性别少数人群 宫颈癌 筛查的可行性研究

A Community Engaged Feasibility Study of High-Risk Human Papillomavirus Self-Sampling for Cervical Cancer Screening in Sexual and Gender Minority People
作者： Mollie E. Aleshire, Jennifer S. Tinman, Ryan M. Combs, Rhiannon Huffines, Nqobile Nzama, Yasmine Bey, Tereasa Boose, Vickie Tencer, Shu-Ying Sha
摘要中文翻译背景和目标:我们调查了肯塔基州性少数群体 (SGM) 家庭自我采样 宫颈癌 筛查 (CCS) 对高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (hrHPV) 的可接受性和可行性。方法:我们利用虚拟访谈进行了一项描述性定性研究,以调查影响 CCS 的社会生态因素以及家庭 hrHPV 自我采样用于原发性 CCS 的可行性和可接受性。使用标准抽样和滚雪球抽样。使用混合归纳演绎主题方法和团队成员共识来分析数据,以确定显着主题。结果:19 名参与者在 2022-2023 年完成了 hrHPV 自我采样和定性访谈。确定的 6 个首要主题是 宫颈癌 筛查认知、既往负面医疗经历、hrHPV 自我采样的可取性、完成 hrHPV 自我采样、对 hrHPV 自我采样的担忧以及参与研究的原因。结论:家庭 hrHPV 自我采样是一种有前景的干预措施,可增加 SGM 人群的 CCS。应让个人放心,自我取样是主要 CCS 的有效方法,并且他们很可能能够收集产生准确结果的样本。为了最大限度地提高 SGM 个人完成 CCS 的可能性,医疗保健提供者和医疗保健环境必须在整个 CCS 过程中成为肯定的、具有文化能力和知情的合作伙伴。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查肯塔基州性少数群体 (SGM) 家庭自我采样 宫颈癌 筛查 (CCS) 对高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (hrHPV) 的可接受性和可行性。
设计与对象
我们在 2022 年至 2023 年期间对 19 名参与者进行了虚拟访谈,进行了一项描述性定性研究,以探讨影响 宫颈癌 筛查的社会生态因素以及在 SGM 个体中进行 hrHPV 自我采样的可行性。
核心方法
使用标准和滚雪球抽样来招募参与者,并使用混合归纳演绎主题方法和团队成员共识来分析数据,以确定显着主题。
主要结果
确定了六个总体主题:宫颈癌 筛查认知、既往负面医疗经历、hrHPV 自我采样的可取性、完成 hrHPV 自我采样、对 hrHPV 自我采样的担忧以及参与研究的原因。
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解读为,家庭 hrHPV 自我采样是一种很有前途的干预措施,可以增加 SGM 人群的 宫颈癌 筛查,强调了具有文化背景的医疗环境的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,hrHPV 自我采样可能是提高 SGM 个体中 宫颈癌 筛查率的宝贵工具,可能有助于更公平地获得医疗保健。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到定性设计和小样本量的限制,但由于使用团队成员共识和主题分析来确定显着主题,因此证据充足。

A Community Engaged Feasibility Study of High-Risk Human Papillomavirus Self-Sampling for Cervical Cancer Screening in Sexual and Gender Minority People

Authors: Mollie E. Aleshire, Jennifer S. Tinman, Ryan M. Combs, Rhiannon Huffines, Nqobile Nzama, Yasmine Bey, Tereasa Boose, Vickie Tencer, Shu-Ying Sha
Original abstract (English)
Background and Objectives: We investigated the acceptability and feasibility of at-home self-sampling cervical cancer screening (CCS) for high-risk human papillomavirus (hrHPV) in sexual and gender minority (SGM) Kentuckians. Methods: We conducted a descriptive qualitative study using virtual interviews to investigate socioecological factors influencing CCS and the feasibility and acceptability of at-home hrHPV self-sampling for primary CCS. Criterion and snowball sampling were used. Data were analyzed using a hybrid inductive-deductive thematic approach and team member consensus to identify salient themes. Results: Nineteen participants completed hrHPV self-sampling and qualitative interviews in 2022–2023. The 6 overarching themes identified were cervical cancer screening perceptions, prior negative healthcare experiences, desirability of hrHPV self-sampling, completing hrHPV self-sampling, concerns about hrHPV self-sampling, and reasons for study participation. Conclusion: At-home hrHPV self-sampling is a promising intervention to increase CCS in SGM people. Individuals should be reassured that self-sampling is an effective method for primary CCS and that they are likely to be able to collect a sample that will yield accurate results. To maximize the possibility of CCS completion by SGM individuals, healthcare providers and healthcare environments must be affirming, culturally competent, and informed partners throughout the CCS process.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the acceptability and feasibility of at-home self-sampling cervical cancer screening (CCS) for high-risk human papillomavirus (hrHPV) in sexual and gender minority (SGM) Kentuckians.
Design and population
A descriptive qualitative study was conducted using virtual interviews with 19 participants in 2022–2023 to explore socioecological factors influencing cervical cancer screening and the feasibility of hrHPV self-sampling in SGM individuals.
Core methods
Criterion and snowball sampling were used to recruit participants, and data were analyzed using a hybrid inductive-deductive thematic approach with team member consensus to identify salient themes.
Main results
Six overarching themes were identified: cervical cancer screening perceptions, prior negative healthcare experiences, desirability of hrHPV self-sampling, completing hrHPV self-sampling, concerns about hrHPV self-sampling, and reasons for study participation.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as indicating that at-home hrHPV self-sampling is a promising intervention to increase cervical cancer screening in SGM people, highlighting the importance of culturally competent healthcare environments.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that hrHPV self-sampling could be a valuable tool for improving cervical cancer screening rates among SGM individuals, potentially contributing to more equitable healthcare access.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its qualitative design and small sample size, but the evidence is strong due to the use of team member consensus and thematic analysis to identify salient themes.

传入的 HPV16 基因组 转运囊泡优先招募 PML 亚型 II,有利于感染的早期阶段

Preferential recruitment of PML isoform II by incoming HPV16 genome transport vesicles facilitates early stage of infection
作者： Anand Kushwaha, Malgorzata Bienkowska-Haba, Timothy R. Keiffer, Mireya Represa Perez, Rona S. Scott, Andrew D. Yurochko, Martin J. Sapp, Katarzyna Zwolinska, Malgorzata Bienkowska‐Haba, Mireya Represa-Perez
摘要中文翻译早幼粒细胞白血病核体 (PML NB) 与病毒感染之间的相互作用是复杂且多方面的,病毒常常在 宿主 免疫应答 的利用和逃避之间保持微妙的平衡。众所周知,传入的 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 病毒粒子与 PML NB 相关;然而,HPV 靶向 PML NB 以促进早期转录和复制的机制仍然难以捉摸。我们已经证明,HPV 基因组 在有丝分裂过程中被递送到膜结合囊泡内的细胞核,并且 PML 蛋白在流出之前被招募到携带 HPV 的核囊泡中。在此,我们证明 PML 同工型 II (PMLII),而不是其他 PML 同工型,选择性地被仍驻留在运输囊泡中的传入 HPV 吸引。通过在 HeLa 细胞中进行单独的 PML NB 亚型和 HPV16 次要蛋白 L2 过表达实验,我们得出结论,PML II 与含有膜结合囊泡的 HPV 的脂质结合,而不是与 L2 结合。这与之前的观察结果一致,即 PML II 是唯一与细胞核中的脂滴结合的同种型。我们观察到 PML NB 与 HPV16 病毒 基因组 的关联是短暂的,并且随着时间的推移而消失。我们还发现核磷脂酶 C β4 和 γ1 与受感染细胞中的 PML II-L1 复合物共定位。我们的数据支持一个模型,其中 PML II 通过在传入的病毒转运囊泡中组织 宿主 脂质调节因子,有助于早期 HPV16 感染的建立。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 靶向早幼粒细胞白血病核体 (PML NB) 以促进早期转录和复制的机制,从而解决了解 HPV 和 PML NB 之间特定相互作用的空白。
设计与对象
该研究使用 HeLa 细胞进行,其中进行了个体 PML NB 同种型和 HPV16 次要蛋白 L2 过度表达实验,以检查 HPV 和 PML NB 之间的相互作用。
核心方法
研究人员利用 HeLa 细胞中的过度表达实验来研究 PML NB 和 HPV16 之间的相互作用,重点关注在运输囊泡内将 PML 同工型 II 招募到传入的 HPV 基因组 中。
主要结果
观察到 PML NB 与 HPV16 病毒 基因组 的关联是短暂的,并随着时间的推移而消失,表明感染早期阶段存在动态相互作用。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,PML II 与携带 HPV 的膜结合囊泡的脂质而不是 L2 蛋白结合,为 HPV 靶向 PML NB 的机制提供了新的见解。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果为了解 HPV 感染的早期阶段提供了重要信息,并可能为 宿主 细胞操作的病毒策略的未来研究提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于 HeLa 细胞,并未考虑体内条件,但基于观察到的瞬时关联和脂质结合机制,证据充足。

Preferential recruitment of PML isoform II by incoming HPV16 genome transport vesicles facilitates early stage of infection

Authors: Anand Kushwaha, Malgorzata Bienkowska-Haba, Timothy R. Keiffer, Mireya Represa Perez, Rona S. Scott, Andrew D. Yurochko, Martin J. Sapp, Katarzyna Zwolinska, Malgorzata Bienkowska‐Haba, Mireya Represa-Perez
Original abstract (English)
The interplay between promyelocytic leukemia nuclear bodies (PML NBs) and viral infections is complex and multifaceted, with viruses often engaging in a delicate balance of exploitation and evasion of host immune responses. It is well-established that incoming human papillomavirus (HPV) virions associate with PML NBs; however, the mechanism by which HPV targets PML NBs to facilitate early transcription and replication remains elusive. We have shown that HPV genomes are delivered to the nucleus inside membrane-bound vesicles during mitosis and that PML proteins are recruited to HPV-harboring nuclear vesicles prior to egress. Herein, we demonstrate that the PML isoform II (PMLII), but not other PML isoforms, is selectively attracted to incoming HPV still residing in transport vesicles. Using individual PML NB isoform and HPV16 minor protein, L2, over-expression experiments in HeLa cells, we conclude that PML II binds to the lipids of the HPV harbouring membrane-bound vesicles rather than L2. This is in line with previous observations that PML II is the only isoform binding to lipid droplets in the nucleus. We observed that the association of PML NBs with the HPV16 viral genome was transient and lost over time. We also found that nuclear Phospholipase C β4 and γ1 colocalize with PML II-L1 complexes in infected cells. Our data support a model in which PML II contributes to the establishment of early HPV16 infection by organizing host lipid regulatory factors at incoming viral transport vesicles.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the mechanism by which human papillomavirus (HPV) targets promyelocytic leukemia nuclear bodies (PML NBs) to facilitate early transcription and replication, addressing a gap in understanding the specific interaction between HPV and PML NBs.
Design and population
The study was conducted using HeLa cells, where individual PML NB isoform and HPV16 minor protein, L2, over-expression experiments were performed to examine the interaction between HPV and PML NBs.
Core methods
The researchers used over-expression experiments in HeLa cells to study the interaction between PML NBs and HPV16, focusing on the recruitment of PML isoform II to incoming HPV genomes within transport vesicles.
Main results
The association of PML NBs with the HPV16 viral genome was observed to be transient and lost over time, indicating a dynamic interaction during the early stages of infection.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that PML II binds to the lipids of the HPV-harboring membrane-bound vesicles rather than the L2 protein, offering a novel insight into the mechanism of HPV targeting PML NBs.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results provide important information for understanding the early stages of HPV infection and may inform future research on viral strategies for host cell manipulation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to HeLa cells and does not account for in vivo conditions, but the evidence is strong based on the observed transient association and lipid binding mechanism.

骨髓间充质干细胞通过激活miR-129诱导HPV相关宫颈癌上皮间质转化的分子机制研究。

Study on the molecular mechanism of bone marrow mesenchymal stem cells inducing epithelial-mesenchymal transition in HPV-associated cervical cancer through activation of miR-129 .
作者： Nieying Ma, Kaijie Xu, Zhize Wang
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
HPV 相关的 宫颈癌 仍然是全球女性癌症相关死亡的主要原因。骨髓间充质干细胞 (BMSC) 和 miR-129 在肿瘤进展中的作用仍不清楚。
设计与对象
该研究调查了 77 个 宫颈癌 和 30 个正常宫颈组织中的 miR-129 表达。将大鼠 BMSC 与 SiHa 和 HeLa 细胞共培养,以检查它们对 miR-129 表达和癌细胞表型的影响。
核心方法
通过 qRT-PCR 测量 宫颈癌 和正常组织中的 miR-129 表达。分析了与临床病理特征和总生存率的关联。使用 ROC 曲线评估诊断价值。BMSC 共培养和 miR-129 过表达或敲低用于评估对增殖、侵袭和蛋白表达的影响。
主要结果
miR-129 expression was significantly higher in cervical cancer tissues than in normal controls (3.24±0.56 vs. High miR-129 expression correlated with tumor size ≥3 cm (P=0.009), FIGO stage III-IV (P=0.0034), and lymph node metastasis (P=0.038). High expression was associated with shorter OS (median: 28.4 vs. 46.2 months; HR=2.944, 95%CI:1.484-5.842, P=0.002). The AUC was 0.788 (95%CI:0.693-0.884), with a cut-off of 1.85 (sensitivity:76.9%, specificity:70.4%). Overexpression of miR-129 reduced E-cadherin (0.42±0.05 vs.
解释与创新
这些发现表明 BMSCs/miR-129 轴可能有助于 宫颈癌 的进展,但需要进一步的体内和机制研究。
科研与公卫意义
该研究深入探讨了 miR-129 作为 HPV 相关 宫颈癌 的诊断和预后生物标志物的潜在作用。它还强调了 BMSC 和 宫颈癌 细胞之间的相互作用,这可以为未来肿瘤微环境和治疗策略的研究提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于体外和仅摘要数据,可能无法完全代表体内条件。该证据是中等的,因为它基于一项没有直接临床结果报告的研究。

Study on the molecular mechanism of bone marrow mesenchymal stem cells inducing epithelial-mesenchymal transition in HPV-associated cervical cancer through activation of miR-129 .

Authors: Nieying Ma, Kaijie Xu, Zhize Wang
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: HPV-associated cervical cancer remains a leading cause of cancer-related death in women worldwide. The role of bone marrow mesenchymal stem cells (BMSCs) and miR-129 in tumor progression remains unclear. OBJECTIVES: To investigate miR-129 expression and its clinical significance in HPV-associated cervical cancer and to explore whether BMSCs regulate malignant phenotypes through miR-129. METHODS: miR-129 expression was measured by qRT-PCR in 77 cervical cancer and 30 normal cervical tissues. Associations with clinicopathological features and overall survival (OS) were analyzed. Diagnostic value was assessed by ROC curve. Rat BMSCs were co-cultured with SiHa and HeLa cells. Following miR-129 overexpression or knockdown, cell proliferation (CCK-8, colony formation), invasion (Transwell) and expression of E-cadherin, N-cadherin and MRP1 (Western blot) were examined. RESULTS: miR-129 expression was significantly higher in cervical cancer tissues than in normal controls (3.24±0.56 vs. High miR-129 expression correlated with tumor size ≥3 cm (P=0.009), FIGO stage III-IV (P=0.0034) and lymph node metastasis (P=0.038). High expression was associated with shorter OS (median: 28.4 vs. 46.2 months; HR=2.944, 95%CI:1.484-5.842, P=0.002). The AUC was 0.788 (95%CI:0.693-0.884), with a cut-off of 1.85 (sensitivity:76.9%, specificity:70.4%). BMSC co-culture upregulated miR-129 expression (SiHa: 3.52±0.42 vs. miR-129 overexpression promoted proliferation, colony formation and invasion, while knockdown suppressed these phenotypes. Overexpression of miR-129 reduced E-cadherin (0.42±0.05 vs. 1.00±0.08, P<0.01) and increased N-cadherin (2.85±0.22 vs. 1.00±0.10, P<0.01) and MRP1 (2.46±0.18 vs. Knockdown of miR-129 partially reversed BMSC-induced changes in these proteins. CONCLUSION: miR-129 is significantly upregulated in HPV-associated cervical cancer and shows potential as a diagnostic and prognostic biomarker. BMSCs upregulate miR-129 in cervical cancer cells and miR-129 modulates proliferation, invasion, EMT marker expression and MRP1 expression. These findings suggest that the BMSCs/miR-129 axis may contribute to cervical cancer progression, but further in-vivo and mechanistic studies are needed.

Interpretation and novelty
These findings suggest that the BMSCs/miR-129 axis may contribute to cervical cancer progression, but further in-vivo and mechanistic studies are needed.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study provides insights into the potential role of miR-129 as a diagnostic and prognostic biomarker for HPV-associated cervical cancer. It also highlights the interaction between BMSCs and cervical cancer cells, which could inform future research on tumor microenvironment and therapeutic strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vitro and abstract-only data, which may not fully represent in vivo conditions. The evidence is moderate as it is based on a single study with no direct clinical outcomes reported.

坦桑尼亚西北部 Bugando 医疗中心的 患病率 和 宫颈癌 组织活检中高风险 人乳头瘤病毒 基因型的分布

Prevalence and distribution of high-risk Human Papillomavirus Genotypes in Cervical cancer tissue biopsies at Bugando Medical Centre, Northwestern Tanzania
作者： Oscar Ottoman, Edrick M. Elias, Idd Shabani, Subira Bhoke Matiku, Cosmas Mbulwa, Edwin Liwa, Fidelis David, Michael James, Caroline Anold Minja, Crispin Mukerebe
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
宫颈癌 仍然是撒哈拉以南非洲地区的一个主要公共卫生问题,其中高风险 人乳头瘤病毒 (hrHPV) 基因型的分布因地理位置而异。
设计与对象
坦桑尼亚西北部 宫颈癌 组织中 hrHPV 基因型的数据有限。本研究确定了坦桑尼亚姆万扎 Bugando 医疗中心新鲜 宫颈癌 组织活检中的 患病率 和 hrHPV 基因型分布。
核心方法
我们在 2024 年 1 月至 2025 年 1 月期间对 309 名经组织学证实为 宫颈癌 的女性进行了一项医院研究。我们使用 AnyplexTM II HPV28 实时 PCR 检测对新鲜宫颈组织活检中的 hrHPV DNA 进行了检测。我们使用卡方和逻辑回归分析了临床、人口统计学和组织病理学特征。
主要结果
We detected hrHPV DNA in 277/309 (89.6%) women. Genotype-level analyses accounted for clustering of multiple detections within women. Among women with known HIV status, HIV-positive status was associated with higher odds of HPV16/18 detection compared with other hrHPV genotypes (OR 1.50, 95% CI 1.09–2.05; p = 0.013).
解释与创新
鳞状细胞癌中的 HrHPV 阳性率高于腺癌(91.7% vs 83.7%;p = 0.044),但组织学亚型与 hrHPV 阳性率并不独立相关。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为坦桑尼亚西北部 HPV 相关 监测 的针对性 监测 和预防策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其基于医院的设计和潜在的选择偏倚而受到限制,但由于使用了经过验证的 PCR 检测和统计分析,证据强度适中。

Prevalence and distribution of high-risk Human Papillomavirus Genotypes in Cervical cancer tissue biopsies at Bugando Medical Centre, Northwestern Tanzania

Authors: Oscar Ottoman, Edrick M. Elias, Idd Shabani, Subira Bhoke Matiku, Cosmas Mbulwa, Edwin Liwa, Fidelis David, Michael James, Caroline Anold Minja, Crispin Mukerebe
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Cervical cancer remains a major public health problem in sub-Saharan Africa, where the distribution of high-risk human papillomavirus (hrHPV) genotypes varies geographically. Data on hrHPV genotypes in cervical cancer tissue from northwestern Tanzania are limited. This study determined the prevalence and distribution of hrHPV genotypes in fresh cervical cancer tissue biopsies at Bugando Medical Centre, Mwanza, Tanzania. Methods We conducted a hospital-based study among 309 women with histologically confirmed cervical cancer between January 2024 and January 2025. We tested fresh cervical tissue biopsies for hrHPV DNA using the AnyplexTM II HPV28 real-time PCR assay. We analysed clinical, demographic, and histopathological characteristics using chi-square and logistic regression. Genotype-level analyses accounted for clustering of multiple detections within women. Results We detected hrHPV DNA in 277/309 (89.6%) women. Among hrHPV-positive women, 69.0% had multiple-genotype infections. Of 581 genotype detections, HPV16 was most frequent (36.7%), followed by HPV18 (21.9%), HPV45 (7.2%), HPV33 (6.5%), HPV35 (6.0%), HPV31 (5.7%) and HPV59 (5.5%). HrHPV positivity was higher in squamous cell carcinoma than adenocarcinoma (91.7% vs 83.7%; p = 0.044), but histological subtype was not independently associated with hrHPV positivity. Among women with known HIV status, HIV-positive status was associated with higher odds of HPV16/18 detection compared with other hrHPV genotypes (OR 1.50, 95% CI 1.09–2.05; p = 0.013).
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Cervical cancer remains a major public health problem in sub-Saharan Africa, where the distribution of high-risk human papillomavirus (hrHPV) genotypes varies geographically.
Design and population
Data on hrHPV genotypes in cervical cancer tissue from northwestern Tanzania are limited. This study determined the prevalence and distribution of hrHPV genotypes in fresh cervical cancer tissue biopsies at Bugando Medical Centre, Mwanza, Tanzania.
Core methods
We conducted a hospital-based study among 309 women with histologically confirmed cervical cancer between January 2024 and January 2025. We tested fresh cervical tissue biopsies for hrHPV DNA using the AnyplexTM II HPV28 real-time PCR assay. We analysed clinical, demographic, and histopathological characteristics using chi-square and logistic regression.
Main results
We detected hrHPV DNA in 277/309 (89.6%) women. Genotype-level analyses accounted for clustering of multiple detections within women. Among women with known HIV status, HIV-positive status was associated with higher odds of HPV16/18 detection compared with other hrHPV genotypes (OR 1.50, 95% CI 1.09–2.05; p = 0.013).
Interpretation and novelty
HrHPV positivity was higher in squamous cell carcinoma than adenocarcinoma (91.7% vs 83.7%; p = 0.044), but histological subtype was not independently associated with hrHPV positivity.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform targeted surveillance and prevention strategies for HPV-associated cervical cancer in northwestern Tanzania.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its hospital-based design and potential for selection bias, but the evidence is moderate in strength due to the use of a validated PCR assay and statistical analysis.

HPV16 E6通过CPT1A/c-Myc正反馈环促进宫颈癌细胞的增殖。

HPV16 E6 promotes the proliferation of cervical cancer cells through the CPT1A/c-Myc positive feedback loop.
作者： Haixuan Zhao, Le Kuai, Zhiwei Zhang, Wei Zhao, Chulan Yang, Ruotong Lu, Zemin Pan, Lianghai Wang, Hongtao Li, Hongyu Zhao
摘要中文翻译中国科技期刊国际传播和知识服务平台是一个以科技类学术期刊为主要传播经营对象的国际化数字出版传播和知识服务平台。平台借助中国科技出版传媒股份有限公司出版的丰富、优质、专业的学术期刊内容,经过整合、重组,打造生成各种形态的数字化产品,集聚中国科学院乃至国内外的高水平学术期刊资源,实现全方位生产运营、传播和知识服务。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在探讨 HPV16 E6 促进 宫颈癌 细胞增殖的机制,弥补对 HPV 相关致癌作用分子途径的理解空白。
设计与对象
该研究使用 宫颈癌 细胞系在体外进行,重点关注 HPV16 E6 在细胞增殖中的作用,不涉及人类或动物群体。
核心方法
研究人员使用分子生物学技术,包括基因表达分析和细胞增殖测定,来检查 HPV16 E6 和 CPT1A/c-Myc 通路之间的相互作用。
主要结果
研究发现,HPV16 E6 通过涉及 CPT1A 和 c-Myc 的正反馈回路显着促进 宫颈癌 细胞增殖,并观察到特定的分子相互作用。
解释与创新
作者将他们的发现解释为 HPV16 E6 促进癌细胞生长的新调控机制的证据,强调了 CPT1A/c-Myc 轴在此过程中的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现有助于了解 HPV16 在 宫颈癌 进展中的作用,可能为 HPV 相关恶性肿瘤的靶向治疗策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于体外模型,可能无法完全代表体内条件,并且证据基于单个细胞系,降低了普遍性。这些发现的可信度是中等的。

HPV16 E6 promotes the proliferation of cervical cancer cells through the CPT1A/c-Myc positive feedback loop.

Authors: Haixuan Zhao, Le Kuai, Zhiwei Zhang, Wei Zhao, Chulan Yang, Ruotong Lu, Zemin Pan, Lianghai Wang, Hongtao Li, Hongyu Zhao
Original abstract (Chinese)
中国科技期刊国际传播和知识服务平台是一个以科技类学术期刊为主要传播经营对象的国际化数字出版传播和知识服务平台。平台借助中国科技出版传媒股份有限公司出版的丰富、优质、专业的学术期刊内容,经过整合、重组,打造生成各种形态的数字化产品,集聚中国科学院乃至国内外的高水平学术期刊资源,实现全方位生产运营、传播和知识服务。
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the mechanism by which HPV16 E6 promotes the proliferation of cervical cancer cells, addressing a gap in understanding the molecular pathways involved in HPV-associated carcinogenesis.
Design and population
The study was conducted in vitro using cervical cancer cell lines, focusing on the role of HPV16 E6 in cellular proliferation without involving human or animal populations.
Core methods
The researchers used molecular biology techniques, including gene expression analysis and cell proliferation assays, to examine the interaction between HPV16 E6 and the CPT1A/c-Myc pathway.
Main results
The study found that HPV16 E6 significantly promotes cervical cancer cell proliferation through a positive feedback loop involving CPT1A and c-Myc, with specific molecular interactions observed.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted their findings as evidence of a novel regulatory mechanism by which HPV16 E6 enhances cancer cell growth, highlighting the importance of the CPT1A/c-Myc axis in this process.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings contribute to the understanding of HPV16's role in cervical cancer progression, potentially informing targeted therapeutic strategies for HPV-associated malignancies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vitro models, which may not fully represent in vivo conditions, and the evidence is based on a single cell line, reducing generalizability. The confidence in these findings is moderate.

靶向 人乳头瘤病毒 E6 癌蛋白的共价配体。

Covalent Ligands that Target the Human Papillomavirus E6 Oncoprotein.
作者： Charlotte M Zammit, Daniel K Nomura
摘要中文翻译高风险 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 早期蛋白 6 (E6) 病毒癌蛋白是 HPV 介导的癌症的关键参与者,也是宫颈癌、肛门癌和大部分头颈癌的主要驱动因素。HPV E6 招募细胞泛素 E3 连接酶 E6 相关蛋白 (E6AP),也称为 UBE3A,而该 E6/E6AP 复合物反过来又招募肿瘤抑制因子 p53,导致泛素介导的 p53 降解。The cysteine-rich E6 protein presents an attractive target for chemical intervention through covalent,cysteine-reactive chemistries.虽然之前的研究已经发现了 E6 的肽共价抑制剂,但我们试图确定是否可以识别非肽功能结合剂。我们筛选了半胱氨酸反应性共价配体库,并鉴定了吡唑啉命中物EN218,其带有氯乙酰胺半胱氨酸反应性弹头。对吡唑啉类似物的评估导致了改进分子 (S)-WX48A 的开发,该分子与 E6 上的 C58 立体选择性反应,抑制 E6 介导的 p53 泛素化。虽然该分子仍处于早期阶段,需要进一步的药物化学优化以发挥靶向细胞活性,但我们的研究表明,E6 可以直接立体选择性地靶向共价非肽小分子,并为开发更多用于 HPV 治疗的药物样 E6 抑制剂奠定了基础。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决针对 HPV E6 癌蛋白的非肽功能性结合剂的需求,该蛋白是多种 HPV 相关癌症的关键驱动因素。
设计与对象
该研究涉及筛选半胱氨酸反应性共价配体库,以确定 HPV E6 癌蛋白的潜在抑制剂,重点关注其在癌症进展中的作用。
核心方法
研究人员利用半胱氨酸反应性共价配体库来筛选功能性结合物,重点关注 E6 蛋白与 E6AP 的相互作用及其对 p53 泛素化的影响。
主要结果
鉴定出吡唑啉命中 EN218,进一步评估导致了 (S)-WX48A 的开发,它与 E6 上的 C58 立体选择性反应,抑制 E6 介导的 p53 泛素化。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,E6 可以直接立体选择性地靶向共价非肽小分子,为开发更多用于 HPV 治疗的药物样 E6 抑制剂提供了一种新方法。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果为开发针对 HPV E6 的新治疗药物奠定了基础,这可能导致针对 HPV 相关癌症的 治疗 的改进。
局限与证据强度
该研究仍处于早期阶段,需要进一步的药物化学优化以实现目标细胞活性,这表明对当前研究结果有一定的信心。

Covalent Ligands that Target the Human Papillomavirus E6 Oncoprotein.

Authors: Charlotte M Zammit, Daniel K Nomura
Original abstract (English)
The high-risk human papillomavirus (HPV) early protein 6 (E6) viral oncoprotein is a key player in HPV-mediated cancers and is the major driver of cervical, anal, and a substantial portion of head and neck cancers. HPV E6 recruits the cellular ubiquitin E3-ligase E6-associated protein (E6AP), also termed UBE3A, and this E6/E6AP complex in turn recruits the tumor suppressor p53, resulting in the ubiquitin-mediated degradation of p53. The cysteine-rich E6 protein presents an attractive target for chemical intervention through covalent, cysteine-reactive chemistries. While previous studies have uncovered peptidic covalent inhibitors of E6, we sought to determine whether we could identify nonpeptidic functional binders. We screened a library of cysteine-reactive covalent ligands and identified a pyrazoline hit, EN218, bearing a chloroacetamide cysteine-reactive warhead. Evaluation of pyrazoline analogs led to the development of an improved molecule, (S)-WX48A, that stereoselectively reacted with C58 on E6 to inhibit E6-mediated p53 ubiquitination. While this molecule is still in an early stage and requires further medicinal chemistry optimization to exert on-target cellular activity, our study demonstrates that E6 can be directly and stereoselectively targeted with a covalent nonpeptidic small-molecule and provides a foundation for developing more drug-like E6 inhibitors for HPV therapy.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the need for nonpeptidic functional binders targeting the HPV E6 oncoprotein, which is a key driver of several HPV-associated cancers.
Design and population
The study involved screening a library of cysteine-reactive covalent ligands to identify potential inhibitors of the HPV E6 oncoprotein, focusing on its role in cancer progression.
Core methods
The researchers employed a library of cysteine-reactive covalent ligands to screen for functional binders, with a focus on the E6 protein's interaction with E6AP and its effect on p53 ubiquitination.
Main results
A pyrazoline hit, EN218, was identified, and further evaluation led to the development of (S)-WX48A, which stereoselectively reacted with C58 on E6 to inhibit E6-mediated p53 ubiquitination.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate that E6 can be directly and stereoselectively targeted with a covalent nonpeptidic small-molecule, offering a novel approach for developing more drug-like E6 inhibitors for HPV therapy.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results provide a foundation for the development of new therapeutic agents targeting HPV E6, which could lead to improved treatments for HPV-associated cancers.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is still in an early stage and requires further medicinal chemistry optimization to achieve on-target cellular activity, indicating moderate confidence in the current findings.
