肯尼亚西南部通过以 MSM 为重点的服务招募的参与者中口腔 人乳头瘤病毒 的自然史:一项为期 18 个月的前瞻性 队列研究 研究

Natural history of oral human papillomavirus among participants recruited through MSM-focused services in southwestern Kenya: an 18-month prospective cohort study
作者： Maricianah Atieno Onono, Protus Omondi, Mark Makau, Fredrick Otieno, Kevin Omondi Owuor, George Otieno, Ibrahim Ndirangu, Simon Irungu, Lizzie Kabete, Imeldah Wakhungu
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
口腔 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) DNA 与一部分口咽癌相关。本研究旨在描述口腔 HPV 发病率、发病率、同型持续性和首次未检测的特征,并检查通过肯尼亚 MSM 重点服务招募的参与者的相关因素。
设计与对象
2023 年 7 月至 12 月期间,我们通过肯尼亚基苏木县的两个重点人群诊所招募了 398 名成年人,按艾滋病毒感染状况划分的人数相同。The study is an 18-month prospective 队列研究 study focusing on participants recruited through MSM-focused services.
核心方法
在基线以及第 6、12 和 18 个月时收集口腔冲洗样本,并通过 PCR 和有针对性的高风险基因分型来检测 HPV DNA。
主要结果
Among 398 participants, oral HPV DNA prevalence was 7·8% at baseline, increased to 21·0% at month 12, and declined to 14·9% at month 18. The reported overall incidence rate was 82·0 per 1,000 person-months; there was no statistical evidence of a difference by HIV status (IRR 1·01, 95% CI 0·74–1·39), although the confidence interval was imprecise.
解释与创新
Receptive oral sex was associated with prevalent detection (aOR 2·78, 95% CI 1·17–6·42) and same-type persistence (aOR 4·14, 95% CI 1·48–11·87). These findings highlight the role of sexual behavior in oral HPV dynamics.
科研与公卫意义
这些发现促进了对包容性 HPV-疫苗接种 策略以及 HIV 和性健康服务的评估,但并不能单独确定 疫苗 的有效性或证明政策改变的合理性。
局限与证据强度
HIV状态与HPV感染率之间的置信区间不够精确,且该研究未直接评估9价HPV疫苗的有效性或政策影响。

Natural history of oral human papillomavirus among participants recruited through MSM-focused services in southwestern Kenya: an 18-month prospective cohort study

Authors: Maricianah Atieno Onono, Protus Omondi, Mark Makau, Fredrick Otieno, Kevin Omondi Owuor, George Otieno, Ibrahim Ndirangu, Simon Irungu, Lizzie Kabete, Imeldah Wakhungu
Original abstract (English)
Oral human papillomavirus (HPV) DNA is associated with a subset of oropharyngeal cancers. We characterised oral HPV prevalence, incidence, same-type persistence, and first non-detection, and examined associated factors among participants recruited through MSM-focused services in Kenya. Between July and December 2023, we enrolled 398 adults through two key-population clinics in Kisumu County, Kenya, with equal numbers by HIV status. Oral-rinse specimens were collected at baseline and at months 6, 12, and 18 and tested for HPV DNA by PCR with targeted high-risk genotyping. Among 398 participants, oral HPV DNA prevalence was 7·8% at baseline, increased to 21·0% at month 12, and declined to 14·9% at month 18. The reported overall incidence rate was 82·0 per 1,000 person-months; there was no statistical evidence of a difference by HIV status (IRR 1·01, 95% CI 0·74–1·39), although the confidence interval was imprecise. Same-type high-risk HPV was detected at consecutive visits in 18 participants (4·5% of the cohort), corresponding to 35 participant-genotype combinations and 38 consecutive-interval persistence events, and more than half of genotype detections were absent at the next scheduled six-month visit. Receptive oral sex was associated with prevalent detection (aOR 2·78, 95% CI 1·17–6·42) and same-type persistence (aOR 4·14, 95% CI 1·48–11·87). Several frequently detected high-risk genotypes are included in the 9-valent HPV vaccine, whereas other detected high-risk types are not vaccine antigens. These findings motivate evaluation of inclusive HPV-vaccination strategies alongside HIV and sexual-health services, but do not alone establish vaccine effectiveness or justify a policy change.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Oral human papillomavirus (HPV) DNA is associated with a subset of oropharyngeal cancers. This study aimed to characterise oral HPV prevalence, incidence, same-type persistence, and first non-detection, and examine associated factors among participants recruited through MSM-focused services in Kenya.
Design and population
Between July and December 2023, we enrolled 398 adults through two key-population clinics in Kisumu County, Kenya, with equal numbers by HIV status. The study is an 18-month prospective cohort study focusing on participants recruited through MSM-focused services.
Core methods
Oral-rinse specimens were collected at baseline and at months 6, 12, and 18 and tested for HPV DNA by PCR with targeted high-risk genotyping.
Main results
Among 398 participants, oral HPV DNA prevalence was 7·8% at baseline, increased to 21·0% at month 12, and declined to 14·9% at month 18. The reported overall incidence rate was 82·0 per 1,000 person-months; there was no statistical evidence of a difference by HIV status (IRR 1·01, 95% CI 0·74–1·39), although the confidence interval was imprecise.
Interpretation and novelty
Receptive oral sex was associated with prevalent detection (aOR 2·78, 95% CI 1·17–6·42) and same-type persistence (aOR 4·14, 95% CI 1·48–11·87). These findings highlight the role of sexual behavior in oral HPV dynamics.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings motivate evaluation of inclusive HPV-vaccination strategies alongside HIV and sexual-health services, but do not alone establish vaccine effectiveness or justify a policy change.
Limitations and evidence strength
The confidence interval for the incidence rate by HIV status was imprecise, and the study did not assess vaccine effectiveness or policy implications directly.

10 至 12 岁儿童的临床医生培训、短信和 人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗接种

Clinician Training, Text Messages, and Human Papillomavirus Vaccination Among Children Aged 10 to 12 Years
作者： Stephanie A. S. Staras, Carma L. Bylund, Matthew J. Gurka, Lindsay A. Thompson, Marta D. Hansen, Tuo Lin, Pengchen Wang, Gabriela Sanchez, Xiaofei Chi, Christopher A. Harle
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估临床医生沟通培训和针对家长的短信对增加 11 至 12 岁儿童 HPV 疫苗接种 的有效性。值得注意的是,青少年中的 HPV 疫苗接种 率仍然低于建议值。
设计与对象
这是一项双重随机、二级巢式临床试验,于 2022 年 2 月至 2024 年 9 月在佛罗里达州 30 个儿科初级保健诊所的临床医生、未接受过 3 种青少年 HPV 疫苗 中任何一种的 10 至 12 岁儿童及其父母中进行。
核心方法
前两组临床医生完成了 1 小时的沟通培训和 5 分钟的强化课程。诊所内的患者按照 1:1:1 的比例随机分为 3 个短信组,并采用以下沟通策略:(1) 捆绑、(2) 重点突出、(3) 无短信。分析考虑了临床层面的聚类。
主要结果
At baseline, 59 clinicians were included, with 17 randomized to the focused group, 21 to the bundled group, and 21 to the deferred group. The mean pre-post clinic-level difference for HPV vaccine initiation was 28.2 (95% CI, 25.3-31.1) percentage points and for completion was 16.1 (95% CI, 13.3-18.9) percentage points. Risk ratios for clinician training and text messaging did not differ between groups for initiation or completion. Among patients whose parents received text messages vs not, the risk ratio for initiation was 1.17 (95% CI, 1.08-1.26) and for completion was 1.29 (95% CI, 1.06-1.56).
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,临床水平 HPV 疫苗接种 随着时间的推移而增加,但通过临床医生培训或短信的随机分配,未检测到 HPV 疫苗接种 的差异。收到后,短信与较高的 HPV 疫苗接种 相关。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,针对家长的短信可能是提高 10 至 12 岁儿童 HPV 疫苗接种 感染率的有用工具,这可以为未来提高 疫苗接种 覆盖率的公共卫生策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究具有较高的置信度,因为它是一项样本量较大的随机试验,但临床医生培训或短信组的 疫苗接种 率缺乏差异可能会限制这些干预措施的证据强度。

Clinician Training, Text Messages, and Human Papillomavirus Vaccination Among Children Aged 10 to 12 Years

Authors: Stephanie A. S. Staras, Carma L. Bylund, Matthew J. Gurka, Lindsay A. Thompson, Marta D. Hansen, Tuo Lin, Pengchen Wang, Gabriela Sanchez, Xiaofei Chi, Christopher A. Harle
Original abstract (English)
Importance Human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination rates among adolescents remain below recommendations. Objective To evaluate the effectiveness of clinician communication training and parent-targeted text messages at increasing HPV vaccination among children aged 11 to 12 years. Design, Setting, and Participants This doubly randomized, 2-level nested clinical trial was conducted from February 2022 to September 2024 among clinicians at 30 pediatric primary care clinics in Florida, children aged 10 to 12 years who had not received any of the 3 adolescent HPV vaccines, and their parents. Interventions occurred for 1 year, and vaccine records were collected for 2 years. Interventions Clinics were randomized 1:1:1 to 3 training groups: (1) the bundled approach, which presents the HPV vaccine in the middle of 2 other recommended vaccines; (2) the focused approach, which emphasizes cancer prevention; and (3) deferred, which offered clinicians the opportunity to participate in the focused training after the trial concluded. Clinicians in the first 2 groups completed a 1-hour communication training and a 5-minute booster session. Patients within clinics were randomized 1:1:1 to 3 text message groups, aligned with the communication strategies: (1) bundled, (2) focused, and (3) no text messages. Parents were sent text messages at the start of the study or prior to their child’s 11th birthday as well as prior to appointments. Main Outcomes and Measures Primary outcomes were HPV vaccine initiation (receipt of first dose) and completion (receipt of second dose). Analyses accounted for clinic-level clustering. Effects were analyzed as randomized and text messages as received. Results At baseline, 59 clinicians (44 [75%] female; 12 [20%] Asian, 21 [36%] Hispanic, and 21 [36%] non-Hispanic White) within 30 randomized clinics were included, with 17 randomized to the focused group, 21 to the bundled group, and 21 to the deferred group. A total of 7234 children were aged 10 (3576 [49%]), 11 (2617 [36%]), and 12 (1041 [14%]) years at study start (1673 [23%] Hispanic, 1001 [14%] non-Hispanic Black, and 1210 [17%] non-Hispanic White). A total of 28 clinics (93%), 50 clinicians (85%), and 6498 children (90%) completed the 2-year follow-up. The mean pre-post clinic-level difference for HPV vaccine initiation was 28.2 (95% CI, 25.3-31.1) percentage points and for completion was 16.1 (95% CI, 13.3-18.9) percentage points. Risk ratios for clinician training and text messaging did not differ between groups for initiation or completion. Among patients whose parents received text messages vs not, the risk ratio for initiation was 1.17 (95% CI, 1.08-1.26) and for completion was 1.29 (95% CI, 1.06-1.56). Conclusions and Relevance In this study of Florida children aged 10 to 12 years, clinic-level HPV vaccination increased over time. No differences in HPV vaccination by random assignment of clinician training or text messages were detected. When received, text messages were associated with higher HPV vaccination. Trial Registration ClinicalTrials.gov Identifier: NCT05006833
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the effectiveness of clinician communication training and parent-targeted text messages at increasing HPV vaccination among children aged 11 to 12 years. Importance was placed on the fact that HPV vaccination rates among adolescents remain below recommendations.
Design and population
This was a doubly randomized, 2-level nested clinical trial conducted from February 2022 to September 2024 among clinicians at 30 pediatric primary care clinics in Florida, children aged 10 to 12 years who had not received any of the 3 adolescent HPV vaccines, and their parents.
Core methods
Clinicians in the first 2 groups completed a 1-hour communication training and a 5-minute booster session. Patients within clinics were randomized 1:1:1 to 3 text message groups, aligned with the communication strategies: (1) bundled, (2) focused, and (3) no text messages. Analyses accounted for clinic-level clustering.
Main results
At baseline, 59 clinicians were included, with 17 randomized to the focused group, 21 to the bundled group, and 21 to the deferred group. The mean pre-post clinic-level difference for HPV vaccine initiation was 28.2 (95% CI, 25.3-31.1) percentage points and for completion was 16.1 (95% CI, 13.3-18.9) percentage points. Risk ratios for clinician training and text messaging did not differ between groups for initiation or completion. Among patients whose parents received text messages vs not, the risk ratio for initiation was 1.17 (95% CI, 1.08-1.26) and for completion was 1.29 (95% CI, 1.06-1.56).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that clinic-level HPV vaccination increased over time, but no differences in HPV vaccination by random assignment of clinician training or text messages were detected. When received, text messages were associated with higher HPV vaccination.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that parent-targeted text messages may be a useful tool to increase HPV vaccination rates among children aged 10 to 12 years, which could inform future public health strategies for improving vaccination coverage.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study had a high confidence level as it was a randomized trial with a large sample size, but the lack of differences in vaccination rates by clinician training or text message groups may limit the strength of the evidence for these interventions.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

MobiLab 快速癌蛋白 E6/E7 抗原检测和 Cobas 4800 高风险 人乳头瘤病毒 脱氧核糖核酸检测在资源匮乏环境中进行 宫颈癌 筛查的一致性和比较性能:横断面研究

Agreement and comparative performance of the MobiLab rapid oncoprotein E6/E7 antigen test and Cobas 4800 high-risk human papillomavirus deoxyribonucleic acid test for cervical cancer screening in a low-resource setting: a cross-sectional study
作者： Talai Kibor Collins, Poli Philippe Amubuomombe, Itsura Peter Muhandale, Banchiri Ruth Okero, Cheruiyot Edymond Kiplangat, Orang’o Elkanah Omenge, Kapten Muthoka, Mercy Kosgei, Odongo Benjamin Elly, Njiru James
摘要中文翻译全球约 97-99% 的 宫颈癌 病例为高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (HR-HPV) 持续感染。检测病毒脱氧核糖核酸 (DNA) 的分子检测被认为是筛查和早期检测的黄金标准,但由于其成本和技术要求,在许多资源匮乏的环境中仍然难以实现。MobiLab 快速癌蛋白 E6/E7 抗原检测为识别与恶性转化相关的活性癌基因表达提供了一种潜在的护理点替代方案。然而,它与基于 DNA 的标准平台的协议尚未完全建立。本研究旨在评估 MobiLab 快速癌蛋白 E6/E7 抗原检测和用于 DNA 检测的聚合酶链反应 (PCR) 的一致性和性能,在资源匮乏的环境中使用 Cobas 4800 作为参考标准。这项横断面研究于 2024 年 2 月至 7 月期间进行,纳入了在 Moi 教学和转诊医院接受 宫颈癌 筛查的女性。总共有 257 名 30 岁及以上的女性被随机选择参加这项研究。医疗保健提供者从所有参与者身上采集宫颈拭子,进行 HR-HPV PCR DNA 检测,然后使用 Delphi 阴道取样器从所有参与者收集宫颈样本,进行 MobiLab 快速癌蛋白 E6/E7 抗原检测。Cobas 4800 检测是一种定性实时 PCR 检测,可扩增 HR-HPV 基因组 的 L1 区域中的靶序列。它可检测 14 种 HR-HPV 类型,包括 16、18 和 12 种其他致癌类型(31、33、35、39、45、51、52、56、58、59、66 和 68)。为了实现研究目标,我们计算了比较分析的一致性指标,包括总体一致性、正一致性和负一致性,以及科恩卡帕 (κ) 系数。还确定了敏感性、特异性、预测值和受试者工作特征曲线下面积 (AUC)。收集并分析临床特征的数据。所有统计检验均为双尾,p < 0.05 被认为具有统计显着性。MobiLab快速癌蛋白E6/E7抗原检测的总体阳性率为58.0%(149/257)。MobiLab 快速癌蛋白 E6/E7 抗原检测和 Cobas 4800 HR-HPV DNA 检测的交叉制表显示出近乎完美的一致性,两种检测对 149 个阳性结果中的 148 个阳性结果和 108 个阴性结果中的 107 个进行了类似的分类。总体一致率为99.22%,正、负一致率分别为99.33%和99.07%。两种检测显示出近乎完美的一致性(Cohen’s κ = 0.992;95% 置信区间 [CI]:0.96–1.00;p < 0.001)。比较性能分析的敏感性为 99.33%(95% CI:96.32–99.98%),特异性为 99.07%(95% CI:94.95–99.98%),阳性预测值为 99.33%(95% CI:95.46–99.90%),阴性预测值为 99.07%(95% CI) CI:93.82–99.87%),AUC 为 0.992(95% CI:0.979–1.000)。MobiLab 快速癌蛋白 E6/E7 抗原检测的结果与 Cobas 4800 HR-HPV DNA 检测的结果一致。需要结合细胞学、阴道镜和组织病理学终点的进一步研究来确定临床有效性并确定 MobiLab 快速癌蛋白 E6/E7 抗原检测在资源有限环境中 HPV 筛查项目中的潜在作用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
全球约 97-99% 的 宫颈癌 病例为高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (HR-HPV) 持续感染。检测病毒脱氧核糖核酸 (DNA) 的分子检测被认为是筛查和早期检测的黄金标准,但由于其成本和技术要求,在许多资源匮乏的环境中仍然难以实现。

科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,MobiLab 快速癌蛋白 E6/E7 抗原检测可以作为 宫颈癌 筛查资源匮乏环境中基于 DNA 检测的可靠替代方案。需要结合细胞学、阴道镜和组织病理学终点的进一步研究来确定临床有效性并确定 MobiLab 快速癌蛋白 E6/E7 抗原检测在资源有限环境中 HPV 筛查项目中的潜在作用。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告该研究的局限性。证据强度很高,因为该研究提供了具有统计意义的详细协议和绩效指标。

Agreement and comparative performance of the MobiLab rapid oncoprotein E6/E7 antigen test and Cobas 4800 high-risk human papillomavirus deoxyribonucleic acid test for cervical cancer screening in a low-resource setting: a cross-sectional study

Authors: Talai Kibor Collins, Poli Philippe Amubuomombe, Itsura Peter Muhandale, Banchiri Ruth Okero, Cheruiyot Edymond Kiplangat, Orang’o Elkanah Omenge, Kapten Muthoka, Mercy Kosgei, Odongo Benjamin Elly, Njiru James
Original abstract (English)
Persistent infection with high-risk human papillomavirus (HR-HPV) accounts for approximately 97–99% of cervical cancer cases worldwide. Molecular assays that detect viral deoxyribonucleic acid (DNA) are considered the gold standard for screening and early detection but remain inaccessible in many low-resource settings because of their cost and technical requirements. The MobiLab rapid oncoprotein E6/E7 antigen test offers a potential point-of-care alternative for identifying active oncogene expression associated with malignant transformation. However, its agreement with standard DNA-based platforms has not been fully established. This study aimed to evaluate the agreement and performance of the MobiLab rapid oncoprotein E6/E7 antigen test and polymerase chain reaction (PCR) for DNA testing, using a Cobas 4800 as the reference standard in a low-resource setting. This cross-sectional study was conducted between February and July 2024 and included women who underwent cervical cancer screening at Moi Teaching and Referral Hospital. In total, 257 women aged 30 years and older were randomly selected for this study. A cervical swab was obtained from all participants by healthcare providers for HR-HPV PCR DNA testing, followed by the collection of a cervical sample from all participants using the Delphi vaginal sampler for the MobiLab rapid oncoprotein E6/E7 antigen test. The Cobas 4800 assay is a qualitative, real-time PCR test that amplifies target sequences in the L1 region of the HR-HPV genome. It detects 14 HR-HPV types, including 16, 18, and 12 other oncogenic types (31, 33, 35, 39, 45, 51, 52, 56, 58, 59, 66, and 68). To achieve the objective of the study, we calculated the agreement metrics for a comparative analysis, including overall, positive, and negative agreement, as well as Cohen’s kappa (κ) coefficient. The sensitivity, specificity, predictive values, and area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC) were also determined. Data on clinical characteristics were collected and analysed. All the statistical tests were two-tailed, and p < 0.05 was considered to indicate statistical significance. The overall positivity rate of the MobiLab rapid oncoprotein E6/E7 antigen test was 58.0% (149/257). Cross-tabulation of the MobiLab rapid oncoprotein E6/E7 antigen test and the Cobas 4800 HR-HPV DNA assay showed near-perfect agreement, with 148 of 149 positive results and 107 of 108 negative results classified similarly by both assays. The overall agreement was 99.22%, with positive and negative agreement rates of 99.33% and 99.07%, respectively. The two assays showed near-perfect agreement (Cohen’s κ = 0.992; 95% confidence interval [CI]: 0.96–1.00; p < 0.001). Comparative performance analysis yielded a sensitivity of 99.33% (95% CI: 96.32–99.98%), a specificity of 99.07% (95% CI: 94.95–99.98%), a positive predictive value of 99.33% (95% CI: 95.46–99.90%), a negative predictive value of 99.07% (95% CI: 93.82–99.87%), and an AUC of 0.992 (95% CI: 0.979–1.000). The results of the MobiLab rapid oncoprotein E6/E7 antigen test agreed with those of the Cobas 4800 HR-HPV DNA assay. Further studies incorporating cytological, colposcopic, and histopathological endpoints are needed to establish the clinical validity and determine the potential role of the MobiLab rapid oncoprotein E6/E7 antigen test in HPV screening programs in resource-limited settings.

Question and background

Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that the MobiLab rapid oncoprotein E6/E7 antigen test could serve as a reliable alternative to DNA-based assays in low-resource settings for cervical cancer screening. Further studies incorporating cytological, colposcopic, and histopathological endpoints are needed to establish the clinical validity and determine the potential role of the MobiLab rapid oncoprotein E6/E7 antigen test in HPV screening programs in resource-limited settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations of the study. The evidence strength is high as the study provides detailed agreement and performance metrics with statistical significance.

人乳头瘤病毒 临床选择的伊拉克妇女的基因型分布和共感染模式:横断面研究

Human Papillomavirus Genotype Distribution and Co-Infection Patterns in Clinically Selected Iraqi Women: A Cross-Sectional Study
作者： Dalya M. Abdul-Kareem, Mushtak T. S. Al-Ouqaili, Rafal M. Murshid, Tong Woei Yenn
摘要中文翻译背景:高危型人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)基因型的持续感染是宫颈癌发生的主要病因。然而,HPV 基因型分布和共感染模式因人群和临床环境而异。本研究旨在确定临床选择的疑似 HPV 相关疾病的伊拉克女性的 HPV 基因型和合并感染模式。方法:在这项横断面研究中,临床选择的 100 名在伊拉克拉马迪和巴格达诊所就诊的女性(2025 年 12 月至 2026 年 7 月)每人贡献一份标本,根据临床评估从宫颈、外阴、肛门或肛周部位通过拭子或浅表病变刮取收集。通过聚合酶链反应和微阵列杂交进行 HPV 基因分型,针对 35 种 HPV 基因型。结果:100 名女性中有 65 名检测到 HPV DNA(65.0%;95% 置信区间,55.3–73.6%)。其中,48 例仅低风险 (LR),9 例仅高风险/潜在高风险 (HR/PHR),8 例混合 LR/HR/PHR 感染。HPV-6 和 HPV-11 是最常检测到的基因型(分别检测到 88 种基因型中的 36 种和 27 种),并且在 20 名女性中发现了多基因型感染。结论:虽然也发现了 HR/PHR 和多基因型感染,但临床选择的 队列研究 中 HPV-6 和 HPV-11 占主导地位。这些初步的描述性数据强调需要对伊拉克的 HPV 基因型分布进行更大规模、更具代表性的研究。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
高危人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)基因型的持续感染是宫颈癌发生的主要病因。然而,HPV 基因型分布和共感染模式因人群和临床环境而异。
设计与对象
在这项横断面研究中,临床选择的 100 名女性在伊拉克拉马迪和巴格达的诊所就诊(2025 年 12 月至 2026 年 7 月),每人贡献一份标本,根据临床评估从宫颈、外阴、肛门或肛周部位通过拭子或浅表病变刮擦采集。
核心方法

解释与创新

科研与公卫意义
研究结果提供了伊拉克女性 HPV 基因型分布和合并感染模式的描述性数据,可为该地区 HPV 相关疾病的针对性 监测 和预防策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究受限于其横断面设计和临床选择的队列研究,可能无法代表一般人群。证据是描述性的和初步的,表明可信度适中。

Human Papillomavirus Genotype Distribution and Co-Infection Patterns in Clinically Selected Iraqi Women: A Cross-Sectional Study

Authors: Dalya M. Abdul-Kareem, Mushtak T. S. Al-Ouqaili, Rafal M. Murshid, Tong Woei Yenn
Original abstract (English)
Background: Persistent infection with high-risk human papillomavirus (HPV) genotypes is the principal etiological factor in cervical carcinogenesis. However, HPV genotype distribution and co-infection patterns differ across populations and clinical settings. The present study aimed to characterize HPV genotypes and co-infection patterns among clinically selected Iraqi women with suspected HPV-associated disease. Methods: In this cross-sectional study, 100 clinically selected women attending clinics in Ramadi and Baghdad, Iraq (December 2025–July 2026), each contributed one specimen, collected by swab or superficial lesion scraping from a cervical, vulval, anal, or perianal site based on clinical assessment. HPV genotyping was performed by polymerase chain reaction followed by microarray hybridization, targeting 35 HPV genotypes. Results: HPV DNA was detected in 65 of 100 women (65.0%; 95% confidence interval, 55.3–73.6%). Of these, 48 had low-risk (LR)-only, 9 high-risk/potentially high-risk (HR/PHR)-only, and 8 mixed LR/HR/PHR infections. HPV-6 and HPV-11 were the most frequently detected genotypes (36 and 27 of 88 genotype detections, respectively), and multiple-genotype infections were identified in 20 women. Conclusions: HPV-6 and HPV-11 predominated in this clinically selected cohort, although HR/PHR and multiple-genotype infections were also identified. These preliminary, descriptive data highlight the need for larger, more representative studies of HPV genotype distribution in Iraq.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Persistent infection with high-risk human papillomavirus (HPV) genotypes is the principal etiological factor in cervical carcinogenesis. However, HPV genotype distribution and co-infection patterns differ across populations and clinical settings.
Design and population
In this cross-sectional study, 100 clinically selected women attending clinics in Ramadi and Baghdad, Iraq (December 2025–July 2026), each contributed one specimen, collected by swab or superficial lesion scraping from a cervical, vulval, anal, or perianal site based on clinical assessment.
Core methods
HPV genotyping was performed by polymerase chain reaction followed by microarray hybridization, targeting 35 HPV genotypes.
Main results
HPV DNA was detected in 65 of 100 women (65.0%; 95% confidence interval, 55.3–73.6%). Of these, 48 had low-risk (LR)-only, 9 high-risk/potentially high-risk (HR/PHR)-only, and 8 mixed LR/HR/PHR infections. HPV-6 and HPV-11 were the most frequently detected genotypes (36 and 27 of 88 genotype detections, respectively), and multiple-genotype infections were identified in 20 women.
Interpretation and novelty
HPV-6 and HPV-11 predominated in this clinically selected cohort, although HR/PHR and multiple-genotype infections were also identified. These preliminary, descriptive data highlight the need for larger, more representative studies of HPV genotype distribution in Iraq.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide descriptive data on HPV genotype distribution and co-infection patterns in Iraqi women, which can inform targeted surveillance and prevention strategies for HPV-associated diseases in the region.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design and clinically selected cohort, which may not be representative of the general population. The evidence is descriptive and preliminary, suggesting a moderate confidence level.

前瞻性 队列研究 中的基线红细胞叶酸和低度宫颈上皮内瘤变的结果

Baseline erythrocyte folate and outcomes of low grade cervical intraepithelial neoplasia in a prospective cohort
作者： Ling Ding, Zongxuan Li, Xiaoyuan Qiao, Nan Hu, Anting Jia, Jiamin Liu, Xiao Zheng, Huimin Li, Kailu Zhao, Mingxuan Zhang
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在探讨基线红细胞叶酸与 1 级宫颈上皮内瘤变 (CIN1) 的逆转、持续性和进展之间的关联。
设计与对象
这项基于社区的前瞻性 队列研究 纳入了 564 名患有 CIN1 的中国女性,并在 6、12 和 24 个月时进行了随访评估。
核心方法
通过化学发光免疫测定法测量基线红细胞叶酸。分析包括 Cox 回归、受限三次样条和受试者工作特征 (ROC) 曲线。
主要结果
Among the 453 women assessed at 24 months, 342 (75.5%) had reversal, 58 (12.8%) had persistence, and 53 (11.7%) had progression to CIN2+. Lower baseline erythrocyte folate was associated with the composite adverse outcome in the reported adjusted model (adjusted hazard ratio [aHR] = 2.42, 95% confidence interval [CI]: 1.62–3.61).
解释与创新
作者解释说,在对报告模型中包含的协变量进行调整后,较低的基线红细胞叶酸与不良 CIN1 结局相关。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,基线红细胞叶酸水平可能对 CIN1 结局具有预后价值,可能为未来针对 HPV 相关疾病的营养干预研究提供信息。
局限与证据强度
事件时间假设和残余混杂限制解释。由于该研究的观察性质和潜在的混杂因素,证据是中等的。

Baseline erythrocyte folate and outcomes of low grade cervical intraepithelial neoplasia in a prospective cohort

Authors: Ling Ding, Zongxuan Li, Xiaoyuan Qiao, Nan Hu, Anting Jia, Jiamin Liu, Xiao Zheng, Huimin Li, Kailu Zhao, Mingxuan Zhang
Original abstract (English)
Objective To examine the association between baseline erythrocyte folate and reversal, persistence, and progression of cervical intraepithelial neoplasia grade 1 (CIN1). Methods This community-based prospective cohort included 564 Chinese women with CIN1, with follow-up assessments at 6, 12, and 24 months. Baseline erythrocyte folate was measured by chemiluminescence immunoassay. Histological outcomes were classified as reversal, persistence, or progression to CIN2 or worse (CIN2+); persistence and progression formed a composite adverse outcome. Analyses included Cox regression, restricted cubic splines, and receiver operating characteristic (ROC) curves. Exploratory experiments assessed proliferation and apoptosis in C33A and CaSki cervical cancer cells. Results Among the 453 women assessed at 24 months, 342 (75.5%) had reversal, 58 (12.8%) had persistence, and 53 (11.7%) had progression to CIN2+. Lower baseline erythrocyte folate was associated with the composite adverse outcome in the reported adjusted model (adjusted hazard ratio [aHR] = 2.42, 95% confidence interval [CI]: 1.62–3.61). No statistically significant nonlinearity was detected for this outcome at 6 or 12 months ( p = 0.655 and p = 0.127), whereas an L-shaped association was observed at 24 months (P for nonlinearity = 0.005). At 24 months, the area under the ROC curve (AUC) for folate was 0.703 (95% CI: 0.647–0.760) overall and 0.742 (95% CI: 0.646–0.837) within the high-risk human papillomavirus (HR-HPV)-positive subgroup. Higher nominal folate concentrations reduced proliferation and increased apoptosis in the two cell lines. Conclusion Lower baseline erythrocyte folate was associated with adverse CIN1 outcomes after adjustment for the covariates included in the reported model. Event-time assumptions and residual confounding limit interpretation. These findings do not establish causality, an interaction with HR-HPV, or a therapeutic benefit of folate supplementation. Prognostic use requires further validation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to examine the association between baseline erythrocyte folate and reversal, persistence, and progression of cervical intraepithelial neoplasia grade 1 (CIN1).
Design and population
This community-based prospective cohort included 564 Chinese women with CIN1, with follow-up assessments at 6, 12, and 24 months.
Core methods
Baseline erythrocyte folate was measured by chemiluminescence immunoassay. Analyses included Cox regression, restricted cubic splines, and receiver operating characteristic (ROC) curves.
Main results
Among the 453 women assessed at 24 months, 342 (75.5%) had reversal, 58 (12.8%) had persistence, and 53 (11.7%) had progression to CIN2+. Lower baseline erythrocyte folate was associated with the composite adverse outcome in the reported adjusted model (adjusted hazard ratio [aHR] = 2.42, 95% confidence interval [CI]: 1.62–3.61).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that lower baseline erythrocyte folate was associated with adverse CIN1 outcomes after adjustment for the covariates included in the reported model.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that baseline erythrocyte folate levels may have prognostic value for CIN1 outcomes, potentially informing future studies on nutritional interventions for HPV-related diseases.
Limitations and evidence strength
Event-time assumptions and residual confounding limit interpretation. The evidence is moderate due to the observational nature of the study and potential for confounding factors.

根据亚洲 队列研究 状态对口咽鳞状细胞癌进行基因组分析:TCOG T1322 注册研究

Genomic profiling of oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinoma by human papillomavirus status in an Asian cohort: the TCOG T1322 registry study
作者： Shang‐Hung Chen, Bor-Hwang Kang, Shang-Yin Wu, Hui-Ching Wang, Chin-Fu Hsiao, Pen-Yuan Chu, Chun-Hung Hua, Chen-Chi Wang, Jenn-Ren Hsiao, Li-Tzong Chen
摘要中文翻译本研究根据亚洲 队列研究 的 HPV 状态,描述了 OPSCC 的基因组改变、突变特征和治疗可操作性。方法 这项基于组织的多中心下一代测序注册研究(TCOG T1322;NCT05522881,注册于 2022 年 8 月 31 日)纳入了台湾经病理证实的头颈鳞状细胞癌患者。结果 290 例 OPSCC 患者中,190 例 HPV 阳性,100 例 HPV 阴性。致谢 作者衷心感谢所有研究参与者及其家人对本研究做出的宝贵贡献。资助 本研究得到台湾国家卫生研究院 CA-GP-11、CA-GP-13 和 CA-SP-01 的资助。伦理声明 伦理批准和参与同意 这项注册研究由国家卫生研究院 (NHRI) 的台湾肿瘤合作组 (TCOG) 与台湾各地的七家医院合作进行(TCOG T1322;ClinicalTrials.gov 标识符:NCT05522881,预期注册日期为 2022 年 8 月 31 日)。该研究是根据赫尔辛基宣言、国际协调委员会良好临床实践 (ICH-GCP) 指南的原则以及所有适用的监管要求进行的。摘要 目的 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 阳性和 HPV 阴性口咽鳞状细胞癌 (OPSCC) 是生物学上不同的实体,具有不同的临床行为和治疗反应。然而,OPSCC 的基因组研究主要集中在西方人群,亚洲患者的分子格局仍未完全确定。使用 440 个基因靶向组对肿瘤样本进行全面的基因组分析。肿瘤基因组改变...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在调查亚洲人 队列研究 口咽鳞状细胞癌 (OPSCC) 的基因组谱,因为 HPV 阳性和 HPV 阴性 OPSCC 是生物学上不同的实体,具有不同的临床行为和治疗反应。这项研究是必要的,因为 OPSCC 的基因组研究主要集中在西方人群,而亚洲患者的分子格局尚未完全确定。
设计与对象
这是一项在台湾进行的多中心基于组织的下一代测序注册研究(TCOG T1322;NCT05522881),招募了经病理证实的头颈鳞状细胞癌患者。该研究纳入了 290 名 OPSCC 患者,其中 190 名患有 HPV 阳性肿瘤,100 名患有 HPV 阴性肿瘤。
核心方法
使用 440 个基因靶向组对肿瘤样本进行全面的基因组分析。根据 p16 定义的 HPV 状态分析基因组改变、通路失调、突变特征、肿瘤突变负荷 (TMB) 和治疗可操作性。
主要结果
在 290 名 OPSCC 患者中,190 名患有 HPV 阳性肿瘤,100 名患有 HPV 阴性肿瘤。HPV 阳性肿瘤经常存在 PIK3CA (37%)、BCL6 (14%) 和免疫检查点配体扩增 CD274/PDCD1LG2 (6%) 的改变。HPV 阴性肿瘤富含 TP53 突变 (89%)、CDKN2A 缺失 (34%)、FAT1 改变 (33%) 和 CCND1 扩增 (30%)。
解释与创新
这些发现强调了亚洲 队列研究 中 HPV 阳性和 HPV 阴性 OPSCC 的不同基因组图谱,为了解其生物异质性提供了见解,并确定了潜在的转化治疗靶点以供进一步研究。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果有助于了解亚洲人群中 HPV 阳性和 HPV 阴性 OPSCC 之间的分子差异,这可能为该特定人群提供更有针对性的诊断和治疗策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其对亚洲 队列研究 的关注而受到限制,这可能会影响研究结果对其他人群的普遍适用性。证据是中等的,因为它基于特定 队列研究 的基因组分析,未经更广泛的验证。

Genomic profiling of oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinoma by human papillomavirus status in an Asian cohort: the TCOG T1322 registry study

Authors: Shang‐Hung Chen, Bor-Hwang Kang, Shang-Yin Wu, Hui-Ching Wang, Chin-Fu Hsiao, Pen-Yuan Chu, Chun-Hung Hua, Chen-Chi Wang, Jenn-Ren Hsiao, Li-Tzong Chen
Original abstract (English)
This study characterized genomic alterations, mutational signatures, and therapeutic actionability of OPSCC according to HPV status in an Asian cohort. Methods This multicenter tissue-based next-generation sequencing registry study (TCOG T1322; NCT05522881, registered on August 31, 2022) enrolled patients with pathologically confirmed head and neck squamous cell carcinoma in Taiwan. Results Among 290 patients with OPSCC, 190 had HPV-positive and 100 had HPV-negative tumors. Acknowledgements The authors sincerely thank all study participants and their families for their invaluable contributions to this research. Funding This study was supported by grants CA-GP-11, CA-GP-13, and CA-SP-01 from the National Health Research Institutes, Taiwan. Ethics declarations Ethics approval and consent to participate This registry study was conducted by the Taiwan Cooperative Oncology Group (TCOG) of the National Health Research Institutes (NHRI) in collaboration with seven hospitals across Taiwan (TCOG T1322; ClinicalTrials.gov identifier: NCT05522881, prospectively registered on August 31, 2022). The study was performed in accordance with the principles of the Declaration of Helsinki, International Council for Harmonisation Good Clinical Practice (ICH-GCP) guidelines, and all applicable regulatory requirements. Abstract Purpose Human papillomavirus (HPV)-positive and HPV-negative oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinoma (OPSCC) are biologically distinct entities with different clinical behaviors and therapeutic responses. However, genomic studies of OPSCC have largely focused on Western populations, and the molecular landscape in Asian patients remains incompletely defined. Tumor samples underwent comprehensive genomic profiling using a 440-gene targeted panel. Tumor genomic alterations...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate the genomic profiles of oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinoma (OPSCC) in an Asian cohort, as HPV-positive and HPV-negative OPSCC are biologically distinct entities with different clinical behaviors and therapeutic responses. The research was needed because genomic studies of OPSCC have largely focused on Western populations, leaving the molecular landscape in Asian patients incompletely defined.
Design and population
This was a multicenter tissue-based next-generation sequencing registry study (TCOG T1322; NCT05522881) conducted in Taiwan, enrolling patients with pathologically confirmed head and neck squamous cell carcinoma. The study included 290 patients with OPSCC, of whom 190 had HPV-positive tumors and 100 had HPV-negative tumors.
Core methods
Tumor samples were subjected to comprehensive genomic profiling using a 440-gene targeted panel. Genomic alterations, pathway dysregulation, mutational signatures, tumor mutational burden (TMB), and therapeutic actionability were analyzed based on p16-defined HPV status.
Main results
Among the 290 patients with OPSCC, 190 had HPV-positive tumors and 100 had HPV-negative tumors. HPV-positive tumors frequently harbored alterations in PIK3CA (37%), BCL6 (14%), and immune checkpoint ligand amplifications CD274/PDCD1LG2 (6%). HPV-negative tumors were enriched for TP53 mutations (89%), CDKN2A deletions (34%), FAT1 alterations (33%), and CCND1 amplifications (30%).
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight the distinct genomic profiles of HPV-positive and HPV-negative OPSCC in an Asian cohort, providing insights into their biological heterogeneity and identifying potential translational therapeutic targets for further investigation.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results contribute to the understanding of the molecular differences between HPV-positive and HPV-negative OPSCC in Asian populations, which may inform more targeted diagnostic and therapeutic strategies for this specific demographic.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its focus on an Asian cohort, which may affect the generalizability of findings to other populations. The evidence is moderate, as it is based on genomic profiling of a specific cohort without broader validation.

人乳头瘤病毒 相关口咽癌高 患病率 患者的生存率:来自南美国家转诊公立医院的真实世界数据

Survival in patients with a high prevalence of human papillomavirus-associated oropharyngeal cancer: real-world data from a national referral public hospital in South America
作者： Felipe Carvajal, Eduardo Villa, Felipe Cardemil, Maribel Bruna, Felipe Bustos, Sebastián Castro, Mario Tapia, Piero Bettoli, Fernanda Soto, Irma Leal
摘要中文翻译本研究评估了在南美癌症转诊中心接受放射治疗的高 患病率 HPV 相关 OPSCC 患者的肿瘤学结果。材料和方法:智利国家癌症研究所进行了一项回顾性 队列研究 研究,包括 2012 年至 2023 年间接受治疗的非转移性疾病患者。使用 Kaplan-Meier 方法进行生存分析。结果:总共分析了 146 名患者;75.3%为男性,平均年龄60.7岁。患病率 或 人乳头瘤病毒 在口咽和非口咽头颈癌中的应用——按时间和地区趋势的系统回顾和荟萃分析。5 年总生存率为 60.9% [95% 置信区间 (CI):52.7–70.3]。p16+ [风险比 (HR) = 0.36;95% CI:0.19–0.68;p < 0.01]与生存率改善独立相关,而吸烟史≥10包年(HR = 2.18;p = 0.02)和东部肿瘤合作组(ECOG)2-4(HR = 3.30;p < 0.01)与较差的结果相关。发病率 与 人乳头瘤病毒 相关的口咽癌患者的生存率较高:来自南美一家国家转诊公立医院的真实世界数据 摘要背景:口咽鳞状细胞癌 (OPSCC) 的 发病率 在全球范围内呈上升趋势。来自南美洲的数据有限。已经描述了 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 相关 OPSCC 向更高 患病率 的流行病学转变。使用描述性统计来总结数据。采用逻辑回归和 Cox 比例风险回归进行单变量和多变量分析 (MVA)。62.3% 的人检测出 HPV 阳性 (p16+)。所有患者均接受放疗,其中82.8%接受同步检查...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估 人乳头瘤病毒 相关口咽癌患者的生存结果,因为口咽鳞状细胞癌 (OPSCC) 的 发病率 在全球范围内不断上升。
设计与对象
智利国家癌症研究所进行了一项回顾性 队列研究 研究,包括 2012 年至 2023 年间接受治疗的非转移性疾病患者。总共分析了 146 名患者;75.3%为男性,平均年龄60.7岁。
核心方法
采用逻辑回归和 Cox 比例风险回归进行单变量和多变量分析 (MVA)。62.3% 的患者检测到 HPV 阳性(p16+)。
主要结果
5 年总生存率为 60.9% [95% 置信区间 (CI):52.7–70.3]。p16+ [风险比 (HR) = 0.36;95% CI:0.19–0.68;p < 0.01]与生存率改善独立相关,而吸烟史≥10包年(HR = 2.18;p = 0.02)和东部肿瘤合作组(ECOG)2-4(HR = 3.30;p < 0.01)与较差的结果相关。
解释与创新
作者解释说,该南美人群的生存结果(HPV 相关 OPSCC 的 患病率 较高)与国际公布的数据相当。在探索性分析中,HPV 关联和同步化疗与更好的生存结果相关,顺铂方案之间没有显着差异。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为 HPV 相关口咽癌的临床实践和 治疗 策略提供参考,特别是关于同步化疗的作用以及患者特征对生存结果的影响。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和潜在的选择偏倚而受到限制。证据是中等的,因为它是基于观察数据而不是随机试验。

Survival in patients with a high prevalence of human papillomavirus-associated oropharyngeal cancer: real-world data from a national referral public hospital in South America

Authors: Felipe Carvajal, Eduardo Villa, Felipe Cardemil, Maribel Bruna, Felipe Bustos, Sebastián Castro, Mario Tapia, Piero Bettoli, Fernanda Soto, Irma Leal
Original abstract (English)
This study evaluates oncological outcomes in patients with a high prevalence of HPV-associated OPSCC treated with radiotherapy at a cancer referral centre in South America. Materials and methods: A retrospective cohort study was conducted at the National Cancer Institute of Chile, including patients with non-metastatic disease treated with curative intent between 2012 and 2023. Survival analysis was performed using the Kaplan–Meier method. Results: A total of 146 patients were analysed; 75.3% were male, with a mean age of 60.7 years. Prevalence of human papillomavirus in oropharyngeal and nonoropharyngeal head and neck cancer--systematic review and meta-analysis of trends by time and region. The 5-year overall survival was 60.9% [95% confidence interval (CI): 52.7–70.3]. p16+ [hazard ratio (HR) = 0.36; 95% CI: 0.19–0.68; p < 0.01] was independently associated with improved survival, whereas a smoking history of ≥ 10 pack-years (HR = 2.18; p = 0.02) and Eastern Cooperative Oncology Group (ECOG) 2–4 (HR = 3.30; p < 0.01) were linked to worse outcomes. Survival in patients with a high prevalence of human papillomavirus-associated oropharyngeal cancer: real-world data from a national referral public hospital in South America Abstract Background: The incidence of oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinoma (OPSCC) is rising globally. Data from South America are limited. An epidemiological shift towards a higher prevalence of human papillomavirus (HPV)-associated OPSCC has been described. Descriptive statistics were used to summarise the data. Univariate and multivariate analyses (MVA) were conducted with logistic regression and Cox proportional hazards regression. HPV positivity (p16+) was detected in 62.3%. All patients underwent radiotherapy, with 82.8% receiving concurrent che...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate survival outcomes in patients with human papillomavirus-associated oropharyngeal cancer, as the incidence of oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinoma (OPSCC) is rising globally.
Design and population
A retrospective cohort study was conducted at the National Cancer Institute of Chile, including patients with non-metastatic disease treated with curative intent between 2012 and 2023. A total of 146 patients were analysed; 75.3% were male, with a mean age of 60.7 years.
Core methods
Univariate and multivariate analyses (MVA) were conducted with logistic regression and Cox proportional hazards regression. HPV positivity (p16+) was detected in 62.3% of patients.
Main results
The 5-year overall survival was 60.9% [95% confidence interval (CI): 52.7–70.3]. p16+ [hazard ratio (HR) = 0.36; 95% CI: 0.19–0.68; p < 0.01] was independently associated with improved survival, whereas a smoking history of ≥ 10 pack-years (HR = 2.18; p = 0.02) and Eastern Cooperative Oncology Group (ECOG) 2–4 (HR = 3.30; p < 0.01) were linked to worse outcomes.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that survival outcomes in this South American population, with a high prevalence of HPV-associated OPSCC, were comparable to internationally published data. In exploratory analyses, HPV association and concurrent chemotherapy were associated with better survival outcomes, with no significant differences between cisplatin schedules.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform clinical practice and treatment strategies for HPV-associated oropharyngeal cancer, particularly regarding the role of concurrent chemotherapy and the impact of patient characteristics on survival outcomes.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and potential for selection bias. The evidence is moderate as it is based on observational data rather than randomized trials.

不同年龄、孕产妇、疾病状态女性阴道微生态特征及其与HPV感染的相关性

Vaginal microecological characteristics in women across different age, maternal and disease states and their correlation with HPV infection

查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查女性人群阴道微生态的变化,阐明不同年龄组和生理状态下阴道微生态的变化,为阴道疾病的临床预防、诊断和治疗提供证据,保障女性生殖健康。
设计与对象
纳入2024年3月至2024年9月期间在锦州医科大学第三附属医院进行常规健康检查的200名女性。将受试者分为5个年龄组(21-29岁、30-39岁、40-49岁、50-59岁、≥60岁)、孕/产后组和非孕/非产后组,并在同一年龄组内进一步分为阴道疾病组和健康组。
核心方法
所有参与者均接受了阴道pH测量、细菌群落分析、阴道微生态功能测定和HPV检测等测试。比较不同年龄组、孕产妇状况和疾病状态之间的pH、菌群结构和功能测试结果等指标。
主要结果
分析阴道微生态与HPV感染的相关性。
解释与创新
作者解释说,这些发现为支持阴道疾病的临床预防、诊断和治疗、保障女性生殖健康提供了证据。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为制定预防 HPV 相关阴道疾病、诊断 和 治疗 的针对性临床策略提供参考。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其观察性设计和缺乏详细的统计分析而受到限制,但由于明确关注 HPV 感染及其与阴道微生态的相关性,证据强度适中。

Vaginal microecological characteristics in women across different age, maternal and disease states and their correlation with HPV infection

Authors: Lian Feng, Ying Liu
Original abstract (English)
Article Open Access - Authors: - Article Number: 6|Published online on: November 12, 2026https://doi.org/10.3892/etm.2026.13354 - Expand metrics + The aim of the present study was to investigate variations in vaginal microecology across female populations, elucidate alterations in vaginal microecology across age groups and physiological states, and provide evidence to support clinical prevention, diagnosis and treatment of vaginal diseases to safeguard women's reproductive health. Indicators of pH, flora structure, and functional test results were compared among different age groups, maternal statuses, and disease states. View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate variations in vaginal microecology across female populations, elucidate alterations in vaginal microecology across age groups and physiological states, and provide evidence to support clinical prevention, diagnosis and treatment of vaginal diseases to safeguard women's reproductive health.
Design and population
A total of 200 women who underwent routine health check-ups at the Third Affiliated Hospital of Jinzhou Medical University between March and September 2024 were enrolled. Subjects were divided into five age groups (21-29, 30-39, 40-49, 50-59, ≥60 years), a pregnant/postpartum group and a non-pregnant/non-postpartum group, and further categorized into a vaginal disease group and a healthy group within the same age group.
Core methods
All participants underwent tests including vaginal pH measurement, bacterial community analysis, vaginal microecological functional assays, and HPV detection. Indicators of pH, flora structure, and functional test results were compared among different age groups, maternal statuses, and disease states.
Main results
The correlation between vaginal microecology and HPV infection was analyzed.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as providing evidence to support clinical prevention, diagnosis and treatment of vaginal diseases to safeguard women's reproductive health.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of targeted clinical strategies for the prevention, diagnosis and treatment of HPV-associated vaginal diseases.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its observational design and the lack of detailed statistical analysis, but the evidence is moderate in strength due to the clear focus on HPV infection and its correlation with vaginal microecology.

塞拉利昂艾滋病毒感染者中 患病率 和 人乳头瘤病毒 感染的危险因素:一项横断面研究

Prevalence and risk factors for human papillomavirus infection among women living with HIV in Sierra Leone: A cross-sectional study
作者： Jiba, D. F., Yang, G., Zhang, T., Zhao, L., Dykens, A. J., Deen, G. F., Namanga, E. S., Kamara, M. N., Barrie, U., Kallon, A. B.
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
宫颈癌 是一种影响子宫颈的恶性肿瘤,是全球女性中第四大常见癌症和癌症相关 死亡率 的原因。这一负担对资源匮乏地区影响尤为严重,大约 90% 的病例都发生在这些地区。
设计与对象
本研究旨在评估在塞拉利昂康诺特医院就诊的艾滋病毒感染者 (WLHIV) 妇女的 患病率 和 高危型HPV 感染的危险因素。
核心方法
我们对 2024 年 9 月至 12 月期间到弗里敦康诺特教学医院艾滋病毒诊所就诊的 25-65 岁艾滋病毒感染者女性进行了一项横断面研究。收集了宫颈内拭子用于 HPV DNA 检测。使用 R (v4.4.0) 分析数据。Logistic回归分析用于确定HPV阳性的独立危险因素。
主要结果
A total of 191 WLHIV were enrolled. High-risk HPV DNA was detected in 60 (31.4%) (95% CI 25.3%-38.3%) participants, with 50% (30/60) having multiple serotypes. The most common genotype was HPV 52, followed by 16 and 51. Only 30% of HPV-positive women had genotypes 16 or 18, targeted by the bivalent vaccine. On multivariable analysis, former contraceptive use was independently associated with increased HPV positivity (aOR=4.86, 95% CI: 1.65-17.37, p = 0.0032).
解释与创新
塞拉利昂 高危型HPV 感染的高 患病率 和 WLHIV 中非 16/18 基因型的优势强调需要将 HPV 筛查纳入 HIV 服务,并考虑在塞拉利昂扩大 HPV 疫苗 覆盖范围。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了将 HPV 筛查纳入 HIV 护理的重要性,并表明塞拉利昂需要扩大 HPV 疫苗 覆盖范围,以解决 WLHIV 中普遍存在的特定基因型。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其横截面设计和潜在的混杂因素而受到限制。证据是中等的,因为它是基于在特定环境下进行的一项研究。

Prevalence and risk factors for human papillomavirus infection among women living with HIV in Sierra Leone: A cross-sectional study

Authors: Jiba, D. F., Yang, G., Zhang, T., Zhao, L., Dykens, A. J., Deen, G. F., Namanga, E. S., Kamara, M. N., Barrie, U., Kallon, A. B.
Original abstract (English)
Abstract BackgroundCervical cancer, a malignancy affecting the cervix, is the fourth most common cancer and cause of cancer-related mortality among women worldwide. This burden disproportionately affects low-resource regions, where approximately 90% of cases occur. HIV-positive women are at high risk of persistent Human Papillomavirus (HPV) infection and cervical cancer, but screening for this cancer is not yet integrated into HIV care in Sierra Leone. This study aimed to assess the prevalence and risk factors of high-risk HPV infection among women living with HIV (WLHIV) attending Connaught Hospital in Sierra Leone. Methods We conducted a cross-sectional study of women living with HIV aged 25-65 years attending the HIV clinic at Connaught Teaching Hospital in Freetown between September and December 2024. Endocervical swabs were collected for HPV DNA testing. Data was analyzed using R (v4.4.0). Logistic regression analysis was used to determine the independent risk factors for HPV positivity. Results A total of 191 WLHIV were enrolled. The mean age was 36.8 years; 44.5% were single, and 67% were employed. High-risk HPV DNA was detected in 60 (31.4%) (95% CI 25.3%-38.3%) participants, with 50% (30/60) having multiple serotypes. The most common genotype was HPV 52, followed by 16 and 51. Only 30% of HPV-positive women had genotypes 16 or 18, targeted by the bivalent vaccine. On multivariable analysis, former contraceptive use was independently associated with increased HPV positivity (aOR=4.86, 95% CI: 1.65-17.37, p = 0.0032). Conclusion The high prevalence of high-risk HPV infection and predominance of non 16/18 genotypes among WLHIV in Sierra Leone underscore the need to integrate HPV screening within HIV services and consider broader HPV vaccine coverage in Sierra Leone. Former contraceptive use is an independent predictor of HPV positivity, emphasizing the need for robust screening strategies in women using contraceptives.

Question and background
Cervical cancer, a malignancy affecting the cervix, is the fourth most common cancer and cause of cancer-related mortality among women worldwide. This burden disproportionately affects low-resource regions, where approximately 90% of cases occur.
Design and population
This study aimed to assess the prevalence and risk factors of high-risk HPV infection among women living with HIV (WLHIV) attending Connaught Hospital in Sierra Leone.
Core methods
We conducted a cross-sectional study of women living with HIV aged 25-65 years attending the HIV clinic at Connaught Teaching Hospital in Freetown between September and December 2024. Endocervical swabs were collected for HPV DNA testing. Data was analyzed using R (v4.4.0). Logistic regression analysis was used to determine the independent risk factors for HPV positivity.
Main results
A total of 191 WLHIV were enrolled. High-risk HPV DNA was detected in 60 (31.4%) (95% CI 25.3%-38.3%) participants, with 50% (30/60) having multiple serotypes. The most common genotype was HPV 52, followed by 16 and 51. Only 30% of HPV-positive women had genotypes 16 or 18, targeted by the bivalent vaccine. On multivariable analysis, former contraceptive use was independently associated with increased HPV positivity (aOR=4.86, 95% CI: 1.65-17.37, p = 0.0032).
Interpretation and novelty
The high prevalence of high-risk HPV infection and predominance of non 16/18 genotypes among WLHIV in Sierra Leone underscore the need to integrate HPV screening within HIV services and consider broader HPV vaccine coverage in Sierra Leone.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the importance of integrating HPV screening into HIV care and suggest the need for broader HPV vaccine coverage in Sierra Leone to address the specific genotypes prevalent among WLHIV.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design and the potential for confounding factors. The evidence is moderate as it is based on a single study conducted in a specific setting.

按 HPV 基因型进行 CIN2 病变风险分层的纵向甲基化分析

Longitudinal methylation analysis for risk stratification of CIN2 lesions by HPV genotype
作者： Hanna Pikkujämsä, Karoliina M. Aro, Laura Bergqvist, Ralf C. Bützow, Maija Jakobsson, Joakim Dillner, Pekka Nieminen, Ilkka E. J. Kalliala, Belinda Nedjai, Karolina Louvanto
摘要中文翻译摘要 简介 迫切需要能够区分退行性宫颈上皮内瘤变 (CIN) 病变和进行性宫颈上皮内瘤变 (CIN) 病变的生物标志物,以改善 宫颈癌 筛查计划中的风险分层并避免过度治疗。我们的目的是评估 S5 甲基化分类器在预测不同 HPV 基因型组中未经治疗的 2 级 CIN (CIN2) 临床轨迹方面的纵向性能。材料和方法 本研究纳入了 122 名经组织学证实患有 CIN2 的女性(18-30 岁),她们参加了芬兰赫尔辛基大学医院正在进行的 监测 试验 (ISRCTN91953024)。每 6 个月对患者进行一次细胞学检查、HPV 基因分型和阴道镜检查,持续长达 24 个月。比较了组织学恢复正常的女性 (n = 89) 和进展为 CIN3+ 的女性 (n = 22,包括一种微浸润癌) 或持续性 CIN2 (n = 11) 的女性的 S5 甲基化水平(焦磷酸测序)。根据基线 HPV 基因型对分析进行分层。结果 基线 S5 分类器显着预测进展/持续性,比值比 (OR) 为 1.15 (95%CI 1.05–1.25)。当排除 HPV16/18 时,S5 分类器表现出类似的边缘预测关联(OR 1.19;95% CI 1.00–1.41),而细胞学和 HPV 基因分型却没有。纵向分析显示,进展者中的 S5 甲基化水平在整个 24 个月期间保持升高,显着预测了在组织学 诊断 进展之前长达 18 个月的进展/持续性。结论 在这个活跃的 监测 队列研究 中,S5 分类器显示出作为预后分类生物标志物的潜力,用于识别未经治疗的 CIN2 的进展,包括非 HPV16/18 类型的病例。我们的探索性发现表明,甲基化水平升高可能会先于组织学进展长达 18 个月。随着 HPV16/18 患病率 的下降,与标准分类相比,宿主 病毒甲基化组合可能会提供改进的风险分层,尽管需要在疫苗接种人群中进行更大规模的前瞻性研究来证实这些发现并建立临床实用性。

问题与背景
迫切需要能够区分退行性宫颈上皮内瘤变 (CIN) 病变和进行性宫颈上皮内瘤变 (CIN) 病变的生物标志物,以改善 宫颈癌 筛查计划中的风险分层并避免过度治疗。
设计与对象
这项研究纳入了 122 名经组织学证实患有 CIN2 的女性(18-30 岁),他们参加了芬兰赫尔辛基大学医院正在进行的 监测 试验 (ISRCTN91953024)。每 6 个月对患者进行一次细胞学检查、HPV 基因分型和阴道镜检查,持续长达 24 个月。
核心方法
比较了组织学恢复正常的女性 (n = 89) 和进展为 CIN3+ 的女性 (n = 22,包括一种微浸润癌) 或持续性 CIN2 (n = 11) 的女性之间的 S5 甲基化水平(焦磷酸测序)。根据基线 HPV 基因型对分析进行分层。
主要结果
基线 S5 分类器显着预测进展/持续性,比值比 (OR) 为 1.15 (95%CI 1.05–1.25)。当排除 HPV16/18 时,S5 分类器表现出类似的边缘预测关联(OR 1.19;95% CI 1.00–1.41),而细胞学和 HPV 基因分型却没有。
解释与创新
S5 分类器显示出作为预后分类生物标志物的潜力,可用于识别未经治疗的 CIN2 的进展,包括非 HPV16/18 类型的病例。
科研与公卫意义
我们的探索性发现表明,甲基化水平升高可能会先于组织学进展长达 18 个月。
局限与证据强度
随着 HPV16/18 患病率 的下降,与标准分类相比,宿主 病毒甲基化组合可能会提供改进的风险分层,尽管需要在疫苗接种人群中进行更大规模的前瞻性研究来证实这些发现并建立临床实用性。

Longitudinal methylation analysis for risk stratification of CIN2 lesions by HPV genotype

Authors: Hanna Pikkujämsä, Karoliina M. Aro, Laura Bergqvist, Ralf C. Bützow, Maija Jakobsson, Joakim Dillner, Pekka Nieminen, Ilkka E. J. Kalliala, Belinda Nedjai, Karolina Louvanto
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Introduction There is an urgent need for biomarkers that can distinguish regressive from progressive cervical intraepithelial neoplasia (CIN) lesions to improve risk stratification in cervical cancer screening programs and avoid overtreatment. We aimed to evaluate the longitudinal performance of the S5 methylation classifier in predicting the clinical trajectory of untreated CIN grade 2 (CIN2) across different HPV genotype groups. Material and Methods This study included 122 women (aged 18–30) with histologically confirmed CIN2 enrolled in an active surveillance trial at Helsinki University Hospital, Finland (ISRCTN91953024). Patients were monitored every 6 months for up to 24 months with cytology, HPV genotyping, and colposcopy. S5 methylation levels (pyrosequencing) were compared between women who regressed to normal histology ( n = 89) and those who progressed to CIN3+ ( n = 22, including one microinvasive cancer) or had persistent CIN2 ( n = 11). Analysis was stratified based on baseline HPV genotypes. Results The baseline S5 classifier significantly predicted progression/persistence with an odds ratio (OR) of 1.15 (95%CI 1.05–1.25). When excluding HPV16/18, the S5 classifier demonstrated a similar, borderline predictive association (OR 1.19; 95%CI 1.00–1.41), whereas cytology and HPV genotyping did not. Longitudinal analysis showed that S5 methylation levels in progressors remained elevated throughout the 24‐month period, significantly predicting progression/persistence up to 18 months prior to histological diagnosis of progression. Conclusions In this active surveillance cohort, the S5 classifier showed potential as a prognostic triage biomarker for identifying progression in untreated CIN2, including cases with non‐HPV16/18 types. Our exploratory findings suggest that elevated methylation levels may precede histological progression by up to 18 months. As HPV16/18 prevalence declines, host‐viral methylation panels may offer improved risk stratification compared to standard triage, though larger prospective studies in vaccinated populations are needed to confirm these findings and establish clinical utility.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
There is an urgent need for biomarkers that can distinguish regressive from progressive cervical intraepithelial neoplasia (CIN) lesions to improve risk stratification in cervical cancer screening programs and avoid overtreatment.
Design and population
This study included 122 women (aged 18–30) with histologically confirmed CIN2 enrolled in an active surveillance trial at Helsinki University Hospital, Finland (ISRCTN91953024). Patients were monitored every 6 months for up to 24 months with cytology, HPV genotyping, and colposcopy.
Core methods
S5 methylation levels (pyrosequencing) were compared between women who regressed to normal histology (n = 89) and those who progressed to CIN3+ (n = 22, including one microinvasive cancer) or had persistent CIN2 (n = 11). Analysis was stratified based on baseline HPV genotypes.
Main results
The baseline S5 classifier significantly predicted progression/persistence with an odds ratio (OR) of 1.15 (95%CI 1.05–1.25). When excluding HPV16/18, the S5 classifier demonstrated a similar, borderline predictive association (OR 1.19; 95%CI 1.00–1.41), whereas cytology and HPV genotyping did not.
Interpretation and novelty
The S5 classifier showed potential as a prognostic triage biomarker for identifying progression in untreated CIN2, including cases with non-HPV16/18 types.
Scientific and public-health significance
Our exploratory findings suggest that elevated methylation levels may precede histological progression by up to 18 months.
Limitations and evidence strength
As HPV16/18 prevalence declines, host-viral methylation panels may offer improved risk stratification compared to standard triage, though larger prospective studies in vaccinated populations are needed to confirm these findings and establish clinical utility.

南非夸祖鲁纳塔尔省青春期前女孩从二价 HPV 疫苗接种 转换为非二价 HPV 疫苗接种 的健康经济影响和成本阈值:考虑艾滋病毒流行的数学模型分析

Health Economic Impact and Cost Threshold for Switching From Bivalent to Nonavalent HPV Vaccination for Preadolescent Girls in KwaZulu Natal, South Africa: A Mathematical Modeling Analysis Accounting for the HIV Epidemic
作者： Christine L. Hathaway, Jacinda Tran, Cari van Schalkwyk, Louise Kuhn, Darcy W. Rao, Ruanne V. Barnabas, Monisha Sharma
摘要中文翻译背景:人乳头瘤病毒 二价 (2vHPV) 和非价 (9vHPV) 疫苗 可预防分别导致 70% 和 90% 宫颈癌 的 HPV 类型。南非指南建议为 9-14 岁的女孩接种 1 剂 2vHPV。将建议改为 9vHPV 可以避免更多的 宫颈癌 负担,但 9vHPV 在南非的成本效益和成本阈值尚不清楚。方法:使用参数化于南非夸祖鲁-纳塔尔省的经过验证的区室 HPV 和 HIV 传播 模型,我们模拟了 100 年来 2vHPV 和 9vHPV 实施的场景,其中 2 剂(72% 覆盖率)和 1 剂(80% 和 90% 覆盖率)具有终生或减弱的单剂 疫苗 功效。我们评估了 宫颈癌 病例和避免的死亡。我们还使用支付意愿阈值从 500 美元/避免的伤残调整生命年 (DALY) 到 3015 美元/避免的 DALY(2023 美元)估计 9vHPV 的最大成本/剂量具有成本效益。我们还评估了 9vHPV 按当前成本(110 美元/剂)与 2vHPV(9.23 美元/剂)相比的增量成本效益比。结果:单剂 9vHPV 覆盖率达到 80% 时,与 2vHPV 相比,可避免多 7.9% 的 宫颈癌 病例和多 6.4% 的死亡(终身疗效)。具有成本效益的 9vHPV 最高价格(终身疗效,80% 覆盖率)范围从 15 美元(500 美元/DALY 避免阈值)到 45 美元/剂量(3015 美元/DALY 避免阈值)。按照目前的市场成本,覆盖率达 90% 的单剂 9vHPV 超过了常用的成本效益阈值,增量成本效益比为 10,480 美元/避免 DALY(终身功效)和 8806 美元/避免 DALY(功效减弱)。结论:与 2vHPV 相比,实施 9vHPV 可以更大程度地减少 宫颈癌 负担,但需要降低 疫苗 成本,9vHPV 才能在夸祖鲁-纳塔尔省具有成本效益。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估南非夸祖鲁纳塔尔省青春期前女孩从二价 HPV 疫苗接种 转换为非二价 HPV 疫苗接种 的潜在健康经济影响,并确定这种转换的成本阈值。
设计与对象
使用参数化于南非夸祖鲁-纳塔尔省的经过验证的区室 HPV 和 HIV 传播 模型进行数学建模分析,模拟 100 年来以不同的覆盖率和 疫苗 功效假设实施 2vHPV 和 9vHPV 的场景。
核心方法
该研究使用南非夸祖鲁-纳塔尔省参数化的区室 HPV 和 HIV 传播 模型来模拟 100 年来 2vHPV 和 9vHPV 的实施情况,具有不同的覆盖率和 疫苗 疗效假设,包括终身或减弱的单剂量疗效。
主要结果
覆盖率达 80% 的单剂 9vHPV 与 2vHPV 相比,可避免多 7.9% 的 宫颈癌 病例和多 6.4% 的死亡(终身疗效)。具有成本效益的 9vHPV 最高价格范围为每剂 15 美元至 45 美元,具体取决于支付意愿门槛。按照目前的市场成本,覆盖率达 90% 的单剂 9vHPV 超过了常用的成本效益阈值,增量成本效益比为 10,480 美元/避免 DALY(终身疗效)和 8806 美元/避免 DALY(疗效减弱)。
解释与创新
作者解释说,与 2vHPV 相比,实施 9vHPV 可能会更大程度地减少 宫颈癌 负担,但他们强调,为了使 9vHPV 在夸祖鲁-纳塔尔省具有成本效益,需要降低 疫苗 成本。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为南非有关 HPV 疫苗接种 策略的公共卫生决策提供信息,特别是在考虑成本效益和改用 9vHPV 的潜在好处时。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于数学模型,在捕捉现实世界的复杂性方面可能存在局限性,并且证据仅是抽象的,这可能会影响结论的强度。

Health Economic Impact and Cost Threshold for Switching From Bivalent to Nonavalent HPV Vaccination for Preadolescent Girls in KwaZulu Natal, South Africa: A Mathematical Modeling Analysis Accounting for the HIV Epidemic

Authors: Christine L. Hathaway, Jacinda Tran, Cari van Schalkwyk, Louise Kuhn, Darcy W. Rao, Ruanne V. Barnabas, Monisha Sharma
Original abstract (English)
Background: Human papillomavirus bivalent (2vHPV) and nonavalent (9vHPV) vaccines protect against HPV types causing 70% and 90% of cervical cancers, respectively. South African guidelines recommend vaccinating girls ages 9–14 with 1 dose of 2vHPV. Switching recommendations to 9vHPV could avert more cervical cancer burden, but the cost-effectiveness and cost-threshold of 9vHPV in South Africa is unknown. Methods: Using a validated compartmental HPV and HIV transmission model parameterized to KwaZulu-Natal, South Africa, we simulated scenarios of 2vHPV and 9vHPV implementation over 100 years with 2 doses (72% coverage) and 1 dose (80% and 90% coverage) with lifelong or waning single-dose vaccine efficacy. We evaluated cervical cancer cases and deaths averted. We also estimated the maximum cost/dose for 9vHPV to be cost-effective using willingness-to-pay thresholds from $500/disability-adjusted life-year (DALY) averted to $3015/DALY averted (2023 USD). We also assessed the incremental cost-effectiveness ratios of 9vHPV at current costs ($110/dose) compared with 2vHPV ($9.23/dose). Results: Single-dose 9vHPV at 80% coverage could avert 7.9% more cervical cancer cases and 6.4% more deaths than 2vHPV (lifelong efficacy). The maximum 9vHPV price to be cost-effective (lifelong efficacy, 80% coverage) ranged from $15 ($500/DALY averted threshold) to $45/dose ($3015/DALY averted threshold). At current market costs, single-dose 9vHPV at 90% coverage exceeds commonly used cost-effective thresholds with incremental cost-effectiveness ratios of $10,480/DALY averted (lifelong efficacy) and $8806/DALY averted (waning efficacy). Conclusions: Implementing 9vHPV can result in a greater reduction in cervical cancer burden than 2vHPV, but vaccine costs need to be reduced for 9vHPV to be cost-effective in KwaZulu-Natal.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the potential health economic impact of switching from bivalent to nonavalent HPV vaccination for preadolescent girls in KwaZulu Natal, South Africa, and to determine the cost threshold for such a switch.
Design and population
A mathematical modeling analysis was conducted using a validated compartmental HPV and HIV transmission model parameterized to KwaZulu-Natal, South Africa, simulating scenarios of 2vHPV and 9vHPV implementation over 100 years with varying coverage rates and vaccine efficacy assumptions.
Core methods
The study used a compartmental HPV and HIV transmission model parameterized to KwaZulu-Natal, South Africa, to simulate the implementation of 2vHPV and 9vHPV over 100 years with different coverage rates and vaccine efficacy assumptions, including lifelong or waning single-dose efficacy.
Main results
Single-dose 9vHPV at 80% coverage could avert 7.9% more cervical cancer cases and 6.4% more deaths than 2vHPV (lifelong efficacy). The maximum 9vHPV price to be cost-effective ranged from $15 to $45/dose, depending on the willingness-to-pay threshold. At current market costs, single-dose 9vHPV at 90% coverage exceeded commonly used cost-effective thresholds with incremental cost-effectiveness ratios of $10,480/DALY averted (lifelong efficacy) and $8806/DALY averted (waning efficacy).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that implementing 9vHPV could result in a greater reduction in cervical cancer burden than 2vHPV, but they emphasized that vaccine costs need to be reduced for 9vHPV to be cost-effective in KwaZulu-Natal.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform public health decision-making regarding HPV vaccination strategies in South Africa, particularly in considering cost-effectiveness and the potential benefits of switching to 9vHPV.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on a mathematical model, which may have limitations in capturing real-world complexities, and the evidence is abstract-only, which may affect the strength of the conclusions.

美国各州护理人员报告的提供者向 13 至 17 岁青少年推荐 HPV 疫苗接种 的差异

U.S. state-level variation in caregiver-reported provider recommendation of HPV vaccination to 13-to-17-year-old adolescents
作者： Chinenye Lynette Ejezie, Chengcheng Zhang, Nada Eldawy, Ming Li, Paula M. Cuccaro
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在调查美国青少年中 HPV 疫苗 提供者推荐的州级差异,因为此类建议的地区差异已被记录,但有限的研究集中在州级差异上。
设计与对象
使用全国青少年免疫调查 (2023) 的数据进行了一项横断面研究,分析了护理人员报告的提供者对美国 13 至 17 岁青少年 HPV 疫苗 的建议。
核心方法
使用多变量逻辑回归对提供者推荐的 HPV 疫苗 的州特定变化进行建模,并采用比例估计来检查州特定的 患病率。
主要结果
Provider recommendation varied considerably across states, with lower odds of reporting a recommendation in Arkansas (AOR = 0.56, 95% CI = 0.33 to 0.96), Georgia (AOR = 0.49, 95% CI = 0.26 to 0.93), and Mississippi (AOR = 0.42, 95% CI = 0.24 to 0.74), and higher odds in New Hampshire (AOR = 2.40, 95% CI = 1.23 to 4.68) compared with California. Greater heterogeneity was observed in the South compared with other regions.
解释与创新
研究结果强调了美国各州 HPV 疫苗 推荐做法的差异,表明可能需要采取有针对性的干预措施来解决 疫苗接种 率的差异。
科研与公卫意义
结果可以为公共卫生策略提供信息,通过确定推荐率较低的州并实施有针对性的干预措施来提高 HPV 疫苗接种 率。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于护理人员报告的数据,这可能会引入偏差,并且证据仅限于州一级的差异,而没有进一步探索潜在因素。

U.S. state-level variation in caregiver-reported provider recommendation of HPV vaccination to 13-to-17-year-old adolescents

Authors: Chinenye Lynette Ejezie, Chengcheng Zhang, Nada Eldawy, Ming Li, Paula M. Cuccaro
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Introduction Although regional differences in provider recommendation of human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccine have been documented, limited research has examined variation at the state level. Therefore, this study examined state-level differences in caregiver-reported health care provider recommendation of HPV vaccines among 13-to-17-year-old adolescents in the United States. Method Using a cross-sectional design to examine data from the National Immunization Survey–Teen (2023), multivariable logistic regression was used to model state-specific variations in provider recommendation of HPV vaccine. Proportion estimation was used to examine state-specific prevalence. Result Provider recommendation varied considerably across states. For example, the odds of reporting a recommendation were lower among caregivers of adolescents in Arkansas (adjusted odds ratio [AOR] = 0.56, 95% confidence interval [CI] = 0.33 to 0.96), Georgia (AOR = 0.49, 95% CI = 0.26 to 0.93), and Mississippi (AOR = 0.42, 95% CI = 0.24 to 0.74), and higher among caregivers in New Hampshire (AOR = 2.40, 95% CI = 1.23 to 4.68) compared with adolescents in California. Greater heterogeneity was observed in the South compared with other regions. For example, the prevalence of not receiving a recommendation was 16% in Maryland and 41% in Mississippi. Conclusion The findings underscore the need to improve HPV vaccine recommendation in some states in the United States. In particular, states with low rates should be targeted for focused interventions.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to examine state-level differences in provider recommendation of HPV vaccines among adolescents in the United States, as regional variations in such recommendations have been documented but limited research has focused on state-level variation.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted using data from the National Immunization Survey–Teen (2023), analyzing caregiver-reported provider recommendations for HPV vaccines among 13-to-17-year-old adolescents across U.S.
Core methods
Multivariable logistic regression was used to model state-specific variations in provider recommendation of HPV vaccine, and proportion estimation was employed to examine state-specific prevalence.
Main results
Provider recommendation varied considerably across states, with lower odds of reporting a recommendation in Arkansas (AOR = 0.56, 95% CI = 0.33 to 0.96), Georgia (AOR = 0.49, 95% CI = 0.26 to 0.93), and Mississippi (AOR = 0.42, 95% CI = 0.24 to 0.74), and higher odds in New Hampshire (AOR = 2.40, 95% CI = 1.23 to 4.68) compared with California. Greater heterogeneity was observed in the South compared with other regions.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight the variability in HPV vaccine recommendation practices across U.S. states, suggesting that targeted interventions may be necessary to address disparities in vaccination rates.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results can inform public health strategies to improve HPV vaccination rates by identifying states with low recommendation rates and implementing focused interventions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on caregiver-reported data, which may introduce bias, and the evidence is limited to state-level variations without further exploration of underlying factors.

阴道镜放大扫描辅助CO 2 激光治疗 (CMSLT)治疗阴道高级鳞状上皮内病变(VaHSIL):一项单中心前瞻性队列研究研究。

Colposcopic magnified scanning-assisted CO 2 laser treatment (CMSLT) for vaginal high-grade squamous intraepithelial lesion (VaHSIL): A single-center prospective cohort study.
作者： Annalisa Pieralli, Raffaella Venzi, Claudia Maccarrone, Claudia Giglioni, Claudia Bianchi, Giulia Fantappiè, Luca Giannoni, Irene Fusco, Massimiliano Fambrini
摘要中文翻译评估阴道镜放大扫描激光 治疗 (CMSLT) 的可行性,分为激光切除 (CMSLE) 和汽化 (CMSLV),使用扫描仪辅助 CO 2 激光作为阴道高级鳞状上皮内病变 (vaHSIL) 患者的单一门诊治疗方式。阴道高级鳞状上皮内病变 (vaHSIL) 是一种与持续性 高危型HPV 感染相关的罕见疾病,具有进展为浸润性癌的具体潜力。由于诊断的复杂性和疾病的罕见性,其最佳临床管理和 治疗 标准化仍存在争议。这项前瞻性非随机 队列研究 研究于 2022 年 1 月至 2024 年 6 月期间对 63 名经组织学证实患有 vaHSIL 的患者进行。根据 2011 年 IFCPC 阴道镜术语,患者被分配到 CMSLV 组 (n = 22) 或 CMSLE 组 (n = 41)。门诊方案使用了由计算机手术扫描仪辅助显微操作器实现的激光系统。为期两年的随访包括每半年进行一次 HPV 检测、细胞学检查和阴道镜检查。随访依从性很高,脱落率限制在 11.3%。vaHSIL 的累积复发风险为 14.5%,两种治疗方式之间没有统计学上的显着差异,尽管 CMSLE 组显示出较高的 2 年无病生存率(CMSLE 队列研究 组的 2 年无病生存率为 70%,而 CMSLE 队列研究 组的浸润性癌进展率为 1.6%),表明初始阴道镜分配时隐匿性癌症的漏诊率为 2.4%。仅发生一种主要并发症(阴道穿孔,子宫切除术后患者的发生率为 1.6%),没有报告轻微并发症或长期后遗症(性交困难或疼痛)。CMSLT 可能代表了可行的方法,因为它恰好是安全、有效的并且与患者的依从性相关。扫描仪辅助技术的精度可最大限度地减少热损伤和隐匿性癌症治疗不足的风险,从而实现量身定制的高效治疗。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估阴道镜放大扫描激光 治疗 (CMSLT) 对于阴道高级鳞状上皮内病变 (vaHSIL) 患者的可行性,这是一种与持续性 高危型HPV 感染和潜在进展为浸润性癌相关的罕见疾病。
设计与对象
2022 年 1 月至 2024 年 6 月期间,对 63 名经组织学证实患有 vaHSIL 的患者进行了一项单中心前瞻性非随机 队列研究 研究。根据 2011 年 IFCPC 阴道镜术语,患者被分配到 CMSLV 组 (n = 22) 或 CMSLE 组 (n = 41)。
核心方法
门诊方案使用了由计算机手术扫描仪辅助显微操作器实现的激光系统。为期两年的随访包括每半年进行一次 HPV 检测、细胞学检查和阴道镜检查。
主要结果
vaHSIL 的累积复发风险为 14.5%,两种治疗方式之间无统计学差异。CMSLE 组显示出较高的 2 年无病生存率(70%,而侵袭性癌进展率为 1.6%(CMSLE 队列研究 中的 1 例)),表明初始阴道镜分配时隐匿性癌症的漏诊率为 2.4%。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为表明 CMSLT 可能是一种可行的方法,因为它安全、有效,并且与患者的依从性相关。扫描仪辅助技术的精度可最大限度地减少热损伤和隐匿性癌症治疗不足的风险,从而实现量身定制的高效治疗。
科研与公卫意义
该研究结果可能为 vaHSIL 标准化 治疗 方案的开发提供信息,特别是在 HPV 相关疾病的背景下,通过强调扫描仪辅助激光 治疗 在提高无病生存率和降低隐匿性癌症风险方面的潜在益处。
局限与证据强度
该研究是非随机的,可能会引入偏差,并且随访期仅限于两年。由于研究设计和样本量相对较小,证据强度中等。

Colposcopic magnified scanning-assisted CO 2 laser treatment (CMSLT) for vaginal high-grade squamous intraepithelial lesion (VaHSIL): A single-center prospective cohort study.

Authors: Annalisa Pieralli, Raffaella Venzi, Claudia Maccarrone, Claudia Giglioni, Claudia Bianchi, Giulia Fantappiè, Luca Giannoni, Irene Fusco, Massimiliano Fambrini
Original abstract (English)
To evaluate the feasibility of Colposcopic Magnified Scanning Laser Treatments (CMSLT), divided into laser excision (CMSLE) and vaporization (CMSLV), using a scanner-assisted CO 2 laser as a singular outpatient therapeutic modality for patients affected by vaginal high-grade squamous intraepithelial lesion (vaHSIL). vaginal high-grade squamous intraepithelial lesion (vaHSIL) is a rare condition associated with persistent high-risk HPV infection, carrying a concrete potential for progression into invasive carcinoma. Due to diagnostic complexities and the rarity of the disease, its optimal clinical management and treatment standardization remain controversial. This prospective non-randomized cohort study was conducted between January 2022 and June 2024 on 63 patients with histologically confirmed vaHSIL. Based on the 2011 IFCPC colposcopic terminology, patients were assigned to either the CMSLV group (n = 22) or the CMSLE group (n = 41). The outpatient protocol utilized a laser system implemented with a computerized surgical scanner-assisted micromanipulator. The two-year follow-up included semi-annual screenings with HPV testing, cytology, and colposcopy. Follow-up adherence was high, with a dropout rate limited to 11.3%. The cumulative recurrence risk for vaHSIL was 14.5%, with no statistically significant difference between the two modalities, although the CMSLE group showed a higher 2-year disease-free survival rate (70% vs. The progression rate to invasive carcinoma was 1.6% 1 cases in the CMSLE cohort), revealing a 2.4% rate of occult cancer missed at initial colposcopic assignment. Only one major complication occurred (vaginal perforation, 1.6% in a post-hysterectomy patient), with no minor complications or long-term sequelae (dyspareunia or pain) reported. CMSLT may represent feasible approach since it happened to be safe, effective and associated with patient compliance. The precision of the scanner-assisted technology minimizes thermal damage and the risk of undertreating occult cancer, allowing for tailored and efficient therapy.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the feasibility of Colposcopic Magnified Scanning Laser Treatments (CMSLT) for patients with vaginal high-grade squamous intraepithelial lesion (vaHSIL), a rare condition associated with persistent high-risk HPV infection and potential progression to invasive carcinoma.
Design and population
A single-center prospective non-randomized cohort study was conducted between January 2022 and June 2024 on 63 patients with histologically confirmed vaHSIL. Patients were assigned to either the CMSLV group (n = 22) or the CMSLE group (n = 41) based on the 2011 IFCPC colposcopic terminology.
Core methods
The outpatient protocol utilized a laser system implemented with a computerized surgical scanner-assisted micromanipulator. The two-year follow-up included semi-annual screenings with HPV testing, cytology, and colposcopy.
Main results
The cumulative recurrence risk for vaHSIL was 14.5%, with no statistically significant difference between the two modalities. The CMSLE group showed a higher 2-year disease-free survival rate (70% vs. The progression rate to invasive carcinoma was 1.6% (1 case in the CMSLE cohort), revealing a 2.4% rate of occult cancer missed at initial colposcopic assignment.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as suggesting that CMSLT may represent a feasible approach since it was safe, effective, and associated with patient compliance. The precision of the scanner-assisted technology minimizes thermal damage and the risk of undertreating occult cancer, allowing for tailored and efficient therapy.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform the development of standardized treatment protocols for vaHSIL, particularly in the context of HPV-associated diseases, by highlighting the potential benefits of scanner-assisted laser treatments in improving disease-free survival and reducing the risk of occult cancer.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is non-randomized, which may introduce bias, and the follow-up period was limited to two years. The evidence strength is moderate due to the study's design and the relatively small sample size.

用于预测口咽癌 治疗 结果的多模态深度学习预后模型。

Multimodal Deep Learning Prognostic Model for Predicting Treatment Outcomes for Oropharyngeal Carcinoma.
作者： Simeng Zhu, Marissa Gilbert, Farzan Siddiqui
摘要中文翻译目的:需要准确预测口咽鳞状细胞癌(OPSCC)的局部区域复发(LRR)来指导 治疗 降级决策,但缺乏整合影像和临床数据的经过验证的预后模型。我们开发并评估了一种多模式深度学习模型,将治疗前计算机断层扫描 (CT) 与临床变量相结合来预测 LRR。方法:在这项回顾性多中心预后研究中,我们收集了 1,384 名经组织学证实的 OPSCC 患者,接受根治性放疗,联合或不联合化疗,且未手术切除原发肿瘤。数据取自 1 个私人机构档案馆和 5 个公开档案馆。将融合 CT 和临床特征的交叉注意力模型与特征串联、仅临床和仅成像模型进行比较。主要结局是LRR。通过 3 倍交叉验证的 Harrell 一致性指数(C 指数)评估歧视,并通过 Kaplan-Meier 分析和对数秩检验进行风险分层。归因分析用于识别有影响力的预测因素。结果:在中位随访时间 59.8 个月(四分位数范围,25.8-89.0 个月)内,1,384 名患者中有 161 名 (11.6%) 发生 LRR。交叉注意力模型实现了最高的平均 C 指数,0.718(范围,0.699-0.734),其次是仅临床模型(0.705;范围,0.680-0.735)、特征串联模型(0.697;范围,0.673-0.721)和仅成像模型(0.674;范围,0.666-0.680)型号。在所有三个方面,高风险患者的无 LRR 生存期均显着短于低风险患者(每个对数秩 P < .001)。吸烟史和 人乳头瘤病毒 状态是最有影响力的临床预测因素。结论:将 CT 与临床变量相结合的多模态交叉注意力深度学习模型在 OPSCC 中的 LRR 预测方面显示出比单模态方法更好的数值辨别力。在此类模型告知个性化 治疗 决策之前,需要进行前瞻性外部验证。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
需要准确预测口咽鳞状细胞癌 (OPSCC) 的局部区域复发 (LRR) 来指导 治疗 降级决策,但缺乏整合影像和临床数据的经过验证的预后模型。
设计与对象
在这项回顾性多中心预后研究中,我们汇集了 1,384 名经组织学证实的 OPSCC 患者,接受根治性放疗,伴或不伴同步化疗,且未手术切除原发肿瘤。
核心方法
数据取自 1 个私人机构档案馆和 5 个公开档案馆。主要结局是LRR。通过 3 倍交叉验证的 Harrell 一致性指数(C 指数)评估歧视,并通过 Kaplan-Meier 分析和对数秩检验进行风险分层。归因分析用于识别有影响力的预测因素。
主要结果
中位随访时间为 59.8 个月(四分位数范围,25.8-89.0 个月),1,384 名患者中有 161 名 (11.6%) 发生 LRR。交叉注意力模型实现了最高的平均 C 指数,0.718(范围,0.699-0.734),其次是仅临床模型(0.705;范围,0.680-0.735)、特征串联模型(0.697;范围,0.673-0.721)和仅成像模型(0.674;范围,0.666-0.680)型号。在所有三个方面,高风险患者的无 LRR 生存期均显着短于低风险患者(每个对数秩 P < .001)。吸烟史和 人乳头瘤病毒 状态是最有影响力的临床预测因素。
解释与创新
将 CT 与临床变量相结合的多模态交叉注意力深度学习模型在 OPSCC 中的 LRR 预测方面显示出比单模态方法更好的数值辨别力。
科研与公卫意义
该研究结果可能通过提高 LRR 预测的准确性,为 HPV 相关口咽癌患者的个体化 治疗 决策提供信息。
局限与证据强度
在此类模型告知个性化 治疗 决策之前,需要进行前瞻性外部验证。

Multimodal Deep Learning Prognostic Model for Predicting Treatment Outcomes for Oropharyngeal Carcinoma.

Authors: Simeng Zhu, Marissa Gilbert, Farzan Siddiqui
Original abstract (English)
PURPOSE: Accurate prediction of locoregional recurrence (LRR) in oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinoma (OPSCC) is needed to guide treatment de-escalation decisions, but validated prognostic models integrating imaging and clinical data are lacking. We developed and evaluated a multimodal deep learning model combining pretreatment computed tomography (CT) with clinical variables to predict LRR. METHODS: In this retrospective multicenter prognostic study, we assembled 1,384 patients with histologically confirmed OPSCC treated with definitive radiotherapy with or without concurrent chemotherapy and without surgical resection of the primary tumor. Data were drawn from 1 private institutional archive and five publicly available archives. A cross-attention model fusing CT and clinical features was compared with feature concatenation, clinical-only, and imaging-only models. The primary outcome was LRR. Discrimination was assessed by Harrell Concordance Index (C-index) across 3-fold cross-validation, and risk stratification by Kaplan-Meier analysis with log-rank testing. Attribution analysis was used to identify influential predictors. RESULTS: LRR occurred in 161 of 1,384 patients (11.6%) over a median follow-up of 59.8 months (interquartile range, 25.8-89.0 months). The cross-attention model achieved the highest mean C-index, 0.718 (range, 0.699-0.734), followed by the clinical-only (0.705; range, 0.680-0.735), feature concatenation (0.697; range, 0.673-0.721), and imaging-only (0.674; range, 0.666-0.680) models. High-risk patients had significantly shorter LRR-free survival than low-risk patients in all three folds (log-rank P < .001 for each). Smoking history and human papillomavirus status were the most influential clinical predictors. CONCLUSION: A multimodal cross-attention deep learning model integrating CT with clinical variables showed numerically better discrimination for LRR prediction in OPSCC than unimodal approaches. Prospective external validation is required before such models inform individualized treatment decisions.

Question and background
Accurate prediction of locoregional recurrence (LRR) in oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinoma (OPSCC) is needed to guide treatment de-escalation decisions, but validated prognostic models integrating imaging and clinical data are lacking.
Design and population
In this retrospective multicenter prognostic study, we assembled 1,384 patients with histologically confirmed OPSCC treated with definitive radiotherapy with or without concurrent chemotherapy and without surgical resection of the primary tumor.
Core methods
Data were drawn from 1 private institutional archive and five publicly available archives. The primary outcome was LRR. Discrimination was assessed by Harrell Concordance Index (C-index) across 3-fold cross-validation, and risk stratification by Kaplan-Meier analysis with log-rank testing. Attribution analysis was used to identify influential predictors.
Main results
LRR occurred in 161 of 1,384 patients (11.6%) over a median follow-up of 59.8 months (interquartile range, 25.8-89.0 months). The cross-attention model achieved the highest mean C-index, 0.718 (range, 0.699-0.734), followed by the clinical-only (0.705; range, 0.680-0.735), feature concatenation (0.697; range, 0.673-0.721), and imaging-only (0.674; range, 0.666-0.680) models. High-risk patients had significantly shorter LRR-free survival than low-risk patients in all three folds (log-rank P < .001 for each). Smoking history and human papillomavirus status were the most influential clinical predictors.
