A multimodal cross-attention deep learning model integrating CT with clinical variables showed numerically better discrimination for LRR prediction in OPSCC than unimodal approaches.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform individualized treatment decisions for patients with HPV-associated oropharyngeal cancer by improving the accuracy of LRR prediction.
Limitations and evidence strength
Prospective external validation is required before such models inform individualized treatment decisions.

HPV 相关妇科恶性肿瘤 发病率 和生存率的个体和环境变量差异:一项基于人群的研究

Differences in HPV-related gynaecological malignancies incidence and survival by individual and contextual variables: a population-based study
作者： Candela Pino-Rosón, Ana María Ruiz Tornero, María José Soto Zabalgogeazcoa, María Felicitas Domínguez-Berjón, Nuria Aragonés, Candela Pino‐Rosón, M. Felicitas Domínguez‐Berjón
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探讨个体和背景因素(如社会经济地位和 诊断 阶段)在 发病率 和 HPV 相关妇科恶性肿瘤(如外阴、阴道和 发病率)生存率方面的差异。摘要 外阴癌、阴道癌和 宫颈癌 都是妇科恶性肿瘤,它们都有一个关键的危险因素:感染 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV)。
设计与对象
该研究是 2018 年至 2020 年在马德里社区 (CM) 进行的一项基于人群的分析,重点关注被诊断患有外阴、阴道和 宫颈癌 的女性。数据取自马德里人口癌症登记处 (PBCR-CM)。由于 HPV 疫苗 可用,许多肿瘤被认为是可以预防的。
核心方法
进行描述性分析以评估社会人口统计学和肿瘤特征。计算了粗略的、特定年龄的和年龄标准化的 发病率 率。计算五年观察生存率和净生存率,并使用灵活的参数模型进行调整,以评估不同阶段死亡风险的差异。
主要结果
与外阴/阴道癌患者 (50%) 相比,宫颈癌 患者的生存率 (71%) 更高。III 级肿瘤和转移性肿瘤患者的生存率最低。在全球范围内计算了五年观察生存率和净生存率,并按社会经济地位和阶段进行分层。
解释与创新
该研究强调了社会经济地位和肿瘤分期对患有 HPV 相关妇科癌症的女性生存结果的影响的重要性。本研究概述了 CM 中与 HPV 相关的妇科癌症。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为有针对性的公共卫生策略提供信息,以解决 HPV 相关癌症生存率的差异,特别是通过改善社会经济地位较低的群体获得护理和早期发现的机会。为了解决不平等问题,PBCR 通过确保高质量的流行病学 监测 发挥着不可或缺的作用。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于来自单个地区(马德里自治区)的数据,并依赖于登记数据,该数据可能无法捕获所有相关的背景因素。由于该研究的观察性质和潜在的无法测量的混杂因素,证据的强度中等。

Differences in HPV-related gynaecological malignancies incidence and survival by individual and contextual variables: a population-based study

Authors: Candela Pino-Rosón, Ana María Ruiz Tornero, María José Soto Zabalgogeazcoa, María Felicitas Domínguez-Berjón, Nuria Aragonés, Candela Pino‐Rosón, M. Felicitas Domínguez‐Berjón
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Vulvar, vaginal and cervical cancers are gynaecological malignancies that share a key risk factor: infection with the human papillomavirus (HPV). As an HPV vaccine is available, many tumours are considered preventable. This study analysed the incidence and survival of vulvar, vaginal and cervical cancers in women living in the Community of Madrid (CM) from 2018–2020, by socioeconomic status and stage. Data of invasive tumours were extracted from the Population-Based Cancer Registry of Madrid (PBCR-CM). Descriptive analyses were performed for sociodemographic and tumour characteristics. Crude, age-specific and age-standardised incidence rates were calculated. Five-year observed and net survival were calculated, globally and stratified by socioeconomic status and stage. Flexible parametric models were adjusted to determine differences in the risk of death by stage. Between 2018–2020, 954 patients were diagnosed, corresponding to an age-standardised rate of 10.8 cases per 100 000 women-years. Most tumours are squamous cell carcinomas, grade II, and diagnosed at early stages. Cervical cancer patients showed greater survival results (71% vs 50% for vulvar/vaginal cancer). Survival was lowest among patients with grade-III and metastatic tumours. This study provides an overview of HPV-related gynaecological cancers in CM. To address inequalities, PBCR plays an indispensable role by ensuring high-quality epidemiological surveillance.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate differences in the incidence and survival rates of HPV-related gynaecological malignancies, such as vulvar, vaginal, and cervical cancers, by individual and contextual factors like socioeconomic status and stage of diagnosis. Abstract Vulvar, vaginal and cervical cancers are gynaecological malignancies that share a key risk factor: infection with the human papillomavirus (HPV).
Design and population
The study was a population-based analysis conducted in the Community of Madrid (CM) from 2018 to 2020, focusing on women diagnosed with vulvar, vaginal, and cervical cancers. Data were extracted from the Population-Based Cancer Registry of Madrid (PBCR-CM). As an HPV vaccine is available, many tumours are considered preventable.
Core methods
Descriptive analyses were performed to assess sociodemographic and tumour characteristics. Crude, age-specific, and age-standardised incidence rates were calculated. Five-year observed and net survival were computed, with adjustments made using flexible parametric models to evaluate differences in the risk of death by stage.
Main results
Cervical cancer patients had higher survival rates (71%) compared to those with vulvar/vaginal cancer (50%). Survival was lowest among patients with grade-III and metastatic tumours. Five-year observed and net survival were calculated, globally and stratified by socioeconomic status and stage.
Interpretation and novelty
The study highlights the importance of socioeconomic status and tumour stage in influencing survival outcomes among women with HPV-related gynaecological cancers. This study provides an overview of HPV-related gynaecological cancers in CM.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform targeted public health strategies to address disparities in survival rates for HPV-associated cancers, particularly by improving access to care and early detection in lower socioeconomic groups. To address inequalities, PBCR plays an indispensable role by ensuring high-quality epidemiological surveillance.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to data from a single region (Community of Madrid) and relies on registry data, which may not capture all relevant contextual factors. The evidence is of moderate strength due to the observational nature of the study and potential for unmeasured confounding.

HPV驱动的DNA甲基化可塑性重塑头颈癌的免疫调节

HPV-driven DNA methylation plasticity reshapes immune regulation in head and neck cancer
作者： Felipe Luz Torres Silva, Fatemeh Farshadi, Marco Antonio Mascarella, Alex Mlynarek, Moulay A. Alaoui-Jamali, Michael Hier, Mariana Camargo Maschietto, Sabrina Daniela Silva Wurzba, Moulay A. Alaoui‐Jamali
摘要中文翻译摘要 近几十年来,头颈癌 (HNC) 中 HPV 相关 HNC 的 发病率 呈指数增长。尽管已知 HPV 感染会重新编程 宿主 表观遗传景观,但 HPV 介导的 DNA 甲基化变化的细胞和功能后果仍不清楚。在这里,我们证明了 HPV 驱动的表观遗传重编程重塑了 HNC 肿瘤微环境 (TME) 内的免疫调节,产生与病毒状态相关的独特甲基化景观。基因组 全甲基化分析鉴定出富含免疫相关途径的差异甲基化位点,支持表观遗传重塑在塑造 HPV 特异性肿瘤免疫相互作用中的核心作用。为了将表观遗传差异与 TME 内的细胞组织和功能联系起来,我们使用高维单细胞成像质谱流式仪 (IMC) 表征了免疫和基质区室、细胞间相互作用和空间结构。主要发现在外部单细胞 RNA 测序 队列研究 中得到独立验证。我们确定了 770 个差异甲基化位置(P < 0.05,|logFC| <0.20),映射到 386 个基因,区分 HPV 阳性和 HPV 阴性肿瘤,并显着富集了免疫相关通路。单细胞高维 IMC 揭示了肿瘤类型之间不同的免疫结构。在 HPV 阴性肿瘤中,免疫排斥主要与成纤维细胞主导的空间组织相关,该空间组织在物理上和功能上将肿瘤细胞与免疫 监测 隔离。相比之下,HPV 阳性肿瘤中的 T 细胞表现出内在的自分泌调节程序,其特征是强大的 I 型和 II 型干扰素信号传导,与增强的肿瘤识别和免疫参与相一致。这些发现表明,HPV 相关的 DNA 甲基化重编程塑造了 HNC 中 TME 的空间和功能组织,促进免疫调节和肿瘤免疫逃避的不同机制。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解 HPV 驱动的 DNA 甲基化变化如何影响头颈癌 (HNC) 的免疫调节。近几十年来,HPV 相关 HNC 的 发病率 呈指数级增长,凸显了探索其对肿瘤生物学影响的必要性。
设计与对象
该研究重点关注 HPV 相关的头颈癌,检查肿瘤微环境 (TME) 和免疫调节。它分析了 HPV 阳性和 HPV 阴性肿瘤中的 基因组 全甲基化模式和免疫结构。
核心方法
研究人员进行了 基因组 全甲基化分析,并使用高维单细胞成像质谱流式分析仪 (IMC) 表征了免疫和基质区室、细胞间相互作用和空间结构。主要发现在外部单细胞 RNA 测序 队列研究 中得到验证。
主要结果
基因组 全甲基化分析确定了 770 个差异甲基化位置(P < 0.05,|logFC| <0.20),映射到区分 HPV 阳性和 HPV 阴性肿瘤的 386 个基因。这些变化在免疫相关途径中显着丰富。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,HPV 相关的 DNA 甲基化重编程塑造了 HNC 中 TME 的空间和功能组织,促进免疫调节和肿瘤免疫逃避的不同机制。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果提供了有关 HPV 驱动的表观遗传变化在免疫调节和肿瘤免疫逃避中的作用的见解,这可以为未来有关 HPV 相关癌症及其 治疗 策略的研究提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅基于抽象证据,这可能会限制分析的深度。然而,研究结果在外部 队列研究 中得到了独立验证,支持中等程度的置信度。

HPV-driven DNA methylation plasticity reshapes immune regulation in head and neck cancer

Authors: Felipe Luz Torres Silva, Fatemeh Farshadi, Marco Antonio Mascarella, Alex Mlynarek, Moulay A. Alaoui-Jamali, Michael Hier, Mariana Camargo Maschietto, Sabrina Daniela Silva Wurzba, Moulay A. Alaoui‐Jamali
Original abstract (English)
Abstract The incidence of HPV-related HNC has exponentially increased over recent decades in head and neck cancer (HNC). Although HPV infection is known to reprogram the host epigenetic landscape, the cellular and functional consequences of HPV-mediated DNA methylation changes remain poorly defined. Here we demonstrated that HPV-driven epigenetic reprogramming reshapes immune regulation within the HNC tumor microenvironment (TME), generating distinct methylation landscapes associated with viral status. Genome-wide methylation analysis identified differentially methylated loci enriched in immune-related pathways, supporting a central role for epigenetic remodeling in shaping HPV-specific tumor–immune interactions. To link epigenetic differences to cellular organization and function within the TME, we characterized immune and stromal compartments, cell–cell interactions, and spatial architecture using high-dimensional single-cell imaging mass cytometry (IMC). Key findings were independently validated in an external single-cell RNA sequencing cohort. We identified 770 differentially methylated positions (P < 0.05, |logFC| <0.20) mapping to 386 genes that distinguish HPV-positive from HPV-negative tumors, with significant enrichment in immune-related pathways. Single-cell high-dimension IMC revealed distinct immune architectures between tumor types. In HPV-negative tumors, immune exclusion was primarily associated with fibroblast-dominated spatial organization that physically and functionally insulated tumor cells from immune surveillance. In contrast, T cells in HPV-positive tumors exhibited an intrinsic autocrine regulatory program characterized by robust type I and type II interferon signaling, consistent with enhanced tumor recognition and immune engagement. These findings demonstrate that HPV-associated DNA methylation reprogramming shapes the spatial and functional organization of the TME in HNC, promoting divergent mechanisms of immune regulation and tumor immune evasion.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand how HPV-driven DNA methylation changes affect immune regulation in head and neck cancer (HNC). The incidence of HPV-related HNC has exponentially increased over recent decades, highlighting the need to explore its impact on tumor biology.
Design and population
The study focused on HPV-related head and neck cancer, examining the tumor microenvironment (TME) and immune regulation. It analyzed genome-wide methylation patterns and immune architecture in HPV-positive and HPV-negative tumors.
Core methods
The researchers conducted genome-wide methylation analysis and characterized immune and stromal compartments, cell–cell interactions, and spatial architecture using high-dimensional single-cell imaging mass cytometry (IMC). Key findings were validated in an external single-cell RNA sequencing cohort.
Main results
Genome-wide methylation analysis identified 770 differentially methylated positions (P < 0.05, |logFC| <0.20) mapping to 386 genes that distinguish HPV-positive from HPV-negative tumors. These changes were significantly enriched in immune-related pathways.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate that HPV-associated DNA methylation reprogramming shapes the spatial and functional organization of the TME in HNC, promoting divergent mechanisms of immune regulation and tumor immune evasion.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results provide insights into the role of HPV-driven epigenetic changes in immune regulation and tumor immune evasion, which could inform future research on HPV-associated cancers and their treatment strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on abstract-only evidence, which may limit the depth of analysis. However, the findings were independently validated in an external cohort, supporting a moderate level of confidence.

福州女性健康检查中HPV 患病率、亚型分布及疫苗相关变化:11年回顾性研究(2014-2025)

HPV Prevalence, Subtype Distribution, and Vaccine-Associated Changes in Fuzhou Women Undergoing Health Examinations: An 11-Year Retrospective Study (2014–2025)
作者： pengju cao, Binbin Zhuang, Yongwen Tang, Yang� Yang, Xiuqing Shen, Shaoting Chen, Huan Zhou, cao p
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景:旨在评估中国福州地区 HPV 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染率、亚型分布以及 HPV 疫苗接种 前后的年龄特异性模式,为 宫颈癌 预防提供信息。方法:本回顾性研究分析了 2014 年 2 月至 2025 年 12 月在福州大学附属省立医院接受 HPV 基因分型的 29,041 名女性(16-79 岁)。参与者分为 疫苗接种 前(2014-2017 年;n=18,542)和 疫苗接种 后(2020-2025 年;n=18,542)。n=10,499)。采用荧光微球杂交,检测出27种HPV亚型(17种高危,10种低危);比较了总体阳性率、高/低风险率和特定类型的 患病率。使用限制三次样条回归对年龄-感染概率进行建模。结果:总体 HPV 阳性率为 15.77%(术前 15.65%,术后 15.97%;P=0.213)。两个阶段均观察到持续的 U 形年龄分布,峰值在 ≤30 岁和≥51 岁(最低点 31-40 岁)。疫苗接种后,高危型HPV下降(14.49%→12.90%;RR=0.89;P<0.001),尤其是在31-40岁组(RR=0.79);低危型HPV增加(5.64%至7.40%;RR=1.31;P<0.001),尤其是≤30岁(RR=2.29)。二价靶向类型 (HPV16/18) 下降了 48.0%,九价靶向类型下降了 13.4%,受益最大的是 31-40 岁。在年轻女性中,HPV16/18 下降,而 HPV6、HPV81 和 HPV44 上升(≤30 岁的 HPV6 RR=4.24;HPV81 RR=3.17)。结论:福州地区的 HPV 疫苗接种 降低了 HPV16/18 患病率,但年轻女性中非 疫苗 型(HPV6、HPV81)的增加提示可能存在型型替代。持续的分子 监测、更广泛的 疫苗 和年龄特异性筛查是必要的。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估中国福州 HPV 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染率、亚型分布以及 HPV 疫苗接种 前后的年龄特异性模式的长期趋势,为 宫颈癌 预防提供信息。
设计与对象
这项回顾性研究分析了2014年2月至2025年12月在福州大学附属省立医院接受HPV基因分型的29,041名女性(16-79岁)。参与者分为疫苗接种前(2014-2017年;n=18,542)和疫苗接种后(2020-2025年;n=18,542)。n=10,499)。
核心方法
使用限制三次样条回归对年龄-感染概率进行建模。采用荧光微球杂交,检测出27种HPV亚型(17种高危,10种低危);比较了总体阳性率、高/低风险率和特定类型的 患病率。
主要结果
总体 HPV 阳性率为 15.77%(术前 15.65%,术后 15.97%;P=0.213)。两个阶段均观察到持续的 U 形年龄分布,峰值在 ≤30 岁和≥51 岁(最低点 31-40 岁)。疫苗接种后,高危型HPV下降(14.49%→12.90%;RR=0.89;P<0.001),尤其是在31-40岁组(RR=0.79);低危型HPV增加(5.64%至7.40%;RR=1.31;P<0.001),尤其是≤30岁(RR=2.29)。
解释与创新
福州的 HPV 疫苗接种 降低了 HPV16/18 患病率,但年轻女性中非 疫苗 类型(HPV6、HPV81)的增加表明可能存在类型替代。
科研与公卫意义
需要持续进行分子 监测、更广泛的 疫苗 和年龄特异性筛查,以解决观察到的 HPV 亚型 患病率 的变化。
局限与证据强度
该研究是回顾性的并且基于单一中心,这可能会限制普遍性。证据是中等的,因为它依赖于没有随机干预的观察数据。

HPV Prevalence, Subtype Distribution, and Vaccine-Associated Changes in Fuzhou Women Undergoing Health Examinations: An 11-Year Retrospective Study (2014–2025)

Authors: pengju cao, Binbin Zhuang, Yongwen Tang, Yang� Yang, Xiuqing Shen, Shaoting Chen, Huan Zhou, cao p
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background: To assess long-term trends in Human Papillomavirus (HPV) infection rates, subtype distribution, and age-specific patterns before and after HPV vaccination in Fuzhou, China, to inform cervical cancer prevention. Methods: This retrospective study analyzed 29,041 women (aged 16–79) who underwent HPV genotyping at Fuzhou University Affiliated Provincial Hospital from February 2014 to December 2025. Participants were grouped into pre-vaccination (2014–2017; n=18,542) and post-vaccination (2020–2025; n=10,499). Using fluorescent microsphere hybridization, 27 HPV subtypes (17 high-risk, 10 low-risk) were detected; overall positivity, high-/low-risk rates, and type-specific prevalence were compared. Age–infection probability was modeled using restricted cubic spline regression. Results: Overall HPV positivity was 15.77% (15.65% pre vs. 15.97% post; P=0.213). A persistent U-shaped age distribution was observed in both phases, peaking at ≤30 and ≥51 years (nadir 31-40). Post-vaccination, high-risk HPV decreased (14.49%→12.90%; RR=0.89; P<0.001), notably in the 31–40 group (RR=0.79); low-risk HPV increased (5.64% to 7.40%; RR=1.31; P<0.001), especially ≤30 years (RR=2.29). Bivalent-targeted types (HPV16/18) fell 48.0% and nine-valent-targeted types 13.4%, with greatest benefit at 31–40. Among young women, HPV16/18 declined while HPV6, HPV81, and HPV44 rose (HPV6 RR=4.24; HPV81 RR=3.17 in ≤30). Conclusions: HPV vaccination in Fuzhou reduced HPV16/18 prevalence, yet rising non-vaccine types (HPV6, HPV81) in young women suggest possible type replacement. Continued molecular surveillance, broader vaccines, and age-specific screening are warranted.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess long-term trends in Human Papillomavirus (HPV) infection rates, subtype distribution, and age-specific patterns before and after HPV vaccination in Fuzhou, China, to inform cervical cancer prevention.
Design and population
This retrospective study analyzed 29,041 women (aged 16–79) who underwent HPV genotyping at Fuzhou University Affiliated Provincial Hospital from February 2014 to December 2025. Participants were grouped into pre-vaccination (2014–2017; n=18,542) and post-vaccination (2020–2025; n=10,499).
Core methods
Age–infection probability was modeled using restricted cubic spline regression. Using fluorescent microsphere hybridization, 27 HPV subtypes (17 high-risk, 10 low-risk) were detected; overall positivity, high-/low-risk rates, and type-specific prevalence were compared.
Main results
Overall HPV positivity was 15.77% (15.65% pre vs. 15.97% post; P=0.213). A persistent U-shaped age distribution was observed in both phases, peaking at ≤30 and ≥51 years (nadir 31-40). Post-vaccination, high-risk HPV decreased (14.49%→12.90%; RR=0.89; P<0.001), notably in the 31–40 group (RR=0.79); low-risk HPV increased (5.64% to 7.40%; RR=1.31; P<0.001), especially ≤30 years (RR=2.29).
Interpretation and novelty
HPV vaccination in Fuzhou reduced HPV16/18 prevalence, yet rising non-vaccine types (HPV6, HPV81) in young women suggest possible type replacement.
Scientific and public-health significance
Continued molecular surveillance, broader vaccines, and age-specific screening are warranted to address the observed changes in HPV subtype prevalence.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is retrospective and based on a single center, which may limit generalizability. The evidence is moderate as it relies on observational data without randomized intervention.

MmuPV1 和 HPV E6 提高 IKKɑ 的核积聚,损害 NF-κB 信号传导,促进持续感染

MmuPV1 and HPV E6 Elevate Nuclear Accumulation of IKKɑ to Impair NF-κB Signaling to Promote Persistent Infection
作者： Preston T. Shepherd, Kayla R. Duxbury, Zachary Morris, James C. Romero-Masters
摘要中文翻译摘要 小鼠乳头瘤病毒 (MmuPV1) E6 癌蛋白对于持续感染和癌症发展至关重要。先前的研究发现 MmuPV1 E6 的氨基酸 R130 对于其与肿瘤抑制因子 MAML1 的相互作用至关重要。该残基 (R130A) 的 突变 会破坏 MAML1 结合并防止小鼠疾病的发展。在这项研究中,我们证明 MmuPV1 E6 还通过与 IκB 激酶 (IKK) 复合物中的蛋白质相互作用来破坏 NF-κB 信号传导。具体来说,MmuPV1 E6 R130A 突变体在结合 IKK 复合体蛋白方面存在缺陷,并且无法损害小鼠角质形成细胞中的 NF-κB 信号传导,这表明这些相互作用对于 E6 介导的该途径的破坏是必要的。此外,MmuPV1 E6 表达与 IKKɑ 核定位增加相关,而表达 E6 R130A 突变体的细胞则没有表现出这种增加。这些发现表明 E6 介导的 IKKɑ 核定位取决于其与 IKK 复合蛋白的相互作用。重要的是,我们证明 人乳头瘤病毒 HPV16 E6 癌蛋白同样会损害 NF-κB 信号传导并增加人类角质形成细胞中 IKKɑ 的核定位,这表明小鼠和 人乳头瘤病毒 之间存在共享机制。此外,对先前发表的 RNA 测序数据的分析表明,MmuPV1 E6 会诱导与核 IKKɑ 增加相关的几种转录变化。总之,这些发现表明 MmuPV1 和 HPV16 E6 都会破坏 NF-κB 信号传导,并与核 IKKɑ 定位增加相关。这项研究提供了第一个证据,证明 E6 癌蛋白与 IKKɑ 相互作用并改变 IKKɑ 的亚细胞定位,从而破坏细胞稳态。重要性在这项研究中,我们证明小鼠乳头瘤病毒 (MmuPV1) E6 癌蛋白与 κB 激酶 (IKK) 复合物抑制剂的蛋白质相互作用,从而损害 NF-κB 信号传导,这是参与先天 免疫应答 等调节过程的关键细胞途径。我们发现 MmuPV1 E6 R130A 突变体表现出与 IKK 复合蛋白的结合减少以及损害 NF-κB 信号传导的能力减弱,突出了这些相互作用的重要性。值得注意的是,我们证明 HPV16 E6 同样会损害人类角质形成细胞中的 NF-κB 信号传导,揭示小鼠和 人乳头瘤病毒 E6 蛋白之间共有的生物学功能。此外,MmuPV1 和 HPV16 E6 都会促进 IKKɑ 的核定位增加,这可能导致 NF-κB 信号传导中断。对先前发表的 RNA 测序数据的分析也揭示了与核 IKKɑ 增加相关的转录变化。总之,这些发现确定了 E6 和 IKKɑ 之间以前未被识别的相互作用,这种相互作用可能会破坏细胞稳态。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在调查 MmuPV1 E6 在破坏 NF-κB 信号传导(免疫调节的关键途径)中的作用,以了解其对持续感染和癌症发展的贡献。
设计与对象
该研究涉及对先前发表的 RNA 测序数据的分析以及对表达 MmuPV1 E6 及其 R130A 突变体的小鼠和人类角质形成细胞的实验,重点关注这些蛋白质对 NF-κB 信号传导的影响。
核心方法
研究人员分析了之前发表的RNA测序数据,并进行了实验来评估MmuPV1 E6与IKK复合蛋白的相互作用及其对角质形成细胞中IKKɑ核定位的影响。
主要结果
MmuPV1 E6 表达与 IKKɑ 核定位增加相关,而 R130A 突变体未能显示出这种增加。此外,HPV16 E6 还促进人类角质形成细胞中 IKKɑ 核定位的增加。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,E6 介导的 NF-κB 信号传导破坏取决于其与 IKK 复合蛋白的相互作用,并且这种机制在小鼠和 人乳头瘤病毒 之间可能是保守的。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果提供了对 HPV E6 蛋白破坏免疫途径的分子机制的见解,这可以为未来关于病毒持久性和肿瘤发生的研究提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于之前发表的 RNA 测序数据,不包括转录变化的直接实验验证,将证据的强度限制为中等。

MmuPV1 and HPV E6 Elevate Nuclear Accumulation of IKKɑ to Impair NF-κB Signaling to Promote Persistent Infection

Authors: Preston T. Shepherd, Kayla R. Duxbury, Zachary Morris, James C. Romero-Masters
Original abstract (English)
Abstract The mouse papillomavirus (MmuPV1) E6 oncoprotein is essential for persistent infection and cancer development. Previous studies identified amino acid R130 of MmuPV1 E6 as critical for its interaction with the tumor suppressor MAML1. Mutation of this residue (R130A) disrupts MAML1 binding and prevents disease development in mice. In this study, we demonstrate that MmuPV1 E6 also disrupts NF-κB signaling through interactions with proteins in the IκB kinase (IKK) complex. Specifically, the MmuPV1 E6 R130A mutant is defective in binding IKK complex proteins and fails to impair NF-κB signaling in mouse keratinocytes, suggesting that these interactions are necessary for E6-mediated disruption of this pathway. Furthermore, MmuPV1 E6 expression is associated with increased nuclear localization of IKKɑ, whereas cells expressing the E6 R130A mutant exhibit no such increase. These findings suggest that E6-mediated nuclear localization of IKKɑ depends on its interaction with IKK complex proteins. Importantly, we demonstrate that the human papillomavirus HPV16 E6 oncoprotein similarly impairs NF-κB signaling and increases nuclear IKKɑ localization in human keratinocytes, suggesting a shared mechanism between mouse and human papillomaviruses. Additionally, analysis of previously published RNA sequencing data reveals that MmuPV1 E6 induces several transcriptional changes associated with increased nuclear IKKɑ. Together, these findings demonstrate that both MmuPV1 and HPV16 E6 disrupt NF-κB signaling in association with increased nuclear IKKɑ localization. This study provides the first evidence of an E6 oncoprotein interacting with and changing subcellular localization of IKKɑ to disrupt cellular homeostasis. Importance In this study, we demonstrate that the mouse papillomavirus (MmuPV1) E6 oncoprotein interacts with proteins of the inhibitor of κB kinase (IKK) complex to impair NF-κB signaling, a key cellular pathway involved in regulating processes such as the innate immune response. We find that the MmuPV1 E6 R130A mutant exhibits reduced binding to IKK complex proteins and a diminished ability to impair NF-κB signaling, highlighting the importance of these interactions. Notably, we demonstrate that HPV16 E6 similarly impairs NF-κB signaling in human keratinocytes, revealing a shared biological function between mouse and human papillomavirus E6 proteins. Furthermore, both MmuPV1 and HPV16 E6 promote increased nuclear localization of IKKɑ, which may contribute to disrupted NF-κB signaling. Analysis of previously published RNA sequencing data also reveals transcriptional changes associated with increased nuclear IKKɑ. Together, these findings identify a previously unrecognized interaction between E6 and IKKɑ that may contribute to disrupting cellular homeostasis.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate the role of MmuPV1 E6 in disrupting NF-κB signaling, a key pathway in immune regulation, to understand its contribution to persistent infection and cancer development.
Design and population
The study involved analysis of previously published RNA sequencing data and experiments with mouse and human keratinocytes expressing MmuPV1 E6 and its R130A mutant, focusing on the effects of these proteins on NF-κB signaling.
Core methods
The researchers analyzed previously published RNA sequencing data and conducted experiments to assess the interaction of MmuPV1 E6 with IKK complex proteins and its effect on nuclear localization of IKKɑ in keratinocytes.
Main results
MmuPV1 E6 expression was associated with increased nuclear localization of IKKɑ, while the R130A mutant failed to show this increase. Additionally, HPV16 E6 also promoted increased nuclear IKKɑ localization in human keratinocytes.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that E6-mediated disruption of NF-κB signaling depends on its interaction with IKK complex proteins and that this mechanism may be conserved between mouse and human papillomaviruses.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results provide insights into the molecular mechanisms by which HPV E6 proteins disrupt immune pathways, which could inform future research on viral persistence and oncogenesis.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on previously published RNA sequencing data and does not include direct experimental validation of the transcriptional changes, limiting the strength of the evidence to moderate.

用于在多米尼加艾滋病毒感染者中进行 宫颈癌 筛查的移动决策支持工具的预实施因素:定性研究。

Preimplementation Factors for a Mobile Decision Support Tool for Cervical Cancer Screening Among Dominican Women Living With HIV: Qualitative Study.
作者： Lauren Gerchow, Pamela Baez, Katherine B Gartrell Rodriguez, Mariana Garza Zuazua, Fabiana Cristina Dos Santos, Maeve Brin, Louise Kuhn, Eunice Sánchez, Anthony García, Yazmina Espiritusanto Castro
摘要中文翻译摘要:感染艾滋病毒 (WLH) 的女性患 宫颈癌 的风险增加六倍。在多米尼加共和国,宫颈癌 和艾滋病毒仍然流行,但筛查率较低。世界卫生组织建议对 WLH 采取当日筛查、分诊和 治疗 方案,但实施面临持续的障碍。这项研究探讨了实施 iSTAR 的障碍和促进因素,iSTAR 是一种移动临床决策支持工具,旨在通过快速 人乳头瘤病毒 DNA 测试和结果解释来指导提供者完成筛查、分类和 治疗 协议。我们在健康公平实施框架的指导下,对 16 家提供者和 25 家 WLH 的形成性访谈和可用性测试的成绩单进行了二次定性分析。在临床实践中,社会、诊所层面、患者和提供者因素相互交叉影响实施,这表明无论是协议的采用还是技术本身都无法维持实施。研究结果支持成功的初步和持续实施的多层次战略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解在感染艾滋病毒的多米尼加女性中实施 宫颈癌 筛查移动决策支持工具的障碍和促进因素,因为该人群中 宫颈癌 的风险很高。
设计与对象
该研究对多米尼加共和国 16 名服务提供者和 25 名艾滋病毒感染者的形成性访谈和可用性测试的成绩单进行了二次定性分析。
核心方法
该分析以健康公平实施框架为指导,重点关注临床接触过程中交叉的社会文化、诊所层面、患者和提供者因素。
主要结果
研究结果表明,多层次策略对于成功地初步和持续实施筛查、分类和 治疗 方案是必要的。
解释与创新
该研究强调了解决社会、诊所层面、患者和提供者因素的重要性,以确保移动决策支持工具的成功实施。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,需要采取综合方法来提高艾滋病毒感染女性的 宫颈癌 筛查率,强调需要采取多层次策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括分析的次要性质以及定性数据可能存在偏差,但证据表明研究结果具有很高的可信度。

Preimplementation Factors for a Mobile Decision Support Tool for Cervical Cancer Screening Among Dominican Women Living With HIV: Qualitative Study.

Authors: Lauren Gerchow, Pamela Baez, Katherine B Gartrell Rodriguez, Mariana Garza Zuazua, Fabiana Cristina Dos Santos, Maeve Brin, Louise Kuhn, Eunice Sánchez, Anthony García, Yazmina Espiritusanto Castro
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT: Women living with HIV (WLH) have a sixfold greater risk of cervical cancer. In the Dominican Republic, cervical cancer and HIV remain prevalent, yet screening rates are low. The World Health Organization recommends a same-day screening, triage, and treatment protocol for WLH, but implementation faces persistent barriers. This study examined barriers and facilitators to implementing iSTAR, a mobile clinical decision support tool designed to guide providers through the screening, triage, and treatment protocol through rapid human papillomavirus DNA testing and results interpretation. We conducted a secondary qualitative analysis guided by the Health Equity Implementation Framework of transcripts from 16 providers and 25 WLH across formative interviews and usability testing. Societal, clinic-level, patient, and provider factors intersected during clinical encounters to shape implementation, indicating that neither protocol adoption nor technology alone will sustain implementation. Findings support multilevel strategies for successful initial and sustained implementation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand barriers and facilitators to implementing a mobile decision support tool for cervical cancer screening among Dominican women living with HIV, given the high risk of cervical cancer in this population.
Design and population
The study involved a secondary qualitative analysis of transcripts from 16 providers and 25 women living with HIV across formative interviews and usability testing in the Dominican Republic.
Core methods
The analysis was guided by the Health Equity Implementation Framework and focused on sociocultural, clinic-level, patient, and provider factors that intersected during clinical encounters.
Main results
Findings indicated that multilevel strategies are necessary for successful initial and sustained implementation of the screening, triage, and treatment protocol.
Interpretation and novelty
The study highlighted the importance of addressing societal, clinic-level, patient, and provider factors to ensure the successful implementation of the mobile decision support tool.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that a comprehensive approach is needed to improve cervical cancer screening rates among women living with HIV, emphasizing the need for multilevel strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include the secondary nature of the analysis and the potential for bias in the qualitative data, but the evidence suggests a high level of confidence in the findings.

长效 IL-7 和 PD-1 阻断的新辅助 HPV 疫苗接种 后,瘤内 CD8+ T 细胞分化与持久的 HPV 特异性免疫相关

Intratumoral CD8+ T-cell differentiation is linked to durable HPV-specific immunity after neoadjuvant HPV vaccination with long-acting IL-7 and PD-1 blockade
作者： Hye Ryun Kim, Chang Gon Kim, Wonrak Son, Yeju Kim, Nam Suk Sim, Dahee Kim, Sun Och Yoon, Chang Geol Lee, Kyung Hwan Kim, Min Hee Hong
摘要中文翻译摘要 持久的抗原特异性免疫是 疫苗接种 治疗的目标,但其与瘤内 CD8+ T 细胞分化的关系仍不清楚。为了解决这个问题,我们在 HPV 阳性荷瘤小鼠中比较了单独和联合使用的抗 PD-1、tirvalimogene teraplasmid (GX-188E) 和长效 IL-7 (GX-I7/NT-I7)。在测试的方案中,三重免疫疗法产生了最大的肿瘤控制和丰富的干性相关瘤内 CD8+ 状态的汇总肿瘤谱。然后,我们在一项 4-队列研究 2 期新辅助研究中评估了相同的成分,该研究纳入了 56 名可切除 HPV 阳性头颈鳞状细胞癌患者。在接受派姆单抗、GX-188E 和 NT-I7 治疗的 11 名患者 队列研究 中,7 名患者出现主要病理缓解,其中 4 名患者出现病理完全缓解。为了定义伴随该活动的肿瘤内免疫程序,配对肿瘤分析鉴定了具有细胞毒性和记忆相关特征的 GZMK+ 效应记忆 CD8+ 状态。GZMK+效应记忆细胞在治疗后肿瘤和肿瘤引流淋巴结之间共享克隆型的T细胞中最为突出,这与间室再循环一致。在两种含有 NT-I7 的小鼠中,较高的 tdLN Myc 表达促进了对人类 GZMK+ 状态的调节分析,揭示了 治疗 相关的较高 MYC 和较低 BATF 调节子特异性;计算机模拟 IL7R 扰动与 IL-7 相关重编程模型一致。治疗 后肿瘤内 MYC 相关和相对 MYC/BATF 调节子测量与 2 年时 HPV16 E6/E7 反应性激活诱导的标志物反应相关;在 8 名患有配对肿瘤的患者中,治疗 相关的相对 MYC/BATF 变化分别与 2 年肽反应性 CD8+ 干细胞样记忆表型相关。总之,这些发现将瘤内 GZMK+ 效应记忆重塑与 2 年后的 HPV 反应性激活和记忆表型联系起来。

问题与背景
持久的抗原特异性免疫是 疫苗接种 治疗的目标,但其与瘤内 CD8+ T 细胞分化的关系仍不清楚。
设计与对象
我们在 HPV 阳性荷瘤小鼠中比较了单独和联合使用的抗 PD-1、tirvalimogene teraplasmid (GX-188E) 和长效 IL-7 (GX-I7/NT-I7)。然后,我们在一项 4-队列研究 2 期新辅助研究中评估了相同的成分,该研究纳入了 56 名可切除 HPV 阳性头颈鳞状细胞癌患者。
核心方法
在测试的方案中,三重免疫疗法产生了最大的肿瘤控制和丰富的干性相关瘤内 CD8+ 状态的汇总肿瘤谱。治疗 后肿瘤内 MYC 相关和相对 MYC/BATF 调节子测量与 2 年时 HPV16 E6/E7 反应性激活诱导的标志物反应相关。

科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能有助于制定治疗策略,通过调节 CD8+ T 细胞分化和功能来增强持久的 HPV 特异性免疫。

该研究仅限于样本量较小的 2 期新辅助试验,证据仅基于摘要数据,可能无法完全体现 免疫应答 的复杂性。证据强度中等。

Intratumoral CD8+ T-cell differentiation is linked to durable HPV-specific immunity after neoadjuvant HPV vaccination with long-acting IL-7 and PD-1 blockade

Authors: Hye Ryun Kim, Chang Gon Kim, Wonrak Son, Yeju Kim, Nam Suk Sim, Dahee Kim, Sun Och Yoon, Chang Geol Lee, Kyung Hwan Kim, Min Hee Hong
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Durable antigen-specific immunity is a goal of therapeutic vaccination, but its relationship to intratumoral CD8+ T-cell differentiation remains poorly defined. To address this question, we compared anti–PD-1, tirvalimogene teraplasmid (GX-188E), and long-acting IL-7 (GX-I7/NT-I7), alone and in combination, in HPV-positive tumor-bearing mice. Among tested regimens, triple immunotherapy produced the greatest tumor control and a pooled tumor profile enriched for stemness-associated intratumoral CD8+ states. We then evaluated the same components in a four-cohort phase 2 neoadjuvant study of 56 patients with resectable HPV-positive head and neck squamous cell carcinoma. In the 11-patient cohort receiving pembrolizumab, GX-188E, and NT-I7, 7 patients had a major pathologic response, including 4 with a pathologic complete response. To define the intratumoral immune program accompanying this activity, paired tumor profiling identified a GZMK+ effector-memory CD8+ state with cytotoxicity- and memory-associated features. GZMK+ effector-memory cells were prominent among T cells bearing clonotypes shared between post-treatment tumors and tumor-draining lymph nodes, consistent with intercompartmental recirculation. Higher tdLN Myc expression in both NT-I7-containing mouse profiles prompted regulatory analysis of the human GZMK+ state, revealing treatment-associated higher MYC and lower BATF regulon specificity; in silico IL7R perturbation was consistent with an IL-7-associated reprogramming model. Post-treatment intratumoral MYC-associated and relative MYC/BATF regulon measures were related to HPV16 E6/E7-reactive activation-induced marker responses at 2 years; in eight patients with paired tumors, treatment-associated relative MYC/BATF changes were separately related to a 2-year peptide-responsive CD8+ stem cell–like memory phenotype. Together, these findings associate intratumoral GZMK+ effector-memory remodeling with HPV-reactive activation and memory phenotypes 2 years later.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Durable antigen-specific immunity is a goal of therapeutic vaccination, but its relationship to intratumoral CD8+ T-cell differentiation remains poorly defined.
Design and population
We compared anti–PD-1, tirvalimogene teraplasmid (GX-188E), and long-acting IL-7 (GX-I7/NT-I7), alone and in combination, in HPV-positive tumor-bearing mice. We then evaluated the same components in a four-cohort phase 2 neoadjuvant study of 56 patients with resectable HPV-positive head and neck squamous cell carcinoma.
Core methods
Among tested regimens, triple immunotherapy produced the greatest tumor control and a pooled tumor profile enriched for stemness-associated intratumoral CD8+ states. Post-treatment intratumoral MYC-associated and relative MYC/BATF regulon measures were related to HPV16 E6/E7-reactive activation-induced marker responses at 2 years.
Main results
In the 11-patient cohort receiving pembrolizumab, GX-188E, and NT-I7, 7 patients had a major pathologic response, including 4 with a pathologic complete response. Paired tumor profiling identified a GZMK+ effector-memory CD8+ state with cytotoxicity- and memory-associated features.
Interpretation and novelty
These findings associate intratumoral GZMK+ effector-memory remodeling with HPV-reactive activation and memory phenotypes 2 years later.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform the development of therapeutic strategies that enhance durable HPV-specific immunity through modulation of CD8+ T-cell differentiation and function.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a phase 2 neoadjuvant trial with a small sample size, and the evidence is based on abstract-only data, which may not fully capture the complexity of immune responses. The evidence strength is moderate.

头颈癌中人乳头瘤病毒16型基因组变异:一项系统综述及结构与功能注释

Human papillomavirus 16 genomic variation in head and neck cancer: a systematic review with structural and functional annotation
作者： Ngoc Ha Tran, Huy Gia Nguyen, Dayna Sais, Nicole Lima, Alison Todd, Tuan Van Nguyen, Michael Elliott, Tuấn Nguyễn
摘要中文翻译人乳头瘤病毒16型(HPV16)阳性头颈癌(HNC)通常与HPV阴性疾病的更佳预后相关。然而,新兴证据表明,特定的HPV16基因组变异可能与较差的临床结果相关。因此,我们对在头颈癌中报道的HPV16单核苷酸多态性(SNPs)进行了系统综述,并将选定的变异映射到病毒基因组和蛋白质区域,以评估其潜在相关性。我们搜索了四个主要数据库(PubMed、Embase、Scopus和Web of Science),寻找有关HPV16 SNPs在头颈癌中报道的研究。大多数研究集中在E6或E7上,而只有少数研究分析了整个HPV16序列。这些SNPs被提取并映射到HPV16 基因组,同时使用UniProt和RCSB蛋白质数据库评估其结构和功能特征。分析了14项研究,识别出E6中有30个SNPs,E7中有7个SNPs。结构映射表明,核心HPV16致癌性基序保持高度保守,而反复出现的变异往往发生在突变允许的区域,例如E6的跨结构域连接区。仅有一项研究报告了HPV16变异与上游调控区中较差患者生存率之间的关联。然而,在确立任何临床相关性之前,这些发现需要独立验证。对头颈癌中HPV16 SNPs的全面映射为理解这些SNPs如何与疾病进展和患者预后相关提供了重要基础。尽管许多识别出的SNPs位于重要的HPV16区域,但它们与疾病进展的关联仍不清楚。需要进一步研究以确定哪些变异与患者预后相关。对HPV16基因组变异及其相关性的更全面理解,可能为所有HPV16相关癌症提供更广泛的临床见解。

范围与问题
然而,新兴证据表明,特定的HPV16基因组变异可能与较差的临床结果相关。

这些SNPs被提取并映射到HPV16 基因组,同时使用UniProt和RCSB蛋白质数据库评估其结构和功能特征。
共识与结论
对头颈癌中HPV16 SNPs的全面映射为理解这些SNPs如何与疾病进展和患者预后相关提供了重要基础。
争议与缺口
尽管许多识别出的SNPs位于重要的HPV16区域,但它们与疾病进展的关联仍不清楚。

需要进一步研究以确定哪些变异与患者预后相关。

Human papillomavirus 16 genomic variation in head and neck cancer: a systematic review with structural and functional annotation

Authors: Ngoc Ha Tran, Huy Gia Nguyen, Dayna Sais, Nicole Lima, Alison Todd, Tuan Van Nguyen, Michael Elliott, Tuấn Nguyễn
Original abstract (English)
Human papillomavirus type 16 (HPV16)-positive head and neck cancer (HNC) is generally associated with a more favourable prognosis than HPV-negative disease. However, emerging evidence suggests that specific HPV16 genomic variants may be associated with poorer clinical outcomes. We therefore conducted a systematic review of HPV16 single nucleotide polymorphisms (SNPs) reported in HNC and mapped selected variants to viral genomic and protein regions to assess their potential relevance. We searched four major databases (PubMed, Embase, Scopus, and Web of Science) for studies reporting HPV16 SNPs in HNC. Most studies focused on either E6 or E7, while only a few studies examined the entire HPV16 sequence. These SNPs were extracted and mapped to the HPV16 genome, while structural and functional characteristics were assessed using UniProt and the RCSB Protein Data Bank. Fourteen studies were analysed, identifying 30 SNPs in E6 and seven in E7. Structural mapping suggested that core HPV16 oncogenic motifs remain highly conserved, while recurrent variants tend to occur in mutationally permissive regions, such as the E6 interdomain linker. Only one study reported associations between HPV16 variants and poorer patient survival within the upstream regulatory region. However, these findings require independent validation before any clinical relevance can be established. The comprehensive mapping of HPV16 SNPs in HNC provides an important grounding for understanding how these SNPs may be associated with disease progression and patient outcomes. Although many of the identified SNPs are within important HPV16 regions, their associations with disease progression remain unclear. Further studies are required to determine which variants are associated with patient outcomes. A more comprehensive understanding of HPV16 genomic variation and its relevance could provide broader clinical insights across HPV16-associated cancers.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
However, emerging evidence suggests that specific HPV16 genomic variants may be associated with poorer clinical outcomes.
Evidence base and method
These SNPs were extracted and mapped to the HPV16 genome, while structural and functional characteristics were assessed using UniProt and the RCSB Protein Data Bank.
Consensus and conclusions
The comprehensive mapping of HPV16 SNPs in HNC provides an important grounding for understanding how these SNPs may be associated with disease progression and patient outcomes.
Controversies and gaps
Although many of the identified SNPs are within important HPV16 regions, their associations with disease progression remain unclear.
Research and practice implications
Further studies are required to determine which variants are associated with patient outcomes.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 综述A high priorityAcademic literature · Review

跨性别双性女性中人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)感染率及高危型HPV感染率:系统综述和荟萃分析的方案

Prevalence of human papillomavirus and HPV vaccination among cisgender bisexual women: Protocol for a systematic review and meta-analysis
作者： Kendall A. Lawley, Karthikeya Kusuma, Stephanie Ann Farquhar, Steven Michael Goodreau, Rachel L. Winer
摘要中文翻译人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)是美国最常见的性传播感染,也是宫颈癌的主要病因。尽管通过HPV疫苗接种可以有效预防宫颈癌,但疫苗接种覆盖率仍不理想。目前关于跨性别双性女性中HPV感染率和高危型HPV感染率的研究有限且不一致。这可能部分归因于对双性取向的定义存在差异。双性取向可能基于性取向身份(例如自我认同为双性)或性行为(例如与多个性别发生性接触)进行操作化定义,而这些不同的定义可能涵盖不同的群体,并导致不同的健康结果。本文提出了一项系统综述和荟萃分析的方案,旨在探讨跨性别双性女性中报告的HPV感染率和高危型HPV感染率是否因双性取向是基于身份还是行为而有所不同。该方案已在PROSPERO(CRD420251252015)注册,并遵循PRISMA-P指南。采用随机效应荟萃分析来估计不同定义类型下的HPV感染率和高危型HPV感染率。研究结果将阐明操作化定义如何影响观察到的HPV相关结果,并为跨性别双性女性提供针对性的宫颈癌预防策略。
查看综述五要素

证据基础与方法
该方案已在PROSPERO(CRD420251252015)注册,并遵循PRISMA-P指南。

研究结果将阐明操作化定义如何影响观察到的HPV相关结果,并为跨性别双性女性提供针对性的宫颈癌预防策略。

Prevalence of human papillomavirus and HPV vaccination among cisgender bisexual women: Protocol for a systematic review and meta-analysis

Authors: Kendall A. Lawley, Karthikeya Kusuma, Stephanie Ann Farquhar, Steven Michael Goodreau, Rachel L. Winer
Original abstract (English)
Human papillomavirus (HPV) is the most common sexually transmitted infection in the United States, and a primary cause of cervical cancer. Although cervical cancer is largely preventable through HPV vaccination, vaccine coverage remains suboptimal. Research on HPV prevalence and vaccination among cisgender bisexual women is limited and inconsistent. This may be partly due to variation in how bisexuality is defined. Bisexuality may be operationalized based on sexual identity (e.g., self-identification as bisexual) or sexual behavior (e.g., sexual contact with more than one sex or gender), and these differing definitions may capture distinct populations and yield divergent health findings. This paper proposes a protocol for a systematic review and meta-analysis to examine whether reported HPV prevalence and vaccination rates among cisgender bisexual women differ based on whether bisexuality is defined by identity or behavior. The protocol is registered in PROSPERO (CRD420251252015) and follows PRISMA-P guidelines. Random-effects meta-analyses will estimate pooled HPV prevalence and vaccination rates by definition type. Findings will clarify how operational definitions shape observed HPV-related outcomes and inform tailored cervical cancer prevention strategies for cisgender bisexual women.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This paper proposes a protocol for a systematic review and meta-analysis to examine whether reported HPV prevalence and vaccination rates among cisgender bisexual women differ based on whether bisexuality is defined by identity or behavior.
Evidence base and method
The protocol is registered in PROSPERO (CRD420251252015) and follows PRISMA-P guidelines.
Consensus and conclusions
Findings will clarify how operational definitions shape observed HPV-related outcomes and inform tailored cervical cancer prevention strategies for cisgender bisexual women.
Controversies and gaps
Research on HPV prevalence and vaccination among cisgender bisexual women is limited and inconsistent.
Research and practice implications
Random-effects meta-analyses will estimate pooled HPV prevalence and vaccination rates by definition type.

病变部位与宫颈上皮内瘤变之间的关联。

Association between lesion location and cervical intraepithelial neoplasia.
作者： Yavuz ÖM, Erkan Şimşek, Taşkum İ, Selcan Sınacı, Ferhat Aslan, Ömer Mert Yavuz, İbrahim Taşkum, Ibrahim Taşkum, Yavuz Ö
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
摘要目的:本研究的目的是确定接受阴道镜检查的高危 人乳头瘤病毒 阳性女性宫颈病变的钟面分布,并评估病变定位与高级别宫颈病变(CIN2+;宫颈上皮内瘤变 2 级或以上)之间的关联。
证据基础与方法
这项单中心回顾性观察研究纳入了 298 名在 2024 年 1 月至 2026 年 3 月期间因高风险 人乳头瘤病毒 阳性而接受阴道镜检查的女性。
共识与结论
颈前定位与高度宫颈病变 (CIN2+) 独立相关,这表明在阴道镜检查期间仔细评估和记录病变位置可能会提高临床显着病变的识别。

Association between lesion location and cervical intraepithelial neoplasia.

Authors: Yavuz ÖM, Erkan Şimşek, Taşkum İ, Selcan Sınacı, Ferhat Aslan, Ömer Mert Yavuz, İbrahim Taşkum, Ibrahim Taşkum, Yavuz Ö
Original abstract (English)
SUMMARY OBJECTIVE: The aim of this study was to determine the clock-face distribution of cervical lesions in women undergoing colposcopy for high-risk human papillomavirus positivity and to evaluate the association between lesion localization and high-grade cervical lesions (CIN2+; cervical intraepithelial neoplasia grade 2 or higher). METHODS: This single-center retrospective observational study included 298 women who underwent colposcopy because of high-risk human papillomavirus positivity between January 2024 and March 2026. Demographic characteristics, human papillomavirus genotypes, cervical cytology, colposcopic biopsy localization, and histopathological findings were retrospectively reviewed. Cervical lesions were classified according to the clock-face system and grouped into anterior, right lateral, posterior, and left lateral regions. The primary outcome was the presence of CIN2+. RESULTS: The mean age was 42.5±9.2 years, and CIN2+ lesions were identified in 54 women (18.1%). Lesions were most frequently located at the 12 and 6 o'clock positions. Compared with the normal/CIN1 group, patients with CIN2+ had a higher frequency of abnormal cytology (48.1 vs. 23.8%; p=0.001) and anterior cervical localization (88.9 vs. In multivariable logistic regression analysis, abnormal cytology (adjusted OR 2.84; 95%CI 1.43–5.63; p=0.003) and anterior cervical localization (adjusted OR 3.67; 95%CI 1.46–9.21; p=0.006) were independently associated with CIN2+. CONCLUSION: Cervical lesions in high-risk human papillomavirus-positive women showed a nonuniform clock-face distribution. Anterior cervical localization was independently associated with high-grade cervical lesions (CIN2+), suggesting that careful assessment and documentation of lesion location during colposcopy may improve the identification of clinically significant lesions.

Evidence base and method

Controversies and gaps
The supplied evidence does not clearly state controversies or evidence gaps.
Research and practice implications

牙医作为哨兵:HPV 相关口咽癌、口腔检查的局限性、疫苗 倡导和美国男性的诊断延迟——从国家利益角度进行综合叙述和范围审查

The dentist as sentinel: HPV-associated oropharyngeal cancer, the limits of the oral examination, vaccine advocacy and diagnostic delay in United States men — an integrated narrative and scoping review with a National Interest perspective
作者： Adrielen Henrique Picoli, Henrique Schultz Lacerda Guimarães, Gissele Lima Ribeiro, Mara Leticia Basulto Deniz, Caroline Weinert Marçal, Rosemberg de Oliveira Costa, Sara Beatriz Cassini Michelon, Pedro Augusto Dias Lima, Mara Leticia Basulto Diniz
摘要中文翻译由 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 引起的口咽癌已成为美国最常见的 HPV 相关癌症,其负担不成比例地落在男性身上。由于牙医会定期检查大部分人口的口腔和咽部,因此牙科就诊对于早期识别和 疫苗 推广来说是一个战略性但未得到充分利用的接触点。这篇综合综述结合了 流行病学、HPV 相关口咽癌延迟的 流行病学、临床表现、诊断途径和延迟驱动因素的叙述性综合,以及牙科专业人员有关 HPV 疫苗接种 的知识、态度和实践的研究范围图,以及允许在牙科环境中推广或管理 疫苗 的政策举措。截至 2026 年 10 月,对 PubMed 索引文献和美国官方来源(CDC、NCHS、美国癌症协会、FDA、HHS 和州立法机构)进行了检查。2001 年至 2017 年间,男性中的 发病率 每年增加 2.7%,区域阶段疾病占主导地位,男性口腔 HPV 发病率 携带率为 11.5%,而女性为 3.2%。原发肿瘤在常规检查中通常是看不见的,无痛性颈部肿块是最常见的首发症状。最新的青少年HPV 疫苗接种在2025年达到63.4%,连续四年没有改善,而86.9%的儿童和66.7%的成人报告在过去一年曾去看牙医。不到三分之一的受访牙医讨论了 疫苗,但超过一半的人表示,如果允许,他们会实施该治疗。提出了筛查、转诊和 疫苗 倡导的哨兵框架,以及牙科劳动力的教育优先事项,并阐述了该问题的国家利益。讨论了该研究的局限性以及对未来研究的建议。

The dentist as sentinel: HPV-associated oropharyngeal cancer, the limits of the oral examination, vaccine advocacy and diagnostic delay in United States men — an integrated narrative and scoping review with a National Interest perspective

Authors: Adrielen Henrique Picoli, Henrique Schultz Lacerda Guimarães, Gissele Lima Ribeiro, Mara Leticia Basulto Deniz, Caroline Weinert Marçal, Rosemberg de Oliveira Costa, Sara Beatriz Cassini Michelon, Pedro Augusto Dias Lima, Mara Leticia Basulto Diniz
Original abstract (English)
Oropharyngeal cancer caused by human papillomavirus (HPV) has become the most common HPV-attributable cancer in the United States, and its burden falls disproportionately on men. Because dentists examine the mouth and pharynx of a large share of the population at regular intervals, the dental visit is a strategic but underused point of contact for both early recognition and vaccine promotion. This integrated review combines a narrative synthesis of the epidemiology, clinical presentation, diagnostic pathway and drivers of delay of HPV-associated oropharyngeal cancer with a scoping map of studies on dental professionals' knowledge, attitudes and practices regarding HPV vaccination and of policy initiatives that allow vaccine promotion or administration in dental settings. PubMed-indexed literature and official United States sources (CDC, NCHS, American Cancer Society, FDA, HHS and state legislatures) were examined up to October 2026. Incidence among men rose 2.7% per year between 2001 and 2017, regional-stage disease predominated, and men carry an oral HPV prevalence of 11.5% against 3.2% in women. The primary tumour is frequently invisible on routine inspection, and a painless neck mass is the most common first sign. Up-to-date adolescent HPV vaccination reached 63.4% in 2025, without improvement for four consecutive years, while 86.9% of children and 66.7% of adults report a dental visit in the past year. Fewer than one-third of surveyed dentists discuss the vaccine, yet more than half say they would administer it if allowed. A sentinel framework for screening, referral and vaccine advocacy is proposed, together with education priorities for the dental workforce, and the national interest of the problem is set out. The study's limitations and suggestions for future research are discussed.
View review five-element analysis

Evidence base and method

This integrated review combines a narrative synthesis of the epidemiology, clinical presentation, diagnostic pathway and drivers of delay of HPV-associated oropharyngeal cancer with a scoping map of studies on dental professionals' knowledge, attitudes and practices regarding HPV vaccination and of policy initiatives that allow vaccine promotion or administration in dental settings.
Consensus and conclusions
Incidence among men rose 2.7% per year between 2001 and 2017, regional-stage disease predominated, and men carry an oral HPV prevalence of 11.5% against 3.2% in women. The primary tumour is frequently invisible on routine inspection, and a painless neck mass is the most common first sign.
Controversies and gaps
Fewer than one-third of surveyed dentists discuss the vaccine, yet more than half say they would administer it if allowed. Up-to-date adolescent HPV vaccination reached 63.4% in 2025, without improvement for four consecutive years, while 86.9% of children and 66.7% of adults report a dental visit in the past year.
Research and practice implications
A sentinel framework for screening, referral and vaccine advocacy is proposed, together with education priorities for the dental workforce, and the national interest of the problem is set out. The study's limitations and suggestions for future research are discussed.

基于 HPV 筛查时代导致 诊断 或 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 独立宫颈恶性肿瘤延迟的诊断陷阱:诊断途径的临床病理学分析

Diagnostic pitfalls leading to delayed diagnosis of human papillomavirus (HPV)–independent cervical malignancies in the era of HPV-based screening: clinicopathologic analysis of diagnostic pathways
作者： Hye Eun Park, Hye-Ra Jung, Hyun-Jung Kim
摘要中文翻译背景:人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 独立宫颈恶性肿瘤 (HPV-IDCM) 是一种罕见但具有临床重要意义的亚型,可能会逃避基于 HPV 的筛查的检测。本研究调查了导致 HPV-IDCM 诊断 延迟的诊断陷阱,并评估了宫颈细胞学在诊断途径中的作用。方法:这个多中心回顾性病例系列是作为韩国细胞病理学会的政策研究项目进行的。在确定明确的组织学 诊断 之前,患有 HPV-IDCM 且重复出现异常细胞学结果的患者。对临床、细胞学、组织学和 HPV 检测结果进行审查,以确定与诊断延迟相关的因素。结果:纳入 4 名患者,中位年龄 74 岁(范围 62 至 79 岁)。组织学诊断包括三种不依赖 HPV 的胃型腺癌和一种不依赖 HPV 的鳞状细胞癌。从第一次异常细胞学发现到最终 诊断 的中位间隔为 18.5 个月(范围为 11 至 30 个月)。在诊断之前,所有患者平均接受了3.5次细胞学检查(范围为3至5次)。相关因素包括 HPV 检测结果反复呈阴性 (3/4)、非诊断性活检或锥切术 (2/4)、尽管持续细胞学异常却延迟组织评估 (1/4)、深部宫颈内膜病变 (3/4) 以及并发 HPV 相关鳞状病变 (1/4)。重新审查在所有病例中均发现了非典型或恶性细胞,表明延迟并非由于细胞学检测失败造成的。结论:在基于 HPV 的筛查时代,HPV-IDCM 仍然容易受到延迟的 诊断 的影响。持续存在的异常细胞学检查结果不应仅仅因为 HPV 检测结果呈阴性而被忽视。细胞学、临床和组织学结果的仔细关联对于及时检测 HPV-IDCM 仍然至关重要。

Diagnostic pitfalls leading to delayed diagnosis of human papillomavirus (HPV)–independent cervical malignancies in the era of HPV-based screening: clinicopathologic analysis of diagnostic pathways

Authors: Hye Eun Park, Hye-Ra Jung, Hyun-Jung Kim
Original abstract (English)
Background: Human papillomavirus (HPV)–independent cervical malignancies (HPV-IDCM) are a rare but clinically important subset that may escape detection by HPV-based screening. This study investigated diagnostic pitfalls contributing to delayed HPV-IDCM diagnosis and assessed the role of cervical cytology in the diagnostic pathway. Methods: This multicenter retrospective case series was conducted as a policy research project of the Korean Society for Cytopathology. Patients with HPV-IDCM and repeated abnormal cytologic findings before definitive histologic diagnosis were identified. Clinical, cytologic, histologic, and HPV test results were reviewed to identify factors associated with diagnostic delay. Results: Four patients were included, with a median age of 74 years (range, 62 to 79 years). Histologic diagnoses comprised three HPV-independent gastric-type adenocarcinomas and one HPV-independent squamous cell carcinoma. The median interval from the first abnormal cytologic finding to definitive diagnosis was 18.5 months (range, 11 to 30 months). Before diagnosis, all patients underwent a median of 3.5 cytologic examinations (range, 3 to 5). Associated factors included repeatedly negative HPV test results (3/4), nondiagnostic biopsy or conization (2/4), delayed tissue evaluation despite persistent cytologic abnormalities (1/4), deeply located endocervical lesions (3/4), and a concurrent HPV-associated squamous lesion (1/4). Re-review identified atypical or malignant cells in all cases, indicating that the delay did not result from failure of cytologic detection. Conclusions: HPV-IDCM remain vulnerable to delayed diagnosis in the era of HPV-based screening. Persistent abnormal cytologic findings should not be dismissed solely because HPV test results are negative. Careful correlation of cytologic, clinical, and histologic findings remains essential for timely HPV-IDCM detection.
View review five-element analysis

Evidence base and method

Consensus and conclusions
Re-review identified atypical or malignant cells in all cases, indicating that the delay did not result from failure of cytologic detection.

The supplied evidence does not clearly state controversies or evidence gaps.

ASC-H 细胞学女性中的 HPV16/18 基因型和组织学重度疾病:基因型分层结果的系统评价

HPV16/18 Genotype and Histologic High-Grade Disease in Women with ASC-H Cytology: A Systematic Review of Genotype-Stratified Outcomes
作者： Maria-Daniela Vasieș (Mazilu), Elena-Lavinia Rusu, Jasmina-Sorina Chiriac, Alexandra-Corina Faur, Elena-Rodica Heredea, Veronica-Daniela Chiriac
摘要中文翻译背景/目标:人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 基因分型可以对患有非典型鳞状细胞的女性进行组织学风险分层,但不能排除高级鳞状上皮内病变 (ASC-H)。我们评估了基因型特异性的高等级结果。方法:PubMed 和 Europe PMC 于 2026 年 10 月 1 日检索,并于 2026 年 10 月 7 日更新。两名评审员根据 PRISMA 2020 独立筛选、提取和评估研究。符合条件的英语语言研究为 HPV16/18 定义组和比较组提供了 ASC-H 特异性组织学结果。使用改编的 QUADAS-2 框架评估偏倚风险(附录中报告了协议指定的 Joanna Briggs Institute 患病率 检查表),并使用 GRADE 评估确定性。一项探索性限制性荟萃分析使用随机效应和修正的 Hartung-Knapp 置信区间对 HPV16/18 与其他 高危型HPV (hrHPV) 的 CIN2+/HSIL+ 进行比较。结果:纳入 17 份报告中的 16 项研究;六家公司将 1655 名女性纳入了有限比较。合并风险比为 1.19(95% 置信区间,0.94-1.49),具有显着异质性(I2 = 64.9%),预测区间为 0.70-2.01。结果因研究选择和基因型定义而异。在对至少 30 名 hrHPV 阴性女性进行的四项研究中,6.8-18.4% 的患者获得了高级别结果,并且报告了 HPV 阴性癌症。留一法、筛查背景和验证限制分析没有显示出一致的差异(所有参与者的组织学:RR 1.01、0.63-1.62)。验证不完整、年龄和筛查背景的混淆、异质测定和人群以及难以获取的报告限制了可信度;这两种结果的确定性都非常低。结论:不能排除 HPV16/18 与其他 hrHPV 类型之间存在适度的相对差异,但汇总的证据并未证明存在一致的增量风险差异。其他 hrHPV 类型和 hrHPV 结果阴性并不能排除重度疾病。这些发现与目前对所有 ASC-H 进行阴道镜评估的指南一致,无需基于基因型的延期。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述的重点是 HPV16/18 基因型与 ASC-H 细胞学女性的组织学高级疾病之间的关联。核心问题是 HPV16/18 基因型是否可以对高级鳞状上皮内病变 (HSIL) 和宫颈上皮内瘤变 2+ (CIN2+) 的风险进行分层。
证据基础与方法
证据基础包括从 PubMed 和 Europe PMC 检索的研究,检索日期为 2026 年 10 月 1 日,更新日期为 2026 年 10 月 7 日。两名评审员使用 PRISMA 2020 独立筛选、提取和评估研究。符合条件的研究为 HPV16/18 定义组和比较组提供了 ASC-H 特异性组织学结果。使用改编的 QUADAS-2 框架评估偏倚风险,并用 GRADE 评定确定性。
共识与结论
HPV16/18与其他高危型HPV类型的高危结局合并风险比为1.19(95%置信区间为0.94–1.49),存在显著的异质性(I2 = 64.9%)和较宽的预测区间(0.70–2.01)。结果因研究选择和基因型定义而异。在四项至少包含30名高危型HPV阴性女性的研究中,高危结局的发生率为6.8–18.4%,并报告了HPV阴性癌变病例。留一法分析、筛查背景分析和验证受限分析均未显示出一致的差异。这些发现与目前对所有ASC-H进行阴道镜评估而不进行基于基因型的延迟评估的指导建议一致。
争议与缺口
证据显示出显着的异质性和广泛的预测区间,表明汇总估计的不确定性。偏倚风险受到不完整验证、年龄和筛查背景的混淆、异质测定和人群以及难以获取的报告的限制。这两种结果的确定性都非常低。
科研与实践启示
需要进一步研究来阐明 HPV16/18 与其他 hrHPV 类型之间的增量风险差异。监测 和诊断方法应考虑测定和群体的异质性。研究结果支持当前对所有 ASC-H 病例进行阴道镜评估的做法,而不依赖于基于基因型的延迟。

HPV16/18 Genotype and Histologic High-Grade Disease in Women with ASC-H Cytology: A Systematic Review of Genotype-Stratified Outcomes

Authors: Maria-Daniela Vasieș (Mazilu), Elena-Lavinia Rusu, Jasmina-Sorina Chiriac, Alexandra-Corina Faur, Elena-Rodica Heredea, Veronica-Daniela Chiriac
Original abstract (English)
Background/Objectives: Human papillomavirus (HPV) genotyping may stratify histologic risk among women with atypical squamous cells that cannot exclude high-grade squamous intraepithelial lesion (ASC-H). We evaluated genotype-specific high-grade outcomes. Methods: PubMed and Europe PMC were searched on 1 October 2026 and updated on 7 October 2026. Two reviewers independently screened, extracted, and appraised studies in accordance with PRISMA 2020. Eligible English language studies provided ASC-H-specific histologic outcomes for HPV16/18-defined and comparator groups. Risk of bias was appraised with an adapted QUADAS-2 framework (the protocol-specified Joanna Briggs Institute prevalence checklist is reported in an appendix), and certainty was rated with GRADE. An exploratory restricted meta-analysis compared HPV16/18 with other high-risk HPV (hrHPV) for CIN2+/HSIL+ using random effects and modified Hartung–Knapp confidence intervals. Results: Sixteen studies in 17 reports were included; six contributed 1655 women to the restricted comparison. The pooled risk ratio was 1.19 (95% confidence interval, 0.94–1.49), with substantial heterogeneity (I2 = 64.9%) and a prediction interval of 0.70–2.01. The results varied with study selection and genotype definition. In four studies with at least 30 hrHPV-negative women, high-grade outcomes occurred in 6.8–18.4%, and HPV-negative carcinomas were reported. Leave-one-out, screening context, and verification-restricted analyses did not show a consistent difference (histology in all participants: RR 1.01, 0.63–1.62). Incomplete verification, confounding by age and screening context, heterogeneous assays and populations, and inaccessible reports limited confidence; certainty was very low for both outcomes. Conclusions: A modest relative difference between HPV16/18 and other hrHPV types cannot be excluded, but the pooled evidence does not demonstrate a consistent incremental risk difference. Other hrHPV types and negative hrHPV results do not exclude high-grade disease. These findings are consistent with current guidance for colposcopic evaluation of all ASC-H without genotype-based deferral.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on the association between HPV16/18 genotypes and histologic high-grade disease in women with ASC-H cytology. The central question is whether HPV16/18 genotypes can stratify the risk of high-grade squamous intraepithelial lesions (HSIL) and cervical intraepithelial neoplasia 2+ (CIN2+).
Evidence base and method
The evidence base includes studies retrieved from PubMed and Europe PMC, searched on 1 October 2026 and updated on 7 October 2026. Two reviewers independently screened, extracted, and appraised studies using PRISMA 2020. Eligible studies provided ASC-H-specific histologic outcomes for HPV16/18-defined and comparator groups. Risk of bias was assessed using an adapted QUADAS-2 framework, and certainty was rated with GRADE.
Consensus and conclusions
The pooled risk ratio for high-grade outcomes in HPV16/18 versus other hrHPV types was 1.19 (95% CI, 0.94–1.49), with substantial heterogeneity (I2 = 64.9%) and a wide prediction interval (0.70–2.01). The results varied with study selection and genotype definition. In four studies with at least 30 hrHPV-negative women, high-grade outcomes occurred in 6.8–18.4%, and HPV-negative carcinomas were reported. Leave-one-out, screening context, and verification-restricted analyses did not show a consistent difference. The findings are consistent with current guidance for colposcopic evaluation of all ASC-H without genotype-based deferral.
Controversies and gaps
The evidence shows substantial heterogeneity and a wide prediction interval, indicating uncertainty in the pooled estimate. The risk of bias was limited by incomplete verification, confounding by age and screening context, heterogeneous assays and populations, and inaccessible reports. Certainty was very low for both outcomes.
Research and practice implications
Further research is needed to clarify the incremental risk difference between HPV16/18 and other hrHPV types. Surveillance and diagnostic methods should account for the heterogeneity in assays and populations. The findings support current practices of colposcopic evaluation for all ASC-H cases without relying on genotype-based deferral.

成年男性中 HPV 疫苗接种 的障碍和促进因素:系统评价和定性荟萃分析

Barriers and facilitators to HPV vaccination among adult men: A systematic review and qualitative meta-synthesis
作者： Yujie Liu, Lingling Wang, Hongxu Zhu, Jing Chen
摘要中文翻译人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染广泛存在,是可预防癌症的主要原因,但成年男性对 HPV 疫苗接种 的吸收仍然不理想。关于男性 疫苗接种 决策和医疗保健响应的多层次决定因素的定性证据仍然有限。这项定性荟萃分析旨在以社会生态模型(SEM)为指导,综合成年男性中 HPV 疫苗接种 的障碍和促进因素,并探讨如何使用以 WHO 卫生服务概念为基础的卫生服务评估框架,将 疫苗接种 相关的健康需求反映在医疗资源中,并转化为服务利用。从建立到 2025 年 12 月,使用受控词汇和自由文本术语对包括 PubMed、Embase 和 PsycINFO 在内的 10 个电子数据库进行了检索。包括四个国家的十项研究。证据基础集中在性少数男性、性工作者和从事医疗保健的男性中,在一般男性人群中的研究较少;这十项研究中有六项是在美国进行的,限制了更广泛地适用于成年男性。主题综合遵循 Thomas 和 Harden 的方法,将研究结果组织到五个 SEM 级别。主要障碍包括对 HPV 的性别认知、低感知易感性、耻辱和披露问题、提供者沟通失败、经济限制和结构性获取挑战。促进因素包括提供者推荐、支持性关系、综合护理途径和健康信息获取。卫生服务评估确定了需求、资源和利用之间的差距,特别是外部预防性护理。这些发现强调,改善成年男性中的 HPV 疫苗接种 需要多层次的行为策略以及医疗保健系统的改进,以更好地识别和响应男性的 疫苗接种 需求。

共识与结论
主要障碍包括对 HPV 的性别认知、低感知易感性、耻辱和披露问题、提供者沟通失败、经济限制和结构性获取挑战。促进因素包括提供者推荐、支持性关系、综合护理途径和健康信息获取。

科研与实践启示
这些发现强调,改善成年男性中的 HPV 疫苗接种 需要多层次的行为策略以及医疗保健系统的改进,以更好地识别和响应男性的 疫苗接种 需求。

Barriers and facilitators to HPV vaccination among adult men: A systematic review and qualitative meta-synthesis

Authors: Yujie Liu, Lingling Wang, Hongxu Zhu, Jing Chen
Original abstract (English)
Human papillomavirus (HPV) infection is widespread and a leading cause of preventable cancers, yet HPV vaccination uptake among adult men remains suboptimal. Qualitative evidence on multilevel determinants of men's vaccination decisions and healthcare responses remains limited. This qualitative meta-synthesis aimed to synthesize barriers and facilitators to HPV vaccination among adult men guided by the Social Ecological Model (SEM), and to explore how vaccination-related health needs are reflected in healthcare resources and translated into service utilization using a health service evaluation framework informed by WHO health service concepts. Ten electronic databases, including PubMed, Embase, and PsycINFO, were searched from inception to December 2025 using controlled vocabulary and free-text terms. Ten studies across four countries were included. The evidence base was concentrated among sexual minority men, sex workers, and healthcare-engaged men, with fewer studies among general male populations; six of the ten studies were conducted in the United States, limiting generalizability to adult men more broadly. Thematic synthesis followed Thomas and Harden's approach, with findings organized across five SEM levels. Key barriers included gendered perceptions of HPV, low perceived susceptibility, stigma and disclosure concerns, provider communication failures, economic constraints, and structural access challenges. Facilitators included provider recommendation, supportive relationships, integrated care pathways, and health information access. Health service evaluation identified gaps between needs, resources, and utilization, particularly outside preventive care. These findings highlight that improving HPV vaccination among adult men requires multilevel behavioral strategies alongside healthcare system improvements that better recognize and respond to men's vaccination needs.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Human papillomavirus (HPV) infection is widespread and a leading cause of preventable cancers, yet HPV vaccination uptake among adult men remains suboptimal.
Evidence base and method
Ten electronic databases, including PubMed, Embase, and PsycINFO, were searched from inception to December 2025 using controlled vocabulary and free-text terms. Thematic synthesis followed Thomas and Harden's approach, with findings organized across five SEM levels.
Consensus and conclusions
Key barriers included gendered perceptions of HPV, low perceived susceptibility, stigma and disclosure concerns, provider communication failures, economic constraints, and structural access challenges. Facilitators included provider recommendation, supportive relationships, integrated care pathways, and health information access.
Controversies and gaps
The evidence base was concentrated among sexual minority men, sex workers, and healthcare-engaged men, with fewer studies among general male populations; six of the ten studies were conducted in the United States, limiting generalizability to adult men more broadly. Health service evaluation identified gaps between needs, resources, and utilization, particularly outside preventive care.
Research and practice implications
These findings highlight that improving HPV vaccination among adult men requires multilevel behavioral strategies alongside healthcare system improvements that better recognize and respond to men's vaccination needs.

意大利威尼托青春期前儿童中 HPV 疫苗接种 的启动:与慢性病和社会经济劣势的关联

HPV vaccination initiation among preadolescents in Veneto, Italy: associations with chronic conditions and socioeconomic disadvantage
作者： Veronica Casotto, Maria Giovanna Manfredi, Roberta Lo Bue, Michele Tonon, Francesca Russo, Anna Cantarutti, Carlo Giaquinto, Silvia Bressan, Alessandra Buja
摘要中文翻译背景:确定 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗接种 摄取率较低的人群对于制定有针对性的公共卫生干预措施至关重要。本研究调查了意大利威尼托地区青春期前青少年的慢性疾病和社会人口因素是否与 HPV 疫苗接种 感染相关。方法:我们使用意大利初级保健儿科数据库 Pedianet 的数据进行了一项回顾性 队列研究 研究。该研究纳入了 2008 年至 2011 年出生的儿童,并对 9 至 12 岁的儿童进行了跟踪调查。疫苗接种 启动被定义为从九岁生日到十三岁生日前一天接受至少一剂 HPV 疫苗。使用多变量多级逻辑回归评估与性别、出生 队列研究、选定的慢性病、残疾、基于收入的医疗保健豁免和儿科医疗保健利用率的关联,并对家庭儿科医生进行随机截距。结果:在 93 名儿科医生随访的 19,245 名儿童中,53.6% 接受了至少一剂 HPV 疫苗 疫苗。哮喘与 疫苗接种 启动的较高几率相关(调整后比值比 [aOR] 1.36;95% 置信区间 [CI] 1.12-1.67),而残疾(aOR 0.71;95% CI 0.58-0.88)、基于收入的医疗保健豁免(aOR 0.81;95% CI 0.76-0.86)和男性性别(aOR 0.79;95% CI 0.74-0.84)与较低的起始几率相关。在同一年龄窗口内接受两次 HPV 疫苗 剂量的关联与起始剂量的关联大致一致。结论:HPV 疫苗接种 启动与健康相关和社会人口因素相关。具有行政认可的残疾的儿童、享有基于收入的医疗保健豁免的儿童和男孩的接受率较低,这些群体确定了可能受益于有针对性的 疫苗接种 支持的群体。进一步的研究应该澄清这些关联背后的障碍。

范围与问题
确定 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗接种 吸收率较低的人群对于制定有针对性的公共卫生干预措施至关重要。
证据基础与方法
我们使用意大利初级保健儿科数据库 Pedianet 的数据进行了一项回顾性 队列研究 研究。使用多变量多级逻辑回归评估与性别、出生 队列研究、选定的慢性病、残疾、基于收入的医疗保健豁免和儿科医疗保健利用率的关联,并对家庭儿科医生进行随机截距。
共识与结论
HPV 疫苗接种 的启动与健康相关和社会人口因素有关。在 93 名儿科医生随访的 19,245 名儿童中,53.6% 接受了至少一剂 HPV 疫苗。哮喘与 疫苗接种 启动的较高几率相关(调整后比值比 [aOR] 1.36;95% 置信区间 [CI] 1.12-1.67),而残疾(aOR 0.71;95% CI 0.58-0.88)、基于收入的医疗保健豁免(aOR 0.81;95% CI 0.76-0.86)和男性性别(aOR 0.79;95% CI 0.74-0.84)与较低的起始几率相关。在同一年龄窗口内接受两次 HPV 疫苗 剂量的关联与起始剂量的关联大致一致。
争议与缺口
进一步的研究应该澄清这些关联背后的障碍。
科研与实践启示
具有行政认可的残疾的儿童、享有基于收入的医疗保健豁免的儿童和男孩的接受率较低,这些群体确定了可能受益于有针对性的 疫苗接种 支持的群体。

HPV vaccination initiation among preadolescents in Veneto, Italy: associations with chronic conditions and socioeconomic disadvantage

Authors: Veronica Casotto, Maria Giovanna Manfredi, Roberta Lo Bue, Michele Tonon, Francesca Russo, Anna Cantarutti, Carlo Giaquinto, Silvia Bressan, Alessandra Buja
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Identifying population groups with lower human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination uptake is essential for developing targeted public health interventions. This study examined whether chronic conditions and sociodemographic factors were associated with HPV vaccination initiation among preadolescents in the Veneto Region of Italy. METHODS: We conducted a retrospective cohort study using data from Pedianet, an Italian primary-care pediatric database. The study included children born between 2008 and 2011 who were followed between 9 and 12 years of age. Vaccination initiation was defined as receipt of at least one HPV vaccine dose from the ninth birthday to the day before the thirteenth birthday. Associations with sex, birth cohort, selected chronic conditions, disability, income-based healthcare exemption, and pediatric healthcare utilization were assessed using multivariable multilevel logistic regression, with a random intercept for family pediatrician. RESULTS: Among 19,245 children followed by 93 pediatricians, 53.6% received at least one HPV vaccine dose. Asthma was associated with higher odds of vaccination initiation (adjusted odds ratio [aOR] 1.36; 95% confidence interval [CI] 1.12-1.67), whereas disability (aOR 0.71; 95% CI 0.58-0.88), income-based healthcare exemption (aOR 0.81; 95% CI 0.76-0.86), and male sex (aOR 0.79; 95% CI 0.74-0.84) were associated with lower odds of initiation. Associations with receipt of two HPV vaccine doses within the same age window were broadly consistent with those for initiation. CONCLUSIONS: HPV vaccination initiation was associated with health-related and sociodemographic factors. Lower uptake among children with administratively recognized disability, those with income-based healthcare exemptions, and boys identifies groups that may benefit from targeted vaccination support. Further research should clarify the barriers underlying these associations.
View review five-element analysis

与 HPV 相关的口咽鳞状细胞癌的突变特征:台湾数据与已发表研究的比较

Mutational Profile of Oropharyngeal Squamous Cell Carcinoma in Relation to HPV : Comparison of Taiwan Data With Published Studies
作者： Hui‐Ching Wang, Shang‐Hung Chen, Pei‐Jen Lou
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 为了描述性地将台湾多中心登记处的 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 状态的基因组图谱与已发表的西方 队列研究 进行比较。方法 我们回顾了 TCOG T1322(ACTOnco + 440 基因组)中 290 例 OPSCC 病例的汇总结果以及来自美国和欧洲的选定研究。没有进行正式的交叉 队列研究 统计比较。结果和讨论 队列研究 中的主要驱动基因模式大致相似。在 T1322 TMB 可评估子集中(HPV 阳性 n = 187;HPV 阴性 n = 99),所提供的摘要报告两组的中位 TMB 均为 3.2 突变/Mb,四分位数范围分别为 1.3-5.7 和 1.9-4.4 突变/Mb。此更新子集的验证 p 值不可用;因此,没有对统计显着性做出任何推断。结论 交叉研究模式是假设生成,定量生物标志物比较需要统一的方法和独立的验证。

Mutational Profile of Oropharyngeal Squamous Cell Carcinoma in Relation to HPV : Comparison of Taiwan Data With Published Studies

Authors: Hui‐Ching Wang, Shang‐Hung Chen, Pei‐Jen Lou
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Background To descriptively compare genomic profiles by human papillomavirus (HPV) status in a Taiwanese multicenter registry with published Western cohorts. Methods We reviewed aggregate findings from 290 OPSCC cases in TCOG T1322 (ACTOnco + 440‐gene panel) and selected studies from the United States and Europe. No formal cross‐cohort statistical comparison was performed. Results and Discussion Major driver‐gene patterns were broadly similar across cohorts. In the T1322 TMB‐evaluable subset (HPV‐positive n = 187; HPV‐negative n = 99), the supplied summary reported a median TMB of 3.2 mutations/Mb in both groups, with interquartile ranges of 1.3–5.7 and 1.9–4.4 mutations/Mb, respectively. A verified p value for this updated subset was unavailable; therefore, no inference about statistical significance is made. Conclusion Cross‐study patterns are hypothesis‐generating, and quantitative biomarker comparisons require harmonized methodology and independent validation.

Evidence base and method

Consensus and conclusions

Controversies and gaps

大麻使用与口腔癌风险的关联:系统回顾和荟萃分析。

Association of Cannabis Use and Risks of Oral Cancer: A Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis.
作者： Asif Jan, Shubham Kumar, Bushra Waheed, Pratima Pandey, Huma Sultana, Daniel Dubwa
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
口腔癌是全球主要的健康问题,其病因与吸烟、饮酒和 HPV 感染等因素有关。这项系统评价和荟萃分析旨在评估大麻使用与患口腔癌风险之间的关联。
证据基础与方法
在 PubMed、EMBASE 和 Web of Science 上进行了系统性检索,以确定截至 2025 年 8 月 15 日的研究。纳入标准包括成人参与者以及关于大麻使用和口腔癌风险的观察性研究。荟萃分析利用随机效应模型来计算 R 软件版本 4.4 中的合并比值比 (OR)。使用纽卡斯尔渥太华量表进行质量评估。
共识与结论
From 1,063 records, 11 studies met the inclusion criteria with a total sample size of 245,594 participants. The meta-analysis revealed a pooled OR of 0.481 (95% CI: 0.306 to 0.757), suggesting a potential reduced risk of oral cancer with cannabis use, particularly noted in case-control studies (pooled OR: 0.455, 95% CI: 0.291 to 0.713). However, cohort study showed no significant association (pooled OR: 2.51, 95% CI: 0.244 to 25.823), and high heterogeneity was observed across studies. Current evidence does not support cannabis use as an independent risk factor for oral cancer.
争议与缺口
尽管一些病例对照研究表明存在反向关联,但这一发现并未得到 队列研究 研究的支持,考虑到显着的异质性和潜在的残留混杂因素,应谨慎解释。需要进一步的前瞻性研究来阐明这种关系。
科研与实践启示
需要进一步的前瞻性研究来阐明大麻使用与口腔癌风险之间的关系。本综述强调需要进行更多研究来解决研究中观察到的异质性,并更好地理解潜在反向关联背后的机制。

Association of Cannabis Use and Risks of Oral Cancer: A Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis.

Authors: Asif Jan, Shubham Kumar, Bushra Waheed, Pratima Pandey, Huma Sultana, Daniel Dubwa
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Oral cancer is a major health concern globally, with its etiology linked to factors such as tobacco use, alcohol consumption, and HPV infection. With changing lifestyle behaviors and the increased legalization of cannabis, its potential role in oral carcinogenesis has garnered attention. This systematic review and meta-analysis aims to evaluate the association between cannabis use and the risk of developing oral cancer. METHODS: A systematic search was conducted across PubMed, EMBASE, and Web of Science to identify studies up to August 15, 2025. Inclusion criteria encompassed adult participants and observational studies on cannabis use and oral cancer risk. Meta-analysis utilized random-effects models to compute pooled odds ratios (ORs) in R software version 4.4. Newcastle Ottawa scale was used for quality assessment. RESULTS: From 1,063 records, 11 studies met the inclusion criteria with a total sample size of 245,594 participants. The meta-analysis revealed a pooled OR of 0.481 (95% CI: 0.306 to 0.757), suggesting a potential reduced risk of oral cancer with cannabis use, particularly noted in case-control studies (pooled OR: 0.455, 95% CI: 0.291 to 0.713). However, cohort study showed no significant association (pooled OR: 2.51, 95% CI: 0.244 to 25.823), and high heterogeneity was observed across studies. CONCLUSION: Current evidence does not support cannabis use as an independent risk factor for oral cancer. Although some case-control studies suggested an inverse association, this finding was not supported by cohort studies and should be interpreted cautiously given the substantial heterogeneity and potential for residual confounding. Further prospective studies are needed to clarify this relationship.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Oral cancer is a major health concern globally, with its etiology linked to factors such as tobacco use, alcohol consumption, and HPV infection. This systematic review and meta-analysis aims to evaluate the association between cannabis use and the risk of developing oral cancer.
Evidence base and method
A systematic search was conducted across PubMed, EMBASE, and Web of Science to identify studies up to August 15, 2025. Inclusion criteria encompassed adult participants and observational studies on cannabis use and oral cancer risk. Meta-analysis utilized random-effects models to compute pooled odds ratios (ORs) in R software version 4.4. Newcastle Ottawa scale was used for quality assessment.
Consensus and conclusions
From 1,063 records, 11 studies met the inclusion criteria with a total sample size of 245,594 participants. The meta-analysis revealed a pooled OR of 0.481 (95% CI: 0.306 to 0.757), suggesting a potential reduced risk of oral cancer with cannabis use, particularly noted in case-control studies (pooled OR: 0.455, 95% CI: 0.291 to 0.713). However, cohort study showed no significant association (pooled OR: 2.51, 95% CI: 0.244 to 25.823), and high heterogeneity was observed across studies. Current evidence does not support cannabis use as an independent risk factor for oral cancer.
Controversies and gaps
Although some case-control studies suggested an inverse association, this finding was not supported by cohort studies and should be interpreted cautiously given the substantial heterogeneity and potential for residual confounding. Further prospective studies are needed to clarify this relationship.
Research and practice implications
Further prospective studies are needed to clarify the relationship between cannabis use and oral cancer risk. This review highlights the need for more research to address the heterogeneity observed in the studies and to better understand the mechanisms underlying the potential inverse association.

对安全网环境中 HPV 自我采样情况上游领域的实施前评估:范围界定审查。

Pre-implementation assessment of upstream domains on the uptake of HPV self-sampling in safety-net settings: a scoping review.
作者： Lynsey G Huppe, Cassandra A Okechukwu, Thelma Nanbigne, Grace Wang
摘要中文翻译人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 筛查可用作 宫颈癌 的早期检测器。美国食品和药物管理局新批准的 HPV 自采样检测可供美国的安全网实践使用,以帮助减轻目前患者中出现的筛查差异。由于患者的可接受性和能力已经建立,本次范围审查从安全网提供者和领导层的角度综合了实施 HPV 自我采样的障碍和促进因素,以及当前研究中尚未探讨的上游因素。遵循 PRISMA-ScR 指南,进行了文献检索,探索同行评审的出版物和灰色文献,以确定 HPV 自我采样的当前状况。调查结果是使用实施研究综合框架进行组织的。从纳入的 57 项工作中发现,安全网实践能够利用当前的工作流程和团队成员来实施 HPV 自我采样。然而,外部制定的指导方针和政策严重影响实施过程,可能导致安全网实践的延迟。最近的指南认可和即将推出的保险范围解决了其中一些障碍,使安全网设置的批准后实施研究成为当务之急。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本次范围界定审查重点关注在安全网环境中实施 HPV 自我采样的障碍和促进因素,重点关注当前研究中尚未探讨的上游因素。该审查审查了安全网提供者和领导者在采用 HPV 自我采样检测方面的作用,这些检测已获得 FDA 批准,旨在减少 宫颈癌 筛查差异。
证据基础与方法
该综述遵循 PRISMA-ScR 指南进行文献检索,包括同行评审的出版物和灰色文献。研究结果是使用实施研究综合框架来组织实施因素的分析的。
共识与结论
审查发现,安全网实践能够利用现有的工作流程和团队成员来实施 HPV 自我采样。然而,外部指导方针和政策极大地影响了实施过程,可能会延迟这些环境中的采用。
争议与缺口
对于安全网环境中影响HPV自我采样实施的上游因素缺乏全面的研究。此外,审查还强调,虽然最近的指南认可和保险范围解决了一些障碍,但仍需要进一步研究以了解这些变化的全面影响。
科研与实践启示
安全网环境中的批准后实施研究是当务之急,因为最近的指南认可和保险覆盖范围预计将减少 HPV 自我采样的一些障碍。这项研究应侧重于了解这些环境中的具体挑战和促进因素,以改善 宫颈癌 筛查的公平机会。

Pre-implementation assessment of upstream domains on the uptake of HPV self-sampling in safety-net settings: a scoping review.

Authors: Lynsey G Huppe, Cassandra A Okechukwu, Thelma Nanbigne, Grace Wang
Original abstract (English)
Human papillomavirus (HPV) screening can be used as an early detector for cervical cancer. The newly US Food and Drug Administration-approved HPV self-sampling tests can be used by safety net practices in the USA to help mitigate the current screening disparities seen in patients. As patient acceptability and capability have already been established, this scoping review synthesizes the barriers and facilitators of implementing HPV self-sampling from the perspective of safety net providers and leadership, and the upstream factors not yet explored in current research. Following PRISMA-ScR guidelines, a literature search was conducted exploring peer-reviewed publications and grey literature to establish the current landscape of HPV self-sampling. Findings were organized using the Consolidated Framework for Implementation Research. From the 57 works included, it was found that safety net practices are capable of implementing HPV self-sampling by utilizing current workflows and team members. However, outer setting guidelines and policies heavily influence the implementation process, likely causing a delay in uptake by safety net practices. Recent guideline endorsements and forthcoming insurance coverage address several of these barriers, making post-approval implementation research in safety net settings an urgent priority.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This scoping review focuses on the barriers and facilitators of implementing HPV self-sampling in safety net settings, with an emphasis on upstream factors not yet explored in current research. The review examines the role of safety net providers and leadership in the uptake of HPV self-sampling tests, which are FDA-approved and aimed at mitigating cervical cancer screening disparities.
Evidence base and method
The review followed PRISMA-ScR guidelines to conduct a literature search, including peer-reviewed publications and grey literature. The findings were organized using the Consolidated Framework for Implementation Research to structure the analysis of implementation factors.
Consensus and conclusions
The review found that safety net practices are capable of implementing HPV self-sampling by utilizing existing workflows and team members. However, external guidelines and policies significantly influence the implementation process, potentially delaying uptake in these settings.
Controversies and gaps
There is a lack of comprehensive research on the upstream factors affecting the implementation of HPV self-sampling in safety net settings. Additionally, the review highlights that while recent guideline endorsements and insurance coverage address some barriers, further research is needed to understand the full impact of these changes.
Research and practice implications
Post-approval implementation research in safety net settings is an urgent priority, as recent guideline endorsements and insurance coverage are expected to reduce some of the barriers to HPV self-sampling uptake. This research should focus on understanding the specific challenges and facilitators within these settings to improve equitable access to cervical cancer screening.
