C 补充信息学术文献 · 综述C supplementaryAcademic literature · Review

HPV 疫苗传播中的叙事和语言策略:对政府、医疗机构和从业者在新浪微博上的账号的内容分析

Narrative and Linguistic Strategies in HPV Vaccine Communication: A Content Analysis of Government, Medical Institutions, and Practitioners’ Accounts on Sina Weibo
作者： Ye Lin, Yuxin Cao, Shaohai Jiang
摘要中文翻译人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗接种 是中国的一个核心公共卫生问题,公众对社交媒体上 HPV 相关内容的参与度差异很大。因此,确定哪些叙事和语言策略能引起更强的反应对于提高 疫苗 沟通的有效性至关重要。本研究使用模糊集定性比较分析(一种识别哪些消息特征组合与结果相关的方法)来检验叙事存在、叙事视角、语法情绪、语言模态和帐户类型如何结合起来与新浪微博上 638 个 HPV 疫苗 帖子的参与度相对应。该分析确定了与高受众参与度相关的几种不同配置,以及与低参与度相关的多种模式。当医疗机构的帖子采用第二人称、第三人称或具有高模态语言(即高确定性的语言)的非人称叙事形式时,他们的帖子与更高的参与度相关,而当医生依靠鼓励对话方向的纯问题帖子时,他们的帖子与更高的参与度相关。低参与度最常见于使用医疗从业者的中等情态语言(即适度自信的措辞)的非叙述性声明性帖子中,而试探性的低情态语言是五种低参与度配置中的三项的特征,而高参与度配置中则没有。这些发现明确了叙事和语言策略与受众参与相关的边界条件,并说明了语法情绪和语言模态如何与消息内容和来源类型相关。此外,研究结果为在社交媒体上设计更具吸引力的 HPV 疫苗 通信提供了实用的见解。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该研究重点关注 HPV 疫苗 在新浪微博上的交流中使用的叙事和语言策略,研究这些策略如何影响受众参与度。核心问题是消息特征的不同组合,例如叙述存在、视角、语法情绪和语言模式,如何与 HPV 疫苗 帖子的参与度相关。
证据基础与方法
该评论采用叙述性内容分析方法,使用模糊集定性比较分析来识别与参与度相关的消息特征的配置。该研究分析了新浪微博上来自政府、医疗机构和从业人员的 638 条 HPV 疫苗 帖子,重点关注叙事形式、语言模式和账户类型。
共识与结论
分析发现,来自医疗机构的帖子使用第二人称、第三人称或非人称叙事形式和高模态语言(即高确定性语言)与更强的受众参与度相关。此外,鼓励通过仅提问形式进行对话的医生帖子也显示出很高的参与度。相比之下,医生使用中等形式语言的非叙述性声明性帖子与低参与度有关。
争议与缺口
该研究强调,试探性的低模态语言是五种低参与度配置中的三种的特征,这表明在理解不同语言风格如何影响参与度方面存在潜在差距。研究结果还表明,叙事和语言策略的有效性可能取决于来源类型和信息内容,但确切的机制仍未得到充分探索。
科研与实践启示
研究结果为在社交媒体上设计更具吸引力的 HPV 疫苗 通信提供了实用的见解。他们强调在传播医疗机构信息时使用高模态语言和适当的叙述形式的重要性,同时也表明对话形式可能对从业者有效。这些发现可以为未来改善与 HPV 疫苗 相关的公共卫生信息传递的策略提供信息。

Narrative and Linguistic Strategies in HPV Vaccine Communication: A Content Analysis of Government, Medical Institutions, and Practitioners’ Accounts on Sina Weibo

Authors: Ye Lin, Yuxin Cao, Shaohai Jiang
Original abstract (English)
Human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination is a central public health concern in China, and public engagement with HPV-related content on social media varies widely. Identifying which narrative and linguistic strategies elicit stronger responses is therefore essential for improving the effectiveness of vaccine communication. Using fuzzy-set qualitative comparative analysis, an approach that identifies which combinations of message features are associated with an outcome, this study examines how narrative presence, narrative perspective, grammatical mood, linguistic modality, and account type combine to correspond to engagement with 638 HPV vaccine posts on Sina Weibo. The analysis identifies several distinct configurations associated with high audience engagement, as well as multiple patterns linked to low engagement. Posts from medical institutions were associated with stronger engagement when they adopted second-person, third-person, or impersonal narrative forms with high-modality language (i.e., language with high certainty), while medical practitioners were associated with high engagement when they relied on question-only posts that encouraged a conversational orientation. Low engagement was most often observed in non-narrative declarative posts using medium-modality language (i.e., moderately assertive wording) from medical practitioners, while tentative, low-modality language characterized three of the five low-engagement configurations and none of the high-engagement configurations. These findings specify boundary conditions under which narrative and linguistic strategies are associated with audience engagement, and illustrate how grammatical mood and linguistic modality function in relation to message content and source type. Moreover, the results offer practical insights for designing more engaging HPV vaccine communication on social media.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The study focuses on the narrative and linguistic strategies used in HPV vaccine communication on Sina Weibo, examining how these strategies influence audience engagement. The central question is how different combinations of message features, such as narrative presence, perspective, grammatical mood, and linguistic modality, relate to engagement levels with HPV vaccine posts.
Evidence base and method
The review employs a narrative content analysis approach, using fuzzy-set qualitative comparative analysis to identify configurations of message features associated with engagement. The study analyzed 638 HPV vaccine posts from government, medical institutions, and practitioners on Sina Weibo, focusing on narrative forms, linguistic modality, and account type.
Consensus and conclusions
The analysis found that posts from medical institutions using second-person, third-person, or impersonal narrative forms with high-modality language (i.e., language with high certainty) were associated with stronger audience engagement. Additionally, medical practitioners' posts that encouraged a conversational orientation through question-only formats also showed high engagement. In contrast, non-narrative declarative posts with medium-modality language from medical practitioners were linked to low engagement.
Controversies and gaps
The study highlights that tentative, low-modality language was characteristic of three of the five low-engagement configurations, indicating a potential gap in understanding how different linguistic styles affect engagement. The findings also suggest that the effectiveness of narrative and linguistic strategies may depend on the source type and message content, but the exact mechanisms remain underexplored.
Research and practice implications
The results provide practical insights for designing more engaging HPV vaccine communication on social media. They emphasize the importance of using high-modality language and appropriate narrative forms when disseminating information from medical institutions, while also suggesting that conversational formats may be effective for practitioners. These findings can inform future strategies for improving public health messaging related to HPV vaccines.

了解南亚年轻女性的宫颈筛查参与度:女性和医疗保健专业人员的观点。

Understanding Cervical Screening Engagement Among Young South Asian Women: Perspectives of Women and Healthcare Professionals.
作者： Katie Merry, Zainab Laheri, Sarah Johns, Yasemin Hirst, Rebecca Jefferson
摘要中文翻译背景:尽管 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗 的使用率有所增加,但南亚年轻女性的宫颈筛查参与率有所下降,特别是在英格兰的贫困地区。造成这种模式的原因仍然知之甚少。本研究探讨了贫困地区 25-29 岁南亚女性宫颈筛查的障碍和促进因素。方法:与 Darwen 一起对布莱克本 25-29 岁的南亚女性 (n = 15) 和参与宫颈筛查的医疗保健专业人员 (n = 6) 进行半结构化访谈。使用反思性主题分析对数据进行分析。结果:筛查的障碍是多因素的,包括对宫颈筛查和 HPV 的了解有限、关于谦虚和性的文化规范、对社区判断和尴尬的恐惧,以及预约机会和竞争责任等结构性挑战。家庭网络既是障碍又是促进者。医疗保健专业人员描述了调整语言以减少恐惧,但强调了与人员配置和资金相关的服务压力,这限制了积极参与。尽管大多数女性报告接受了 HPV 疫苗接种,但对 HPV 和宫颈筛查的持续重要性的了解仍然有限,这凸显了加强宫颈筛查教育的机会。结论:南亚年轻女性的宫颈筛查参与度受到文化、情感和结构等交叉影响的影响,这种影响在贫困环境中可能会加剧。HPV 疫苗接种 应伴随着关于宫颈筛查的持续重要性的明确沟通,特别是在知识和意识仍然较低的情况下。需要采取文化敏感的沟通、灵活的服务提供和社区伙伴关系方法来解决筛查方面持续存在的不平等问题。患者或公众贡献:该研究是根据先前的社区参与和针对服务不足人群的公众参与工作而进行的。访谈指南是与南亚社区的公共顾问合作制定的,以确保它们具有相关性、包容性并反映社区的优先事项。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该研究旨在探讨英格兰贫困地区 25-29 岁南亚女性宫颈筛查的障碍和促进因素,重点关注尽管 HPV 疫苗 摄取量增加,但参与率却下降。
证据基础与方法
该研究对 15 名南亚女性和 6 名参与宫颈筛查的医疗保健专业人员进行了半结构化访谈。使用反思性主题分析对数据进行分析。
共识与结论
南亚年轻女性的宫颈筛查参与度受到文化、情感和结构因素的影响。对 HPV 和宫颈筛查的了解有限、关于谦虚和性的文化规范、对判断的恐惧以及预约机会和竞争责任等结构性挑战被认为是主要障碍。家庭网络发挥着双重作用,既是障碍,也是促进者。医疗保健专业人士指出,需要进行文化响应式沟通和灵活的服务提供,以解决筛查接受方面的不平等问题。
争议与缺口
该研究突显了南亚女性对 HPV 和宫颈筛查缺乏了解,尽管大多数女性都接受了 HPV 疫苗。对于提高筛查参与度的最有效策略尚未达成共识,并且证据仅限于来自英格兰单个贫困地区的定性见解。
科研与实践启示
关于 HPV 疫苗接种 的持续重要性的明确沟通应与 HPV 疫苗接种 一起进行,特别是在知识和意识较低的地区。建议采取文化敏感的沟通、灵活的服务提供和社区伙伴关系方法来解决筛查采用方面持续存在的不平等问题。

Understanding Cervical Screening Engagement Among Young South Asian Women: Perspectives of Women and Healthcare Professionals.

Authors: Katie Merry, Zainab Laheri, Sarah Johns, Yasemin Hirst, Rebecca Jefferson
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Despite increased uptake of the human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccine, cervical screening participation among younger South Asian women has declined, particularly in deprived areas in England. The reasons for this pattern remain poorly understood. This study explored barriers and facilitators to cervical screening among South Asian women aged 25-29 in a deprived locality. METHODS: Semi-structured interviews were conducted with South Asian women aged 25-29 (n = 15) and healthcare professionals involved in cervical screening delivery (n = 6) in Blackburn with Darwen. Data were analysed using reflexive thematic analysis. RESULTS: Barriers to screening were multifactorial and included limited understanding of cervical screening and HPV, cultural norms surrounding modesty and sexuality, fear of community judgement and embarrassment, and structural challenges such as appointment access and competing responsibilities. Family networks acted as both barriers and facilitators. Healthcare professionals described adapting language to reduce fear, but highlighted service pressures related to staffing and funding, which constrained proactive engagement. Although most women reported receiving the HPV vaccination, understanding of HPV and the continued importance of cervical screening remained limited, highlighting opportunities to reinforce cervical screening education. CONCLUSION: Cervical screening engagement among young South Asian women is shaped by intersecting cultural, emotional, and structural influences, which may be intensified in deprived settings. HPV vaccination should be accompanied by clear communication about the continued importance of cervical screening, particularly where knowledge and awareness remain low. Culturally responsive communication, flexible service provision and community partnership approaches are needed to address persistent inequalities in screening uptake. PATIENT OR PUBLIC CONTRIBUTION: The study was informed by prior community engagement and public involvement work with underserved populations. Interview guides were developed in collaboration with a public advisor from the South Asian community to ensure they were relevant, inclusive, and reflective of community priorities.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The study aimed to explore barriers and facilitators to cervical screening among South Asian women aged 25-29 in a deprived locality in England, focusing on the declining participation despite increased HPV vaccine uptake.
Evidence base and method
The study used semi-structured interviews with 15 South Asian women and 6 healthcare professionals involved in cervical screening delivery. Data were analyzed using reflexive thematic analysis.
Consensus and conclusions
Cervical screening engagement among young South Asian women is influenced by cultural, emotional, and structural factors. Limited understanding of HPV and cervical screening, cultural norms around modesty and sexuality, fear of judgment, and structural challenges like appointment access and competing responsibilities were identified as key barriers. Family networks played a dual role as both barriers and facilitators. Healthcare professionals noted the need for culturally responsive communication and flexible service provision to address inequalities in screening uptake.
Controversies and gaps
The study highlights a lack of understanding about HPV and cervical screening among South Asian women, even though most have received the HPV vaccine. There is no consensus on the most effective strategies to improve screening engagement, and the evidence is limited to qualitative insights from a single deprived locality in England.
Research and practice implications
Clear communication about the continued importance of cervical screening should accompany HPV vaccination, especially in areas with low knowledge and awareness. Culturally responsive communication, flexible service provision, and community partnership approaches are recommended to address persistent inequalities in screening uptake.

相同的切缘,不同的病变:切缘亚型、腺体受累和宫颈管刮除状态作为重复环形电外科切除手术中残留 CIN2+ 的预测因子:一项 11 年回顾性 队列研究 研究。

Same margin, different lesion: margin subtype, glandular involvement, and endocervical curettage status as predictors of residual CIN2+ at repeat loop electrosurgical excision procedure: An 11-year retrospective cohort study.
作者： Yunus Emre Purut, Gercik İ, Anıl Doğukan Tutal, Sercan Kantarcı, Ceren Sağlam Purut, Göktürk Han Doğan, İlayda Gercik
摘要中文翻译目的:本研究的目的是评估初始环形电外科切除术边缘亚型、腺体受累、宫颈内膜刮除结果和标本大小与再次环形电外科切除术病理学和接受进一步手术的关系。方法:在这项回顾性 队列研究 研究中,回顾了 287 名在三级中心(2015-2025 年)接受再循环电外科切除手术的患者。初始保证金状态分为保证金连续性、保证金相邻性或不可评估保证金。使用卡方/Fisher-Freeman-Halton 检验、Cohen's kappa 和多变量逻辑回归评估与再循环电外科切除手术病理学、残留 CIN2+ 疾病和接受第三次手术干预的关联。结果:初始和再次循环电外科切除手术病理学显示出显着的组级相关性(p=0.039),但除偶然性外没有个案一致性(kappa=-0.009,p=0.754);大多数初始 CIN2/3 的患者在再次循环电外科切除过程中出现 CIN1。边缘邻近独立预测重复循环电外科切除过程中残留的 CIN2+ 疾病(OR 11.045,p=0.049),腺体受累也是如此(OR 1.938,p=0.035)。在再次循环电外科切除手术边缘呈阴性的患者中,27.1% 的标本内发现残留 CIN2+ 疾病,首次随访时 人乳头瘤病毒 测试阴性的率为 88.2%,具体情况因基因型而异。重复循环电外科切除过程中的阳性切缘(OR 333.33,p<0.001)和阳性宫颈管刮除术(OR 41.67,p<0.001)是接受第三次手术干预的最强预测因素。结论:边缘邻近和腺体受累独立预测重复环路电外科切除手术中残留的 CIN2+,而再次环路电外科切除手术中的阳性切缘和阳性宫颈管刮除术与接受第三次手术干预密切相关。环路电外科切除术后随访应综合切缘亚型、腺体受累、宫颈管刮除结果和 人乳头瘤病毒 监测,而不是仅依赖切缘状态;人乳头瘤病毒 测试阴性的基因型特异性差异需要在较大的 队列研究 中进行确认。

范围与问题
该研究旨在评估初始环形电外科切除手术边缘亚型、腺体受累、宫颈管刮除结果和标本大小与再次环形电外科切除手术病理学和接受进一步手术的关系。

在这项回顾性 队列研究 研究中,回顾了 287 名在三级中心(2015-2025 年)接受再循环电外科切除手术的患者。初始保证金状态分为保证金连续性、保证金相邻性或不可评估保证金。使用卡方/Fisher-Freeman-Halton 检验、Cohen's kappa 和多变量逻辑回归评估与再循环电外科切除手术病理学、残留 CIN2+ 疾病和接受第三次手术干预的关联。
共识与结论
初始和再循环电外科切除手术病理学显示出显着的组级相关性(p=0.039),但没有超越偶然的个案一致性(kappa=-0.009,p=0.754);大多数初始 CIN2/3 的患者在再次循环电外科切除过程中出现 CIN1。边缘邻近独立预测重复循环电外科切除过程中残留的 CIN2+ 疾病(OR 11.045,p=0.049),腺体受累也是如此(OR 1.938,p=0.035)。重复循环电外科切除过程中的阳性切缘(OR 333.33,p<0.001)和阳性宫颈管刮除术(OR 41.67,p<0.001)是接受第三次手术干预的最强预测因素。

所提供的证据并未报告与 人乳头瘤病毒 相关的争议或证据差距。

环路电外科切除术后随访应综合切缘亚型、腺体受累、宫颈管刮除结果和 人乳头瘤病毒 监测,而不是仅依赖切缘状态;人乳头瘤病毒 测试阴性的基因型特异性差异需要在较大的 队列研究 中进行确认。

Same margin, different lesion: margin subtype, glandular involvement, and endocervical curettage status as predictors of residual CIN2+ at repeat loop electrosurgical excision procedure: An 11-year retrospective cohort study.

Authors: Yunus Emre Purut, Gercik İ, Anıl Doğukan Tutal, Sercan Kantarcı, Ceren Sağlam Purut, Göktürk Han Doğan, İlayda Gercik
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVE: The aim of this study was to assess how initial loop electrosurgical excision procedure margin subtype, glandular involvement, endocervical curettage findings, and specimen size relate to re-loop electrosurgical excision procedure pathology and receipt of further surgery. METHODS: In this retrospective cohort study, 287 patients undergoing re-loop electrosurgical excision procedure at a tertiary center (2015-2025) were reviewed. Initial margin status was classified as margin continuity, margin adjacency, or non-evaluable margin. Associations with re-loop electrosurgical excision procedure pathology, residual CIN2+ disease, and receipt of a third surgical intervention were assessed using chi-square/Fisher-Freeman-Halton tests, Cohen's kappa, and multivariate logistic regression. RESULTS: Initial and re-loop electrosurgical excision procedure pathology showed a significant group-level association (p=0.039) but no case-by-case agreement beyond chance (kappa=-0.009, p=0.754); most patients with initial CIN2/3 had CIN1 at re-loop electrosurgical excision procedure. Margin adjacency independently predicted residual CIN2+ disease at repeat loop electrosurgical excision procedure (OR 11.045, p=0.049), as did glandular involvement (OR 1.938, p=0.035). Among patients with negative re-loop electrosurgical excision procedure margins, residual CIN2+ disease was identified within the specimen in 27.1%, and human papillomavirus test negativity at the first follow-up visit was 88.2%, varying by genotype. Positive margin (OR 333.33, p<0.001) and positive endocervical curettage (OR 41.67, p<0.001) at repeat loop electrosurgical excision procedure were the strongest predictors of receiving a third surgical intervention. CONCLUSION: Margin adjacency and glandular involvement independently predicted residual CIN2+ at repeat loop electrosurgical excision procedure, whereas positive margins and positive endocervical curettage at re-loop electrosurgical excision procedure were strongly associated with receipt of a third surgical intervention. Post-loop electrosurgical excision procedure follow-up should integrate margin subtype, glandular involvement, endocervical curettage findings, and human papillomavirus surveillance rather than rely on margin status alone; genotype-specific differences in human papillomavirus test negativity require confirmation in larger cohorts.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The study aimed to assess how initial loop electrosurgical excision procedure margin subtype, glandular involvement, endocervical curettage findings, and specimen size relate to re-loop electrosurgical excision procedure pathology and receipt of further surgery.
Evidence base and method
In this retrospective cohort study, 287 patients undergoing re-loop electrosurgical excision procedure at a tertiary center (2015-2025) were reviewed. Initial margin status was classified as margin continuity, margin adjacency, or non-evaluable margin. Associations with re-loop electrosurgical excision procedure pathology, residual CIN2+ disease, and receipt of a third surgical intervention were assessed using chi-square/Fisher-Freeman-Halton tests, Cohen's kappa, and multivariate logistic regression.

Initial and re-loop electrosurgical excision procedure pathology showed a significant group-level association (p=0.039) but no case-by-case agreement beyond chance (kappa=-0.009, p=0.754); most patients with initial CIN2/3 had CIN1 at re-loop electrosurgical excision procedure. Controversies and gaps
The supplied evidence does not report on controversies or evidence gaps related to human papillomaviruses.

Post-loop electrosurgical excision procedure follow-up should integrate margin subtype, glandular involvement, endocervical curettage findings, and human papillomavirus surveillance rather than rely on margin status alone; genotype-specific differences in human papillomavirus test negativity require confirmation in larger cohorts.

A 高优先级公共卫生新闻

HPV 疫苗接种 活动将于周三开始穿过 KP

HPV vaccination drive to begin across KP on Wednesday
新闻简报该新闻报道强调,从 2026 年 11 月 10 日星期三开始,在开伯尔-普赫图赫瓦省 (KP) 启动了 HPV 疫苗接种 活动,一直持续到 2026 年 11 月 24 日。该活动针对 9 至 14 岁的少女,保护她们免受 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染,这是导致 宫颈癌 的主要原因。卫生当局敦促家长确保他们的女儿在此期间获得 疫苗。该举措是加强预防性医疗保健和提高对 宫颈癌 认识的更广泛努力的一部分,宫颈癌 仍然是一个重大的公共卫生问题。建议寻求更多信息的家长拨打 Sehat Tahaffuz 帮助热线 1166。该活动目前正在进行中,证据中没有报告具体病例或死亡人数。来源是 Bing News RSS 的部分报道,于 2026 年 11 月 8 日发布。
查看新闻五要素
时间
HPV 疫苗接种驱动器计划于2026年11月10日星期三开始,并将持续到2026年11月24日。该消息的发布日期为2026年11月8日。
地点与对象
该活动正在开伯尔-普赫图赫瓦省 (KP) 各地开展,目标对象是 9 至 14 岁的少女。
事件
开伯尔-普赫图赫瓦省卫生当局宣布开展 HPV 疫苗接种 活动,旨在保护少女免受 HPV 感染,这是导致 宫颈癌 的主要原因。该活动以官方通知为准。
规模、影响与风险
该活动旨在保护 KP 很大一部分青少年女性免受与 宫颈癌 相关的 HPV 感染。所提供的证据中没有报告具体病例或死亡人数。
应对、状态与不确定性
疫苗接种 活动是加强预防性医疗保健和提高对 宫颈癌 认识的持续努力的一部分。该活动目前正在进行中,证据中没有提供有关响应状态的进一步详细信息。
A high priorityPublic-health news

HPV vaccination drive to begin across KP on Wednesday

News Brief
The news report highlights the initiation of an HPV vaccination drive across Khyber Pakhtunkhwa (KP) starting on Wednesday, November 10, 2026, and running until November 24, 2026. The campaign targets adolescent girls aged 9 to 14 years to protect them against human papillomavirus (HPV), a primary cause of cervical cancer. Health authorities have urged parents to ensure their daughters receive the vaccine during this period. The initiative is part of broader efforts to enhance preventive healthcare and raise awareness about cervical cancer, which remains a significant public health concern. Parents seeking more information are advised to contact the Sehat Tahaffuz helpline at 1166. The campaign is currently underway, and no specific case or death counts are reported in the evidence. The source is a partial report from Bing News RSS, published on November 8, 2026.
Original source text (English)
Saturday, 14 November 2026, 05:43:44 am Toggle navigation Home National World Kashmir Current Affairs Programs FM 101 Urdu Dictionary CGTN Saut-ul-Quran Toggle navigation Podcast SIFC Ahang HPV vaccination drive to begin across KP on Wednesday November 08, 2026 Health authorities have urged parents to ensure that their daughters aged 9 to 14 years receive vaccination against human papillomavirus (HPV) during an upcoming immunization campaign across Khyber Pakhtunkhwa. The campaign will run from Wednesday until the 24th of this month and aims to protect adolescent girls against HPV, the primary cause of cervical cancer, through the administration of a safe and effective vaccine. Parents seeking further information about the vaccination drive and related services have been advised to contact the Sehat Tahaffuz helpline at 1166. They said the initiative is part of ongoing efforts to strengthen preventive healthcare and raise awareness about cervical cancer, which remains a major public health concern among women. View five news elements
Time
The HPV vaccination drive is scheduled to begin on Wednesday, November 10, 2026, and will run until the 24th of November 2026. The publication date of the news is November 8, 2026.
Location and population
The campaign is taking place across Khyber Pakhtunkhwa (KP), targeting adolescent girls aged 9 to 14 years.
Event
Health authorities in Khyber Pakhtunkhwa have announced an HPV vaccination drive aimed at protecting adolescent girls against HPV, the primary cause of cervical cancer. The event is confirmed based on the official notice.
Scale, impact and risk
The campaign aims to protect a significant portion of the adolescent female population in KP against HPV, which is associated with cervical cancer. No specific case or death counts are reported in the supplied evidence.
Response, status and uncertainty
The vaccination drive is part of ongoing efforts to strengthen preventive healthcare and raise awareness about cervical cancer. The campaign is currently in progress, and no further details on the response status are provided in the evidence.

新闻简报在国际药品采购机制的支持下,泛美卫生组织 (PAHO) 已在拉丁美洲和加勒比地区签署了 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 检测的区域准入协议。这些协议旨在降低高质量 HPV 检测的成本,使该地区数百万女性更容易获得 宫颈癌 筛查。该计划解决了此前限制 HPV 检测采用的财务、技术和计划障碍。国际药品采购机制将支持在萨尔瓦多、危地马拉、格林纳达、圣文森特和格林纳丁斯以及圣卢西亚等选定国家的实施,以扩大基于 HPV 的筛查计划并加强后续护理。HPV 感染是 宫颈癌 的主要原因,该病每年导致该地区超过 40,000 名女性死亡。这些协议是泛美卫生组织疾病消除倡议和非传染性疾病更好护理计划的一部分,旨在减少 宫颈癌 死亡率 并改善健康公平。预计参与国可节省 30% 至 70% 的成本,这些安排包括优惠价格、技术援助和培训,以支持 HPV 检测技术的采用。
查看新闻五要素
时间
这些协议于 2026 年 11 月 11 日宣布,预计将在选定的国家开始提供实施支持。证据中没有明确说明事件日期,但协议被描述为最近的进展。
地点与对象
这些协议涵盖拉丁美洲和加勒比地区的所有国家和地区,并计划为萨尔瓦多、危地马拉、格林纳达、圣文森特和格林纳丁斯以及圣卢西亚提供具体实施支持。受影响的人口包括该地区数百万妇女。
事件
泛美卫生组织在国际药品采购机制的支持下,在拉丁美洲和加勒比地区达成了 HPV 检测的区域性准入协议。这些协议旨在降低高质量 HPV 检测的成本并改进 宫颈癌 筛查。该事件根据证据中的明确陈述得到证实。
规模、影响与风险
这些协议预计将为参与国节省 30% 至 70% 的成本。几乎所有 宫颈癌 病例都是由 HPV 感染引起,该病每年导致该地区超过 40,000 名女性死亡。影响规模是巨大的,因为这些协议旨在扩大测试范围并减少 宫颈癌 死亡率。该风险并未明确说明,但证据强调了 HPV 检测在预防 宫颈癌 方面的重要性。
应对、状态与不确定性
这些协议已经到位,并得到泛美卫生组织和国际药品采购机制的支持。计划在选定的国家开展实施活动,这些安排是泛美卫生组织疾病消除倡议的一部分。证据并不表明任何未解决的信息或来源的限制。
A high priorityPublic-health news

PAHO secures first-ever region-wide access agreements for HPV testing across Latin America and the Caribbean, with support from Unitaid - Pan American Health Organization (PAHO)

News Brief
The Pan American Health Organization (PAHO) has secured region-wide access agreements for human papillomavirus (HPV) testing across Latin America and the Caribbean, with support from Unitaid. These agreements aim to lower the cost of high-quality HPV testing, making cervical cancer screening more accessible for millions of women in the region. The initiative addresses financial, technical, and programmatic barriers that have previously limited HPV testing uptake. Unitaid will support implementation in selected countries, including El Salvador, Guatemala, Grenada, Saint Vincent and the Grenadines, and Saint Lucia, to expand HPV-based screening programs and strengthen follow-up care. HPV infection is a leading cause of cervical cancer, which kills over 40,000 women annually in the region. The agreements are part of PAHO's Disease Elimination Initiative and the Better Care for NCDs program, designed to reduce cervical cancer mortality and improve health equity. The cost savings for participating countries are estimated to range from 30% to 70%, and the arrangements include preferential pricing, technical assistance, and training to support the adoption of HPV testing technologies.
Original source text (English)

Search Search Home News PAHO secures first-ever region-wide access agreements for HPV testing across Latin America and the Caribbean, with support from Unitaid PAHO secures first-ever region-wide access agreements for HPV testing across Latin America and the Caribbean, with support from Unitaid Opens in a new window Opens in a new window Opens in a new window Opens in a new window Landmark regional agreements will lower costs of high-quality HPV testing and help more women benefit from effective cervical cancer screening. Washington, D.C./Geneva, 11 November 2026 — Millions of women across Latin America and the Caribbean will gain access to more affordable, high-quality human papillomavirus (HPV) testing under landmark regional access agreements negotiated by the Pan American Health Organization (PAHO) through its Regional Revolving Funds. Unitaid supported these efforts to expand access to HPV testing and will advance implementation activities in selected countries. HPV infection causes nearly all cases of cervical cancer, which kills more than 40,000 women in the region each year. Cervical cancer is highly preventable through vaccination, screening and timely treatment. Until now, countries across Latin America and the Caribbean have faced some of the highest HPV test prices in the world. Under these first-ever regional access agreements, countries will be able to access high-quality molecular HPV tests through preferential pricing and a comprehensive support package, with participating countries expected to realize cost savings ranging from 30% to 70%. These technologies are now available through PAHO's Revolving Fund under harmonized regional conditions for all countries and territories in Latin America and the Caribbean. The arrangements expand the range of WHO-recommended molecular HPV testing technologies available through PAHO's Revolving Funds. In addition to access to testing, the agreements include implementation support, equipment access, training, maintenance and technical assistance designed to facilitate country adoption and scale-up. By building these components together, the agreements help to reduce operational barriers that have often limited the introduction of new diagnostic technologies. PAHO/WHO has recommended the use of HPV testing in cervical cancer screening programs since 2013, with updated guidance issued in 2026. However, uptake in the region has been limited due to financial, technical, and programmatic barriers. Unitaid's support to PAHO translates into expanded access to HPV testing in the region. Unitaid will also support implementation activities in El Salvador, Guatemala, Grenada, Saint Vincent and the Grenadines, and Saint Lucia, helping countries to introduce or expand HPV-based screening programs while strengthening follow-up care for women with positive results. "Through PAHO's Revolving Funds, countries now have access to high-quality HPV testing under more favorable conditions, helping accelerate efforts to eliminate cervical cancer in the Americas," said PAHO Director Dr. Jarbas Barbosa. "These agreements reflect PAHO's commitment to working with Member States and partners to expand equitable access to essential health technologies and strengthen cervical cancer prevention programs across the Region." “These agreements build on Unitaid’s global efforts to make cervical cancer prevention more affordable and accessible for women everywhere,” said Dr. Philippe Duneton, Executive Director of Unitaid. “By expanding access to affordable, high-quality HPV testing in Latin America and the Caribbean, and extending favorable pricing to countries that have historically been excluded from such arrangements, this initiative helps accelerate progress toward cervical cancer elimination while creating a more equitable and sustainable market for screening technologies.” Molecular tests directly detect high-risk HPV types responsible for most cervical cancer cases, allowing for early identification of women at risk. Compared to other screening methods, they perform better and can use self-collected samples, which can help expand access to testing among underserved populations. The ESTAMPA study, conducted in Latin America, found that HPV testing detected nearly all cases of precancerous cervical lesions and invasive cancer, while cytology, the conventional screening method, identified fewer than half of the cases. This highlights significant gaps between the accuracy of these two methods. Expanding the use of HPV tests is key to advancing toward the WHO’s global cervical cancer elimination targets – known as the 90-70-90 goals: 90% of girls vaccinated against HPV, 70% of women screened with a high-performance test, and 90% of women with lesions appropriately treated. Currently, 16 countries and territories in Latin America and the Caribbean have introduced HPV testing in their screening programs, although coverage has been limited by the high cost of the tests. The new access to molecular HPV tests through the Revolving Funds is part of PAHO’s Disease Elimination Initiative, which seeks to eliminate more than 30 diseases and related conditions in the Americas, including cervical cancer. It also forms part of the Better Care for NCDs initiative, designed to strengthen health systems and integrate prevention and control of these diseases into primary care. Unitaid’s cervical cancer elimination efforts in Latin America are made possible through contributions from the Government of Japan, alongside support from Unitaid’s other core donors Brazil, Chile, France, the Gates Foundation, Norway, Portugal, the Republic of Korea, Spain and the United Kingdom. About PAHO The Pan American Health Organization (PAHO) works with the countries of the Americas to improve the health and quality of life of its population. Founded in 1902, it is the world’s oldest international public health agency. It serves as the Regional Office of WHO for the Americas and is the specialized health agency of the Inter-American system (OAS). For more information about PAHO and its work, please visit: www.paho.org About Unitaid Unitaid saves lives by making new health products affordable and available in low- and middle-income countries. Collaborating with partners, Unitaid identifies innovative treatments, tackles market barriers, and quickly delivers solutions to those in need. Since 2006, Unitaid has unlocked over 150 health products, addressing HIV, TB, malaria, women’s and children’s health, and pandemic preparedness. Every year, these products benefit more than 300 million people. Unitaid is a hosted partnership of the World Health Organization. www.unitaid.org Media contacts PAHO: Sebastian Oliel: +1 202 316-5679 / Ashley Baldwin: + 1 202 340-4025 - mediateam@paho.org Unitaid: Kyle Wilkinson, +41 79 445 17 45, wilkinsonk@unitaid.who.int News Releases Cancer Adolescent Health Cervical cancer Immunization Brasil United States of America Puerto Rico Noncommunicable Diseases and Mental Health (NMH) It's Time to End Cervical Cancer Diseases Elimination Initiative Better Care for NCDs Initiative Human Papillomavirus (HPV) Vaccine Latest news Public university hospitals in the Americas to develop regional alliance to strengthen health systems 13 Nov 2026 Diabetes in the Americas: 112 million adults live with the disease as gaps in diagnosis and treatment persist 12 Nov 2026 PAHO secures first-ever region-wide access agreements for HPV testing across Latin America and the Caribbean, with support from Unitaid 11 Nov 2026 See all the news

A 高优先级公共卫生新闻

佛得角加紧努力消除宫颈癌:从倾听社区意见到制定拯救生命的解决方案-世卫组织|非洲区域办事处

Cabo Verde steps up efforts to eliminate cervical cancer: from listening to communities to developing solutions to save lives - WHO | Regional Office for Africa
新闻简报佛得角正在加紧努力,消除 宫颈癌 这一公共卫生问题,重点是改善筛查、诊断 和 治疗 的可及性。该倡议由卫生部牵头,得到世卫组织和西班牙政府的支持,其中包括在博阿维斯塔岛实施一个试点项目。2026 年 10 月,我们与包括卫生专业人员和社区领袖在内的 60 多个利益相关者进行了实地考察和磋商,以确定挑战并制定针对具体情况的解决方案。该项目旨在提高 HPV 筛查率、建立护理途径并提高医护人员的能力。2026 年 10 月 21 日在普拉亚举行的技术研讨会强调,要使国家努力与世卫组织加速消除 宫颈癌 的全球战略保持一致。该倡议定于 2027 年启动,目标对象为 1,500 名女性。世卫组织代表强调需要将知识转化为行动和可持续干预措施,包括能力建设和有效的转诊系统。
查看新闻五要素
时间
该倡议计划于2027年启动,2026年10月进行实地考察,并于2026年10月21日举办技术研讨会。发布日期为2026年11月10日。

佛得角,特别是博阿维斯塔岛,目标人口估计为 1,500 名女性。

规模、影响与风险
该倡议旨在支持制定国家加速计划,以加强卫生服务并将国际承诺转化为可持续的干预措施。它包括提高 HPV 筛查率、建立护理途径以及增强医护人员的能力。风险没有明确说明,但目标是减少 宫颈癌 作为公共卫生问题的程度。
应对、状态与不确定性
该倡议正处于规划和实施阶段,包括实地考察、技术研讨会和优先事项讨论。应对措施包括加强技术能力、更新临床方案、提供设备和消耗品以及制定沟通策略。来源状态已满,没有提及未解决的信息。
A high priorityPublic-health news

Cabo Verde steps up efforts to eliminate cervical cancer: from listening to communities to developing solutions to save lives - WHO | Regional Office for Africa

News Brief
Cabo Verde is intensifying its efforts to eliminate cervical cancer as a public health issue, with a focus on improving access to screening, diagnosis, and treatment. The initiative, led by the Ministry of Health and supported by WHO and the Spanish Government, includes a pilot project on Boa Vista Island. Field visits and consultations with over 60 stakeholders, including health professionals and community leaders, were conducted in October 2026 to identify challenges and develop context-specific solutions. The project aims to increase HPV screening uptake, establish care pathways, and improve healthcare staff capacity. A technical workshop on 21 October 2026 in Praia emphasized aligning national efforts with the WHO Global Strategy to Accelerate the Elimination of Cervical Cancer. The initiative is set to begin in 2027 and targets 1,500 women. The WHO representative highlighted the need for knowledge-to-action transformation and sustainable interventions, including capacity-building and effective referral systems.
Original source text (English)

Africa Accountability » Financial reports General Programme of Work Building a Healthier Africa, Together: A manifesto for transformation WHO Africa / Countries / Cabo Verde / Cabo Verde steps up efforts to eliminate cervical cancer: from listening to communities to developing solutions to save lives Cabo Verde steps up efforts to eliminate cervical cancer: from listening to communities to developing solutions to save lives 10 November 2026 | News Release | Reading time: 7 min Cabo Verde steps up efforts to eliminate cervical cancer: from listening to communities to developing solutions to save lives More pictures More images Praia, Cabo Verde – In an archipelagic country such as Cabo Verde, ensuring that all women have access to screening, diagnosis and timely treatment for cervical cancer requires responses tailored to different local realities. The country’s insularity, the challenges of referral and the need to ensure continuity of care make it particularly important to organise accessible, integrated services centred on women’s needs. It is against this backdrop that Cabo Verde is stepping up its efforts to accelerate the elimination of cervical cancer as a public health problem, through an initiative led by the Ministry of Health, with technical assistance from the World Health Organisation (WHO) and financial support from the Spanish Government, via the Spanish Agency for International Development Cooperation (AECID). The initiative aims to support the development of a National Acceleration Plan to consolidate the progress made, strengthen the capacity of health services and translate international commitments into concrete and sustainable interventions. It also includes strengthening technical capacity, updating clinical protocols, providing additional equipment and consumables, and developing a communication and awareness-raising strategy. Listening to bring about change: 12 field visits and around 60 people involved During October 2026, a WHO technical mission, working in close collaboration with the Ministry of Health, carried out 12 field visits and consulted around 60 people, including health professionals, community leaders, representatives from the private sector, non-governmental organisations, programmes run by the National Health Directorate and partner institutions. The mission prioritised a participatory approach, seeking to understand existing capacities, identifying the main constraints and gather input to develop solutions tailored to the national context. Boa Vista Island: bringing screening closer to women and strengthening local capacities On the island of Boa Vista, chosen for the implementation of the pilot project, visits were made to the Health Centre, the laboratory facility, the Maternal and Child Health Service and a private health clinic, which enabled a more in-depth analysis of the conditions necessary for implementing an organised screening pilot project. Discussions with community associations and other local stakeholders also highlighted the importance of community involvement in raising awareness and mobilising women. The project aims to increase the uptake of human papillomavirus (HPV) screening tests, strengthening mechanisms for identifying and inviting women to participate in screening, and establish a care pathway that ensures continuity of care from screening through to diagnosis and, where necessary, treatment. Key needs identified include improving the availability and use of data for decision-making, defining effective strategies to encourage women to take part in screening, and establishing clear flowcharts for follow-up, referral and monitoring. For Dr Risete Gomes, Health Officer for Boa Vista ai, this initiative opens up new prospects for the island, particularly in the field of women’s health. She emphasised the importance of ensuring clear, consistent and standardised communication between healthcare professionals and service users, in order to improve understanding of the screening programme, its benefits and the need for follow-up. She also highlighted the need to strengthen the capacity of healthcare staff, considering this essential to guaranteeing the quality of services and the sustainable implementation of the project. The experience on Boa Vista should enable the testing of mechanisms for organising screening, communication, registration and follow-up of women, yielding lessons that could guide the gradual expansion of the initiative to other islands. The initiative is scheduled to begin in 2027 and aims to reach a target population of an estimated 1,500 women. National leadership and commitment to elimination The technical workshop held on 21 October 2026 in the city of Praia provided an important opportunity to analyse the challenges identified and discuss the priorities for drawing up the National Acceleration Plan. On that occasion, Dr Heidy Teixeira, representing the Minister of Health, reaffirmed Cape Verde’s commitment to aligning the national response with the WHO Global Strategy to Accelerate the Elimination of Cervical Cancer as a Public Health Problem. Recognising that there is still a long way to go, she emphasised the need to accelerate interventions, consolidate the progress made and strengthen an integrated and sustainable national response. Among the advances achieved, she highlighted the fact that the target of 90 per cent HPV vaccination coverage among girls had been exceeded, and that boys had been included in the national vaccination strategy. These results provide an important foundation for stepping up efforts in the other components of the elimination strategy, particularly screening, early diagnosis and treatment. In her address, the WHO Representative in Cabo Verde, Dr Ann Lindstrand, congratulated the Ministry of Health on its leadership of the process and reaffirmed the Organisation’s commitment to continuing to support the country in realising this public health priority. The Representative emphasised the importance of ensuring that interventions reach all islands and communities, leaving no woman behind. “Eliminating cervical cancer as a public health problem is an achievable ambition. But it requires us to turn knowledge into action and commitments into concrete results.” She also emphasised that the provision of equipment, whilst essential, must be accompanied by capacity-building for healthcare professionals, the establishment of effective referral pathways, access to treatment, and information systems capable of monitoring outcomes. The financial partnership with AECID represents a significant contribution to the realisation of this initiative. During his address at the workshop, Fernando Regúlez, a representative of AECID, highlighted the importance of the financial partnership between the Spanish Government, the WHO and Cape Verde in supporting national efforts to eliminate cervical cancer. This collaboration demonstrates the value of strategic partnerships in mobilising resources and strengthening national capacities, helping to translate public health priorities into concrete actions. One of the highlights of this workshop was the symbolic handover of HPV DNA test kits, equipment and consumables to support the roll-out of the pilot project in Boa Vista. The technical mission in October 2026 made it possible to gather evidence, identify operational needs and mobilise different stakeholders around a common vision: to build a more organised, accessible and sustainable national response. The next steps include consolidating the National Acceleration Plan, the operational preparation of the pilot project in Boa Vista, training professionals, defining mechanisms for reaching out to women, and developing tools for registration, referral and monitoring. The true impact of this initiative will be measured by its ability to translate these priorities into concrete improvements in access to and the quality of services, ensuring that every woman identified can undergo screening, receive her results and benefit from timely follow-up and treatment where necessary. With national leadership, technical support from the WHO, financial partnership from AECID and the involvement of health professionals and communities, Cape Verde is laying the foundations to accelerate the elimination of cervical cancer as a public health problem. Because eliminating cervical cancer also means reducing inequalities, protecting lives and ensuring that no woman is left behind. B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

研究揭示了赞比亚西部 HPV 感染与年龄相关的独特驱动因素 - Bioengineer.org

Study Uncovers Distinct Age-Related Drivers of HPV Infection in Western Zambia - Bioengineer.org
新闻简报A study conducted in Western Province, Zambia, between October 2025 and January 2026 found comparable rates of high-risk human papillomavirus (HPV) infection across age groups, but distinct risk factors were identified for different demographics. The research, published on November 12, 2026, enrolled 1,132 women attending cervical cancer screening clinics and used the Hologic Panther platform with transcription-mediated amplification to detect HPV genotypes. It also administered structured questionnaires to gather sociodemographic, behavioral, and reproductive data. The study noted that HPV prevalence was 33.6 percent among women aged 40 years and younger and 33.4 percent among those aged 41 years and older. Spatial variations were observed, with higher infection rates in Mwandi district. The findings emphasize the need for age-specific prevention initiatives, including HPV vaccination, cervical cancer screening, health education, and treatment services, to effectively address the virus's impact in the region.
查看新闻五要素
时间
该研究于2025年10月至2026年1月期间进行,新闻于2026年11月12日发布。
地点与对象
该研究以赞比亚西部省的女性为对象,专门对那些到 宫颈癌 筛查诊所就诊的女性进行了检查。共有 1,132 名参与者。
事件
一项横断面研究显示,不同年龄组的 高危型HPV 感染率相当,但确定了不同人口统计数据的不同危险因素。Bioengineer.org 报道了这一发现。
规模、影响与风险
HPV prevalence was 33.6 percent among women aged 40 years and younger, and 33.4 percent among those aged 41 years and older. The study noted spatial variations, with significantly elevated HPV rates in Mwandi district. The research highlights the need for age-specific prevention strategies.
应对、状态与不确定性
该研究使用混合效应逻辑回归模型来分析通过 Hologic Panther 平台和结构化问卷收集的数据。研究结果表明,应针对每个年龄段的独特驱动因素制定干预措施。

Study Uncovers Distinct Age-Related Drivers of HPV Infection in Western Zambia - Bioengineer.org

News Brief
A study conducted in Western Province, Zambia, between October 2025 and January 2026 found comparable rates of high-risk human papillomavirus (HPV) infection across age groups, but distinct risk factors were identified for different demographics. The research, published on November 12, 2026, enrolled 1,132 women attending cervical cancer screening clinics and used the Hologic Panther platform with transcription-mediated amplification to detect HPV genotypes. It also administered structured questionnaires to gather sociodemographic, behavioral, and reproductive data. The study noted that HPV prevalence was 33.6 percent among women aged 40 years and younger and 33.4 percent among those aged 41 years and older. Spatial variations were observed, with higher infection rates in Mwandi district. The findings emphasize the need for age-specific prevention initiatives, including HPV vaccination, cervical cancer screening, health education, and treatment services, to effectively address the virus's impact in the region.

The study, carried out between October 2025 and January 2026, enrolled 1,132 participants using consecutive sampling to examine the prevalence and determinants of infection. Overall, the researchers detected an HPV prevalence of 33.6 percent, noting virtually identical burdens among younger and older demographics. Specifically, women aged 40 years and younger exhibited an infection rate of 33.6 percent, whereas participants aged 41 years and older had an infection rate of 33.4 percent. To obtain these clinical measurements, healthcare teams collected exfoliated cervical specimens from the participants and screened for high-risk HPV genotypes using the Hologic Panther platform paired with transcription-mediated amplification (TMA). In parallel, investigators administered structured questionnaires to capture detailed sociodemographic, behavioural, and reproductive information from each woman. To account for potential clustering of outcomes across different health facilities in the province, the analytical team applied mixed-effects logistic regression models to identify factors independently associated with viral infection. The statistical analyses revealed that the independent determinants of HPV infection diverged significantly depending on participant age. Among women aged 40 years and younger, infection was independently associated with a higher number of lifetime sexual partners, high parity, lack of HPV vaccination, contraceptive use, low income, single marital status, and attendance at rural healthcare facilities (P < 0.05). In contrast, among women aged 41 years and older, the independent risk factors comprised high parity, low HPV knowledge, unemployment, low income, and broader geographic location (P < 0.05). Beyond age-stratified predictors, the research group documented spatial variations in infection frequency across the study region, observing significantly elevated HPV rates within Mwandi district. The investigators noted that Zambia continues to experience one of the highest cervical cancer burdens globally, where human papillomavirus persists as a leading cause of cervical malignancy in low- and middle-income settings. In interpreting these patterns, the authors indicated that the substantial regional burden of HIV and ongoing transmission dynamics may also play a role in sustaining high levels of HPV infection across age groups. Reflecting on these contrasting associations, the study authors emphasize that interventions should be tailored to address the unique drivers identified within each age demographic. Rather than applying a uniform approach, the findings support integrated, age-specific prevention initiatives that combine HPV vaccination, routine cervical cancer screening, health education, and clinical treatment services to effectively curb the burden of HPV infection and its long-term health consequences across Western Province. 连云港研究发现 人乳头瘤病毒 总体感染率为 18% - Bioengineer.org

Lianyungang Study Finds Eighteen Percent Overall Human Papillomavirus Infection Rate - Bioengineer.org
新闻简报A study conducted in Lianyungang, Northern Jiangsu Province, China, between 2021 and 2025 found an overall human papillomavirus (HPV) infection rate of 18.57 percent among 50,279 women at Lianyungang Maternal and Child Health Hospital, with 9,336 positive cases diagnosed. The research revealed a bimodal age distribution, with the highest positivity rates among women aged 56 years or older (26.05 percent) and those aged 25 years or younger (23.56 percent). HPV52 was the most prevalent genotype at 3.62 percent, followed by HPV16 at 3.01 percent and HPV58 at 2.46 percent. Single infections accounted for 75.83 percent of cases. The study also noted a significant increase in HPV53 prevalence from 1.51 percent in 2021 to 2.72 percent in 2025. These findings aim to support regional cervical cancer prevention and control strategies, highlighting the importance of understanding HPV genotype distribution and infection patterns for public health planning.
查看新闻五要素
时间
该研究于2021年至2025年间进行,发表日期为2026年11月12日(N1、N23、N24)。
地点与对象
该研究以中国苏北省连云港市为研究对象,涉及连云港市妇幼保健院(N1、N7、N9)的 50,279 名妇女。
事件
A retrospective analysis of HPV genotyping records identified an overall infection rate of 18.57 percent among women in Lianyungang, with 9,336 positive cases diagnosed between 2021 and 2025 (N1, N9).
规模、影响与风险
The study found a bimodal age distribution of HPV infection, with the highest positivity rate among women aged 56 years or older at 26.05 percent (1,017 of 3,904) and among women aged 25 years or younger at 23.56 percent (810 of 3,438). HPV52 was the most frequent variant at 3.62 percent (1,822 cases), followed by HPV16 at 3.01 percent (1,514 cases) and HPV58 at 2.46 percent (1,239 cases). Single infections predominated, accounting for 75.83 percent of cases (N1, N4, N15, N17, N19).
应对、状态与不确定性
The research aimed to provide localized epidemiological data to guide cervical cancer prevention and control strategies. It included longitudinal trends and genotype distribution analysis, with HPV53 prevalence increasing significantly from 1.51 percent in 2021 to 2.72 percent in 2025 (N6, N10, N12).

Evaluation of viral infection patterns indicated that single infections predominated across the positive cohort, accounting for 75.83 percent of cases with 7,080 infected women displaying a single viral genotype. Discover more Check Symptoms Book a Checkup Read Med Journals Find Cancer Care Analysis of age-stratified positivity demonstrated a bimodal distribution across demographic groups, reaching a first peak of 23.56 percent (810 of 3,438) among women aged 25 years or younger and a second peak of 26.05 percent (1,017 of 3,904) among women aged 56 years or older. The clinical objective of the investigation centered on analyzing both the prevalence and genotype distribution of human papillomavirus infection across the population of Lianyungang, located in Northern Jiangsu Province, China. By systematically characterizing these viral patterns, the research was designed to provide localized epidemiological data necessary to support the prevention and treatment of cervical cancer and related diseases throughout the municipality. Discover more Study Biology infections Infection infection Buy Maps To evaluate these dynamics, the study design relied on a retrospective analysis of HPV genotyping records collected from 50,279 women at Lianyungang Maternal and Child Health Hospital between 2021 and 2025. The methodological evaluation systematically examined four distinct epidemiological parameters across the five-year surveillance period: overall infection rates, viral genotype distribution, age-specific positivity across demographic cohorts, and multi-year longitudinal trends. The research team concluded that the investigation provides the largest-scale dataset compiled to date regarding recent human papillomavirus epidemiology in Lianyungang. The authors noted that these localized empirical findings are intended to guide public health authorities in the development and implementation of regional cervical cancer prevention and disease control strategies. In evaluating the composition of positive cases, the researchers observed that solitary viral detection predominated over concurrent strains. The age-stratified data demonstrated that infection risk was concentrated at opposite extremes of the tested demographic spectrum. Women aged 56 years and older recorded the highest positivity rate across all evaluated groups at 26.05 percent (1,017 of 3,904), exceeding the initial peak of 23.56 percent (810 of 3,438) identified among women aged 25 years or younger. Regarding viral heterogeneity across the full sample of 50,279 women, the genotype hierarchy revealed distinct frequencies among dominant variants. HPV52 emerged as the most prevalent genotype at 3.62 percent (1,822 cases), followed by HPV16 at 3.01 percent (1,514 cases) and HPV58 at 2.46 percent (1,239 cases). HPV53 and HPV39 accounted for 1.97 percent (989 cases) and 1.72 percent (864 cases), respectively. In addition to these leading variants, the evaluation specifically classified HPV81 as the most common low-risk human papillomavirus genotype, affecting 1.70 percent of women (856 cases). 阴道肿瘤与既往生殖器病变表现出高 HPV 基因型一致性 - Bioengineer.org

Vaginal Neoplasia Shows High HPV Genotype Concordance With Prior Genital Lesions - Bioengineer.org
新闻简报A study published on November 11, 2026, by Bioengineer.org investigated the concordance of human papillomavirus (HPV) genotypes between vaginal neoplasia and prior genital tract lesions. Conducted at University Hospital Královské Vinohrady in Prague, the research analyzed 72 women with histologically confirmed HPV-associated vaginal intraepithelial neoplasia (VaIN2+). Among them, 54.2 percent had a history of prior HPV-related lower genital tract neoplasia. The study found that 96.4 percent of evaluable cases shared at least one HPV genotype between initial and subsequent lesions, with 82.1 percent showing complete concordance. HPV16 was the most common genotype, detected in 51.8 percent of cases, and was independently associated with severe VaIN3+ lesions. The findings suggest a clinical risk of developing VaIN3+ in women with prior HPV-related lower genital tract disease, but the study acknowledges limitations in determining whether the lesions originated from the same virus or new infections. The research team recommends extended long-term surveillance for such patients.
查看新闻五要素
时间
该研究使用长达 31.8 年前的存档标本进行,新闻报道的发布日期为 2026 年 11 月 11 日(N1、N23、N24)。
地点与对象
该研究在捷克共和国布拉格克拉洛夫斯克维诺拉迪大学医院进行,涉及 72 名经组织学证实患有 HPV 相关 VaIN2+ 或更高(N7、N8、N9)的女性的 队列研究。
事件
A tissue-based study found that 96.4 percent of evaluable cases shared at least one HPV genotype between initial and subsequent neoplastic sites, with 82.1 percent showing complete concordance (N2, N14, N15).
规模、影响与风险
The study identified 72 women with HPV-associated vaginal intraepithelial neoplasia (VaIN2+), and 51.8 percent of cases had HPV16 as the predominant genotype. The findings suggest a clinical risk of developing VaIN3+ lesions in women with prior HPV-associated lower genital tract neoplasia (N4, N12, N13).
应对、状态与不确定性
该研究得出的结论是,共享的病毒基因型与病毒的长期持续存在或重新激活相一致,但方法学的局限性阻碍了关于再感染或克隆起源的明确结论。研究团队强调了长期监测(N17、N18、N19)的必要性。

Vaginal Neoplasia Shows High HPV Genotype Concordance With Prior Genital Lesions - Bioengineer.org

News Brief
A study published on November 11, 2026, by Bioengineer.org investigated the concordance of human papillomavirus (HPV) genotypes between vaginal neoplasia and prior genital tract lesions. HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER Saturday, November 14, 2026 No Result View All Result Login HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs Lecturer PhD Studentship Postdoc Research Assistant EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs Lecturer PhD Studentship Postdoc Research Assistant EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER No Result View All Result No Result View All Result Home NEWS Science News Health Vaginal Neoplasia Shows High HPV Genotype Concordance With Prior Genital Lesions by November 11, 2026 in Health Reading Time: 3 mins read 0 Vaginal Neoplasia Shows High HPV Genotype Concordance With Prior Genital Lesions Share on FacebookShare on TwitterShare on LinkedinShare on RedditShare on Telegram Direct tissue-based testing of archived clinical specimens has revealed that secondary vaginal intraepithelial lesions share identical human papillomavirus genotypes with earlier lower genital tract neoplasia in the vast majority of cases. In an investigation tracking paired tissue samples across intervals spanning up to 31.8 years, 96.4 percent of evaluable cases shared at least one viral genotype between initial and subsequent neoplastic sites. The findings demonstrate that women diagnosed with high-grade vaginal intraepithelial neoplasia frequently harbor the exact viral types identified in their previous genital tract disease, providing concrete tissue-level evidence consistent with long-term viral persistence or reactivation. Women who undergo treatment for human papillomavirus-associated neoplasia in the cervix or other lower genital tract locations face a well-recognized ongoing risk of later developing vaginal intraepithelial neoplasia, categorized clinically as VaIN. Despite this established clinical vulnerability, direct molecular data confirming whether secondary vaginal lesions arise from the same persistent virus or from new, independent viral exposures have historically remained limited. To evaluate this question, researchers utilized archived pathological specimens to perform lesion-specific human papillomavirus genotyping, comparing tissue from advanced vaginal lesions against paired historical specimens from earlier lower genital tract neoplasia. The study was designed as a retrospective, single-center cohort investigation conducted at University Hospital Královské Vinohrady in Prague, Czech Republic, operating under institutional ethics approval reference number EK-VP/34/00/2025. Investigators identified a cohort of 72 eligible women with histologically confirmed and human papillomavirus-associated VaIN2+, a classification encompassing grade 2 or grade 3 vaginal intraepithelial neoplasia as well as invasive vaginal carcinoma. Among the 72 enrolled patients, 39 women, representing 54.2 percent of the study cohort, had documented medical histories of prior human papillomavirus-associated lower genital tract neoplasia. Discover more Find an OBGYN Find Cancer Care See an OB-GYN genotypes Order DNA Kits Laboratory evaluations focused on archived formalin-fixed, paraffin-embedded tissue blocks processed through a multiplex real-time polymerase chain reaction assay to identify specific viral genotypes. Valid vaginal lesion genotyping was successfully achieved in 56 women, comprising 30 patients who had a history of prior lower genital tract neoplasia and 26 patients presenting without preceding neoplastic disease. Across all lesions with valid testing results, human papillomavirus genotype 16 predominated, detected in 51.8 percent of cases. Furthermore, statistical analysis demonstrated that human papillomavirus 16 was independently associated with the development of severe VaIN3+ lesions, carrying an adjusted odds ratio of 3.84 with a 95 percent confidence interval of 1.22 to 12.93 and a p-value of 0.024. Direct genotype concordance was specifically assessed in a subset of 28 patients for whom paired tissue specimens from both the original and secondary lesions were available, evaluable, and positive for human papillomavirus. Within this paired group, complete viral concordance was established in 23 women (82.1 percent), partial concordance in four women (14.3 percent), and an absence of concordance in only one woman (3.6 percent). When examining exploratory clinical contrasts, combined positivity for human papillomavirus 16 and 18 occurred more frequently among women with prior neoplasia compared to those without (66.7 percent versus 38.5 percent; p = 0.059), as did VaIN3+ diagnoses (66.7 percent versus 42.3 percent; p = 0.106); however, neither difference remained statistically significant below the 0.05 threshold following adjustment for multiplicity. The research team concluded that the pronounced frequency of shared viral genotypes strongly aligns with long-term viral persistence or subsequent reactivation of the primary human papillomavirus genotype over decades. Nevertheless, the investigators highlighted essential methodological limitations: reinfection with the identical viral genotype over time cannot be definitively excluded, and the analysis did not evaluate whether the paired lesions shared a common clonal origin. Given the extended latency intervals observed between primary and secondary neoplastic diagnoses, the study authors emphasized that these findings reinforce the necessity of extended, long-term surveillance protocols for women who have received treatment for high-grade cervical and lower genital tract lesions. View five news elements
Time
The study was conducted using archived specimens from up to 31.8 years prior, with the publication date of the news report being November 11, 2026 (N1, N23, N24).
Location and population
The research was carried out at University Hospital Královské Vinohrady in Prague, Czech Republic, involving a cohort of 72 women with histologically confirmed and HPV-associated VaIN2+ or higher (N7, N8, N9).
Event
A tissue-based study found that 96.4 percent of evaluable cases shared at least one HPV genotype between initial and subsequent neoplastic sites, with 82.1 percent showing complete concordance (N2, N14, N15).
Scale, impact and risk
The study identified 72 women with HPV-associated vaginal intraepithelial neoplasia (VaIN2+), and 51.8 percent of cases had HPV16 as the predominant genotype. The findings suggest a clinical risk of developing VaIN3+ lesions in women with prior HPV-associated lower genital tract neoplasia (N4, N12, N13).
Response, status and uncertainty
The study concluded that shared viral genotypes align with long-term viral persistence or reactivation, but methodological limitations prevent definitive conclusions about reinfection or clonal origin. The research team emphasized the need for long-term surveillance (N17, N18, N19).

俄罗斯将大幅增加 人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗 的产量 - The Pharma Letter

Russia to sharply increase production of human papillomavirus vaccines - The Pharma Letter
新闻简报Russia plans to significantly boost the production of human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccines to meet domestic demand and replace imported vaccines in the market, according to a report by The Pharma Letter’s local correspondent published on 9 November 2026. The article highlights that Nanolek, a major Russian vaccine manufacturer, aims to produce over 3 million doses annually by 2027. This initiative is part of broader efforts to enhance vaccine self-sufficiency. The report also references earlier updates from 24 June 2026 and 26 January 2026, indicating ongoing developments in Russia’s vaccine production strategy. However, the full article is only accessible to registered users, and the content is based on a syndicated summary. No confirmed cases, deaths, or health impacts related to HPV are reported in the evidence provided.
查看新闻五要素
时间
据《Pharma Letter》当地通讯员于 2026 年 11 月 9 日报道,俄罗斯计划到 2027 年大幅增加 HPV 疫苗 的产量。该文章还引用了 2026 年 6 月 24 日和 2026 年 1 月 26 日的更新。
地点与对象
俄罗斯,重点供应国产疫苗,并在俄罗斯市场替代进口疫苗。
事件
俄罗斯打算增加人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)疫苗的产量以满足国内需求并替代进口的疫苗。这是《医药通讯》当地记者报道的。
规模、影响与风险
The planned output is expected to exceed 3 million doses per year by 2027, with Nanolek, one of Russia's largest vaccine manufacturers, playing a key role. The article does not report any confirmed cases, deaths, or health-system impacts related to HPV.
应对、状态与不确定性
文章报道了俄罗斯计划增加 HPV 疫苗 的产量,但没有提供有关调查、测试或实施现状的详细信息。内容基于联合摘要,未经注册无法访问完整的原创文章。

B routine priorityPublic-health news

News article (Chinese original; verified English title unavailable)

Original title (Chinese)
HPV16/18到底有多凶?从感染到癌变的完整过程,看完一身冷汗
News Brief
The supplied evidence does not clearly report the event time in verified English. The supplied source does not clearly identify the location or affected population. The supplied evidence does not clearly report the central event in verified English. The supplied evidence does not clearly report the scale, impact, or risk in verified English. The supplied source does not clearly report the response status or unresolved information.
Original source text (Chinese)
新闻 体育 汽车 房产 旅游 教育 时尚 科技 财经 娱乐 HPV16/18到底有多凶?从感染到癌变的完整过程,看完一身冷汗 2026-11-13 16:00 来源: 孚瑞坦 发布于:福建省 在所有诱发宫颈癌的高危型HPV中,HPV16、18是公认的“致癌毒王”。临床数据显示,超70%的宫颈癌病例都与这两种亚型的持续感染相关,它们致病力更强、进展速度更快,是女性宫颈健康的头号隐形威胁。很多人只知道HPV会致癌,却不清楚病毒从入侵到最终引发癌变,究竟经历了怎样的过程。 第一步:黏膜破损入侵,感染毫无症状 HPV病毒不进入血液循环,专挑皮肤黏膜的微小破损处入侵,直达宫颈鳞状上皮的基底层细胞。这个阶段没有任何不适症状,大多数感染者完全不知情。如果自身免疫力足够强,大多数感染会在1-2年内被自然清除,属于一过性感染。 第二步:持续感染形成,病毒实现免疫逃逸 小部分的HPV感染无法被人体自行清除,发展为持续性感染。HPV具备极强的免疫逃逸特性,感染过程中不会触发强烈的免疫反应,病毒可以长期潜伏在细胞内不断复制。当高危型HPV持续感染超过2年,宫颈细胞发生病变的风险会显著升高。 第三步:基因整合,癌变“开关”被激活 HPV16、18的致癌性之所以突出,核心在于其基因整合能力。病毒会将自身基因组嵌入宿主细胞的染色体中,导致调控病毒复制的E2基因失活,解除对E6、E7致癌基因的抑制。大量表达的E6、E7蛋白会分别灭活人体的p53、Rb两大抑癌蛋白,让细胞失去增殖调控,开始无限分裂、异常分化,癌变进程就此正式启动。 第四步:宫颈上皮内瘤变,癌前的预警信号 异常增殖的细胞会逐步引发宫颈上皮内瘤变(CIN),按照病变程度分为CINI、CINII、CINIII三级,级别越高,距离浸润癌越近。这个阶段大多无明显症状,多数患者仅在筛查中发现异常。若在此阶段及时干预,阻断病毒、逆转病变,就能有效阻止癌症发生。 第五步:进展为浸润性宫颈癌 如果CIN阶段未被发现和干预,病变细胞突破上皮基底膜,侵入宫颈间质,就发展为浸润性宫颈癌。此时可能出现接触性出血、不规则阴道流血、异常排液等症状,但往往已错过最佳干预窗口。 从HPV感染到宫颈癌通常需要5-10年甚至更久,这也为临床干预留下了充足的窗口。《中国子宫颈癌三级规范化防治蓝皮书》明确指出,清除高危HPV持续性感染,是无创临床干预的关键时机,既能避免病情进展,也可减少不必要的有创治疗。 针对高危HPV感染的干预,合规有效的医疗器械产品为临床提供了新的选择。孚瑞坦抗人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)凝胶敷料是国家二类医疗器械,采用IgY生物抗体靶向技术,通过“抗体鸡尾酒疗法”多靶点作用:中和清除游离病毒,作用于受感染的宿主细胞,协同改善生殖道环境,可阻断生殖道高危HPV感染、降低病毒载量、防止宫颈病变发生。 一项纳入120例高危HPV感染合并CIN患者的随机对照临床研究显示,使用孚瑞坦抗HPV凝胶敷料治疗后,HPV转阴率可达88.33%,疗效显著优于传统干扰素方案,可有效阻断高危HPV感染进程,为宫颈健康提供可靠防护。[1] HPV感染并不可怕,可怕的是忽视筛查与干预。定期进行HPV分型检测,发现高危感染后及时选择合规、有临床数据支撑的干预方案,是守护宫颈健康的核心关键。 展开全文 品牌简介 孚瑞坦,专业健康品牌,深耕病毒领域。品牌强国医疗器械行业优选成员单位。现产品体系涵盖HPV、HSV等病毒防治需求。拥有以IgY(卵黄免疫球蛋白)为核心的多项专利,致力于用前沿生物科技,惠及全球亿万家庭。 温馨提示:请仔细阅读产品说明书或者在医务人员的指导下购买和使用。本文旨在健康科普,具体诊疗请遵医嘱。 参考文献: [1]苏玲.孚瑞坦抗人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)凝胶敷料治疗高危HPV感染合并CIN的临床疗效分析[J].中文科技期刊数据库(全文版)医药卫生,2024(5):0186-0189返回搜狐,查看更多 阅读 () 平台声明 该文观点仅代表作者本人,搜狐号系信息发布平台,搜狐仅提供信息存储空间服务。

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

柬埔寨发起全国 HPV 运动,保护女孩免受 宫颈癌 感染

Cambodia launches nationwide HPV campaign to protect girls from cervical cancer
新闻简报Cambodia has launched a nationwide human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination campaign aimed at protecting girls from cervical cancer, as reported in a joint press release issued on November 11, 2026. The initiative targets girls aged 13 to 14 and offers catch-up doses for those aged 10 to 12 who may have missed previous vaccinations. The campaign will be implemented in two phases from November to December 2026, with vaccines distributed through schools, community centers, and local health facilities. This effort builds on the successful integration of the HPV vaccine into Cambodia's National Immunization Program in 2023. The World Health Organization (WHO) representative, Marianna Trias, emphasized that HPV vaccination is one of the most effective tools for preventing cervical cancer. Gavi, the Vaccine Alliance, has committed over 3 million U.S. dollars in funding to support the campaign, which aims to protect more than 700,000 girls. According to the press release, HPV vaccination is estimated to avert 13 deaths per 1,000 children vaccinated. The campaign is part of Cambodia's broader strategy to safeguard the health of future generations and strengthen links between adolescent girls and essential health services.
查看新闻五要素
时间
该活动于2026年11月启动,第一阶段于11月启动,第二阶段于2026年12月启动(N7)。该新闻稿于周三发布,活动启动活动于周二举行(N1、N2、N8)。
地点与对象
该活动在柬埔寨全国范围内开展,针对 13 至 14 岁的女孩,并为 10 至 12 岁的女孩(N2、N6)提供补种疫苗。
事件
正如联合新闻稿 (N2) 中所述,柬埔寨发起了全国性 HPV 疫苗接种 运动,以保护女孩免受 宫颈癌 感染。
规模、影响与风险
该运动旨在保护超过 700,000 名女孩,估计每 1,000 名接种疫苗的儿童可避免 13 人死亡 (N13)。
应对、状态与不确定性
该活动正在进行中,全球疫苗免疫联盟提供财政支持,并通过学校、社区中心和当地卫生设施(N10、N14、N16)提供 疫苗。

Cambodia launches nationwide HPV campaign to protect girls from cervical cancer

Original source text (English)
Cambodia launches nationwide HPV campaign to protect girls from cervical cancer-Xinhua Cambodia launches nationwide HPV campaign to protect girls from cervical cancer Source: Xinhua Editor: huaxia 2026-11-11 12:32:45 PHNOM PENH, Nov. 11 (Xinhua) -- Cambodia has launched a nationwide human papillomavirus (HPV) Multi-Age Cohort and catch-up vaccination campaign to expand cervical cancer protection for adolescent girls, according to a joint press release issued Wednesday. Cervical cancer remains a major public health challenge in Cambodia, ranking as the second leading cancer among women of all ages. Because nearly all cervical cancer cases are linked to persistent infection with high-risk strains of HPV, vaccination stands as one of the most effective preventive measures available. Building on the successful integration of the HPV vaccine into Cambodia's National Immunization Program in 2023, the new campaign will run alongside routine vaccinations for nine-year-old girls. The initiative provides free HPV vaccines to all girls aged 13 to 14, while offering catch-up doses for girls aged 10 to 12 who may have missed their routine vaccinations between 2023 and 2025. To maximize coverage, the nationwide campaign will be rolled out in two phases from November to December 2026. At the campaign launch on Tuesday, Cambodian Minister of Health Chheang Ra reaffirmed the country's commitment to protecting future generations. "Every girl deserves protection from cervical cancer," he said. "By expanding HPV vaccination opportunities across the country, we are ensuring that girls who missed previous opportunities can still benefit from this safe, effective, and life-saving vaccine." The campaign will utilize a coordinated approach, delivering vaccines through schools, community centers, and local health facilities. Marianna Trias, the World Health Organization representative to Cambodia, said HPV vaccination is one of the most effective tools for preventing cervical cancer. "By expanding vaccination opportunities for girls up to age 14, Cambodia is accelerating progress towards eliminating cervical cancer while safeguarding the health and well-being of future generations," she said. HPV vaccination is among the most impactful interventions, averting an estimated 13 deaths for every 1,000 children vaccinated, according to the joint press release. Supporting these efforts, Gavi, the Vaccine Alliance, has committed substantial financial resources to ensure the initiative's success. "With more than 3 million U.S. dollars in funding for this campaign, which aims to protect more than 700,000 girls in schools and communities, Gavi reaffirms our commitment to support Cambodia in its efforts to build stronger links between adolescent girls and essential health services," said Priya Gajraj, Gavi's director of core countries. ■ Cambodia launches nationwide HPV campaign to protect girls from cervical cancer
View five news elements
Time
The campaign was launched in November 2026, with the first phase starting in November and the second phase in December 2026 (N7). The press release was issued on Wednesday, and the campaign launch event occurred on Tuesday (N1, N2, N8).
Location and population
The campaign is nationwide in Cambodia, targeting girls aged 13 to 14 and offering catch-up doses for girls aged 10 to 12 (N2, N6).
Event
Cambodia launched a nationwide HPV vaccination campaign to protect girls from cervical cancer, as stated in a joint press release (N2).
Scale, impact and risk
The campaign aims to protect more than 700,000 girls, with an estimated 13 deaths averted per 1,000 children vaccinated (N13).
Response, status and uncertainty
The campaign is ongoing, with Gavi providing financial support and vaccines being delivered through schools, community centers, and local health facilities (N10, N14, N16).

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

医院研究显示最常见的 HPV 毒株病变风险较低

Most Common HPV Strain Shows Lower Lesion Risk in Hospital Study
新闻简报A retrospective cross-sectional study conducted at a tertiary medical center in Henan, China, analyzed HPV genotype distribution and cervical lesion risk among 9,941 women aged 14 or older. The study found that while HPV52 was the most prevalent strain, it was not statistically linked to high-grade cervical lesions. In contrast, HPV16 and HPV18 showed a strong independent association with such lesions, with an adjusted odds ratio of 10.67. Among 850 patients who underwent histological examination, 117 were diagnosed with cervical intraepithelial neoplasia grade 2 or worse, representing 13.8 percent of the subgroup. The research highlighted that HPV prevalence varied significantly based on clinical context, with higher rates among women undergoing abnormal-result re-examination. The findings suggest that genotype-specific risk stratification and screening age boundaries need further evaluation in future studies. The study was published on November 9, 2026, and the data was collected from 2025.
查看新闻五要素
时间
该研究从2025年开始进行,发布日期为2026年11月9日。
地点与对象
该研究在中国河南的一家三级医疗中心进行,涉及 9,941 名 14 岁或以上的女性,其中 7,116 人接受机会性筛查,2,825 人接受先前异常结果的复查。
事件
一项回顾性横断面研究发现,最常见的 HPV 毒株 HPV52 与高级别宫颈病变没有统计学相关性,而 HPV16 和 HPV18 显示与此类病变有很强的独立联系。
规模、影响与风险
Among 850 patients who underwent histological examination, 117 were diagnosed with cervical intraepithelial neoplasia grade 2 or worse, representing 13.8 percent of the subgroup. HPV16 and HPV18 had an adjusted odds ratio of 10.67 for high-grade lesions, while HPV52 had an adjusted odds ratio of 1.23, which was not statistically significant.
应对、状态与不确定性
该研究通过分析基因型分布和组织学风险,解决了临床 流行病学 的空白。它指出,基因型特异性风险分层策略和筛查计划的年龄上限需要在未来的研究中进一步评估。

Most Common HPV Strain Shows Lower Lesion Risk in Hospital Study

News Brief

The study found that while HPV52 was the most prevalent strain, it was not statistically linked to high-grade cervical lesions. Original source text (English)
HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER Saturday, November 14, 2026 No Result View All Result Login HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs Lecturer PhD Studentship Postdoc Research Assistant EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs Lecturer PhD Studentship Postdoc Research Assistant EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER No Result View All Result No Result View All Result Home NEWS Science News Biology Most Common HPV Strain Shows Lower Lesion Risk in Hospital Study by November 9, 2026 in Biology Reading Time: 3 mins read 0 Most Common HPV Strain Shows Lower Lesion Risk in Hospital Study Share on FacebookShare on TwitterShare on LinkedinShare on RedditShare on Telegram A retrospective cross-sectional study conducted at a tertiary medical center in Henan, China, has found that the most frequently detected human papillomavirus strain among gynecology attendees is not the genotype most strongly associated with high-grade cervical lesions. While HPV52 emerged as the most prevalent viral type across the patient cohort, its statistical association with cervical intraepithelial neoplasia grade 2 or worse was not statistically significant. In contrast, HPV16 and HPV18 demonstrated the strongest independent link to high-grade lesions despite being detected less frequently than HPV52. The investigation addressed an important gap in clinical epidemiology surrounding opportunistic hospital testing. China’s organized cervical screening framework targets women aged 35 to 64 years, yet substantial volumes of HPV testing occur opportunistically in tertiary hospital settings among women outside this standard age bracket. Because the genotype-specific and age-stratified epidemiology of this hospital population had remained poorly characterized, researchers analyzed clinical and laboratory records from 2025 to clarify genotype distributions and histological risks. The study cohort comprised 9,941 women aged 14 years or older who underwent genotyping for 23 distinct HPV types, evaluated either alone or alongside liquid-based cytology. Within this overall population, 7,116 individuals presented for opportunistic screening, whereas 2,825 attended for the re-examination of prior abnormal results. Histological diagnoses were available for a subgroup of 850 women, allowing investigators to calculate odds ratios for cervical intraepithelial neoplasia grade 2 or worse and establish absolute disease risks using naive calculations paired with verification-bias-adjusted models. Across the entire cohort, overall HPV prevalence was measured at 23.2 percent, with a 95 percent confidence interval spanning 22.3 to 24.0 percent. High-risk HPV prevalence reached 17.8 percent using a standard 14-type assay panel and 16.3 percent when restricted strictly to International Agency for Research on Cancer Group 1 classifications. Clinical context heavily influenced detection rates: women undergoing abnormal-result re-examination exhibited an HPV prevalence of 44.5 percent, roughly three times higher than the 14.7 percent observed among those attending opportunistic screening. Age-specific prevalence displayed a distinct bimodal pattern rather than a continuous decline across older age groups. Positivity peaked at 52.2 percent among individuals aged 20 years or younger, declined to 19.1 percent among women aged 30 to 39 years, and rose again to 40.2 percent among patients aged 60 years or older. Across the cohort, the three most frequently identified genotypes were HPV52, HPV16, and HPV58, highlighting substantial viral diversity across different generations of patients. Among the 850 patients who underwent histological examination, 117 were diagnosed with cervical intraepithelial neoplasia grade 2 or worse, representing 13.8 percent of the biopsied subgroup. More than half of these high-grade cases—54.7 percent—carried HPV16 or HPV18. Multivariate analysis confirmed that HPV16 and HPV18 infection was independently associated with high-grade lesions, yielding an adjusted odds ratio of 10.67 compared to patients negative for all 14 high-risk types. By contrast, the most widespread strain, HPV52, showed a genotype-specific adjusted odds ratio of 1.23, an association that did not achieve statistical significance. Estimates of absolute disease risk per genotype-positive individual further reinforced these divergent clinical trajectories. Modeled absolute risk for high-grade lesions ranged from 18.9 to 32.9 percent for HPV16 and from 9.1 to 18.7 percent for HPV58, while HPV52 conferred an absolute risk range of only 4.3 to 10.2 percent. Sensitivity analyses confirmed that this risk hierarchy remained completely stable after accounting for verification-bias adjustments, demonstrating that frequency of detection does not mirror pathological severity. The study authors concluded that infection rates in this hospital-based setting did not diminish with advancing age, challenging assumptions derived from conventional screening cohorts. Because the most prevalent viral strains differed from those presenting the highest pathological risk, the researchers noted that genotype-specific risk stratification strategies and the upper age boundaries for screening programs warrant rigorous evaluation in future prospective, population-based studies. (2026). HPV genotype distribution and the risk of high-grade cervical lesions in a hospital-based gynecology population in Henan, China: a retrospective cross-sectional study.
